Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces for Hard Math Problems
针对难题数学问题的多样化替代推理路径
Post-training large language models (LLMs) for mathematical reasoning often relies on accepted traces whose final answers pass a verifier. For hard math problems, however, such traces can be rare under independent Monte Carlo (MC) sampling. To address this, we propose Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces (DART), a trace-mining method that starts from a rejected trace, decomposes it into reasoning steps, and ranks them by position-weighted uncertainty. It then generates deliberately distinct alternative candidates for highly ranked steps using reference-answer guidance and a meta-thought before completing the resulting traces. On greedy-unsolved DeepMath-103K problems, DART recovers 37% more problems than MC sampling at a matched number of generated traces. Its estimated generation cost per recovered problem is nearly identical, while its cost per accepted trace is 30% lower. Post-training Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on traces mined by DART achieves the highest point estimate in five of six evaluations across MATH-500 and AIME benchmarks. These results demonstrate that revisiting rejected traces with DART can expand problem coverage and provide effective post-training data.
中文翻译
后训练大型语言模型（LLMs）进行数学推理通常依赖于通过验证器检验正确的推理路径。然而，对于困难的数学问题，独立蒙特卡洛（MC）采样下此类路径可能极为稀少。为解决此问题，我们提出DART（多样化替代推理路径）方法：从被拒绝的路径出发，将其分解为推理步骤，并根据位置加权不确定性进行排序；随后利用参考答案指导和元思考生成高度不同的替代候选路径以完成推理链。在贪婪未解的DeepMath-103K问题上，DART在生成路径数量相同的情况下比MC采样多恢复37%的问题，其每恢复一个问题的估计生成成本几乎相同，而每通过一条路径的成本降低30%。使用DART挖掘的路径对Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct进行后训练，在MATH-500和AIME基准测试的六项评估中，有五项达到最高点估计值。结果表明，通过DART重新审视被拒绝路径可显著扩展问题覆盖范围，并提供有效的后训练数据。
👤 Lukas Aichberger, Adam Golinski, Arno Blaas, Arwen Bradley, Jacob Springer et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34920]

$μ$-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-User Task Completion in Shared Interaction Voice Agents
$μ$-Bench：面向共享交互语音代理的多用户任务完成基准测试
Voice agents in shared spaces must satisfy interdependent multi-user requests while upholding per-user authority, privacy, and task constraints. Existing evaluations offer limited coverage when participants react throughout a session. We introduce $μ$-Bench, a benchmark for joint task completion in closed-loop, full-duplex multi-user voice interaction. Simulated participants act from isolated profiles of goals, knowledge, and permissions, adapting to the agent's speech and tool outcomes. Across 20 domains, 100 scenarios vary group size, acoustics, and follow-up demands to form 800 cases (408 tool-augmented, 392 tool-free). Joint Task Success (JTS) requires all user goals, privacy boundaries, and terminal states to hold jointly, while Pure JTS further disallows unauthorized tool calls; Attribution, Threading, and Coordination (ATC) diagnostics localize failures. Across 13 voice agents, proprietary systems peak at 62.24\% tool-free and 65.44\% tool-augmented success, while open-weight models remain below 20\%. ATC diagnostics show that open-weight models lag primarily in multi-turn attribution and threading, and controlled contrasts reveal that larger groups consistently degrade joint task success across capable systems. By coupling reactive multi-user simulation with verifiable session-level outcomes, $μ$-Bench provides a rigorous testbed for shared voice agents.
中文翻译
在共享空间中的语音代理需在满足各用户权限、隐私及任务约束的前提下，协调处理相互依赖的多用户请求。现有评估在参与者全程互动时覆盖有限。我们提出$μ$-Bench，一个用于闭环全双工多用户语音交互中联合任务完成的基准测试。模拟参与者基于独立的目标、知识和权限配置行动，并根据代理的语音输出和工具结果动态调整。涵盖20个领域，100种情景，变化群体规模、声学环境和后续需求，共形成800个案例（408个工具增强型，392个无工具型）。联合任务成功率（JTS）要求所有用户目标、隐私边界和终止状态同时满足；纯联合任务成功率（Pure JTS）进一步禁止未经授权的工具调用；ATC诊断（归属、线程、协调）用于定位失败原因。在13个语音代理中，专有系统在无工具场景下最高达62.24%，工具增强场景下达65.44%；而开源模型均低于20%。ATC诊断显示，开源模型主要在多轮归属与线程处理方面落后；受控对比实验表明，更大群体始终会降低各能力系统的联合任务成功率。通过结合反应式多用户仿真与可验证的会话级结果，$μ$-Bench为共享语音代理提供了严格的测试平台。
👤 Qihao Lin, Guanxu Chen, Shuhang Zheng, Xiaoya Lu, Jingyi Yang et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

DRBench Pro: A Knowledge-Centric Benchmark for Professional Deep Research Agents
DRBench Pro：面向专业深度研究代理的知识中心型基准测试
Deep research agents gather information from multiple sources and synthesize it into reports on complex, open-ended questions. Existing benchmarks assess report quality along several dimensions, but lack a dedicated knowledge-centric evaluation of which research directions a report covers and how deeply it explores them. We introduce DRBench Pro, a benchmark that makes structured knowledge coverage the primary evaluation target. Our automated pipeline builds local knowledge trees from Wikipedia and uses sampled subgraphs to generate research queries and evidence-grounded rubrics. Each rubric corresponds to a relation in the tree, preserving the structure of the knowledge being evaluated. Agents receive only the research query. This structure allows us to measure breadth across major research directions and depth along related knowledge chains, with their geometric mean giving overall coverage. The benchmark includes 144 cases across 24 domains and 44,102 rubrics. Evaluating 16 deep research agents reveals different strengths in breadth and depth, with the best-performing agent reaching 57.04% overall coverage. Rubric judgments agree closely with human annotations, and agent rankings remain stable across the tested metric configurations.
中文翻译
深度研究代理从多个来源收集信息，并将之整合成关于复杂开放性问题的报告。现有基准测试虽从多个维度评估报告质量，但缺乏对报告所覆盖的研究方向及其深入程度的专门知识中心评价。我们引入DRBench Pro，将结构化知识覆盖作为核心评估目标。我们的自动化流程从维基百科构建本地知识树，并使用采样的子图生成研究问题和基于证据的评分标准。每个评分标准对应树中的一个关系，保留了被评估知识的结构。代理仅接收研究问题，该结构使我们能够测量在主要研究方向上的广度以及相关知识链上的深度，其几何平均值代表整体覆盖度。该基准包含24个领域中的144个案例和44,102个评分标准。对16个深度研究代理的评估揭示了它们在广度与深度上的不同优势，表现最佳的代理达到57.04%的整体覆盖度。评分标准判断与人工标注高度一致，且在不同测试指标配置下代理排名保持稳定。
👤 Han Huang, Yuchen Yan, Xingyu Wu, Yijun Li, Chao Deng et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Repairing the Trustworthiness of Distilled Language Models
修复蒸馏语言模型的可信性
Knowledge distillation enables the deployment of large language models through smaller student models, but the compression process can introduce regressions in trustworthiness even when downstream performance is preserved. Repairing such failures is challenging because the original distillation corpus is often unavailable, while direct preference optimization may alter capabilities inherited from the teacher. We propose a targeted repair framework based on the proximal Bregman response function (PBRF) that addresses both limitations. We estimate the curvature required for PBRF using a surrogate proxy dataset, removing dependence on the original transfer corpus, and extend the framework to preference-based supervision through an odds-ratio objective. This enables the use of preferred and dispreferred response pairs while constraining changes to the distilled model. Our results show that the proposed approach improves targeted trustworthiness behaviors for both pre-training and post-training models while largely preserving downstream performance, providing a practical alternative to re-distillation or unconstrained preference optimization. Our experiment results show that across different distilled models, the trustworthy regression can be improved by up to 8% with less than 1% decrement in perplexity.
中文翻译
知识蒸馏使得通过较小的学生模型部署大型语言模型成为可能，但压缩过程可能导致可信性下降，即使下游性能保持不变。修复此类问题具有挑战性，因为原始蒸馏语料库通常不可用，而直接偏好优化可能会改变从教师模型继承的能力。我们提出一种基于近似Bregman响应函数（PBRF）的目标修复框架，解决了这两个局限性。我们使用代理数据集估计PBRF所需的曲率，从而摆脱对原始迁移语料库的依赖，并通过奇数比目标将框架扩展至基于偏好的监督。这使得在约束蒸馏模型变化的前提下，能够使用偏好和不偏好响应对进行训练。实验结果表明，该方法在预训练和后训练模型上均显著提升了特定可信性行为，同时基本保持了下游性能，为重新蒸馏或无约束偏好优化提供了一种实用替代方案。在不同蒸馏模型上，可信性退化可改善高达8%，且困惑度下降不足1%。
👤 Kumar Shubham, Devendra Vyas, Vidushi Agarwal, Jayin Khanna, Prathosh AP 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

The Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark: Do Language Models Know the Foundations of Early-Grade Literacy?
语言学知识基准：语言模型是否掌握早期阅读素养的基础知识？
Many languages of instruction in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) are poorly represented in the data used to train language models. Before these models can support early-grade literacy in such languages, we need to know whether they hold the linguistic knowledge of each language that literacy teaching relies on. To test this, we present the Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark and the reproducible, largely automated pipeline that turns curriculum documents, teacher guides and pupil books into a vetted question set organised on a fixed grid of nine linguistic domains and seven grade levels. The pipeline combines LLM-driven source survey, feature consolidation, item generation and quality control with an item-selection step inspired by item response theory and a final expert review. We instantiate it for Kiswahili (Kenya; 135 items) and English (United Kingdom; 137 items) and evaluate 121 and 119 models respectively. Frontier models exceed 90% accuracy in both languages, but accuracy falls steeply for smaller and cheaper models, and more so in Kiswahili. The two best models under 10 billion parameters reach 91.7% and 73.0% in English but only 51.9% and 40.7% in Kiswahili, and the mean accuracy of models deployable on mobile and tablet hardware is 47% in English against 24% in Kiswahili. The models most practical for low-resource classrooms are therefore the least capable in the languages they would serve. We release a 20-item sample per language, all pipeline prompts, open-source code, and a public leaderboard. This is the first rung of a three-level evaluation ladder for measuring and generating decodable text to support foundational literacy.
中文翻译
许多低收入和中等收入国家（LMICs）的教学语言在训练语言模型的数据中代表性不足。在这些语言中，语言模型能否支持早期阅读素养之前，我们需要了解它们是否具备阅读教学所依赖的语言学知识。为此，我们提出了语言学知识基准及一个可复现、高度自动化的流程，该流程将课程文件、教师指南和学生课本转化为经过验证的问题集，按九个语言领域和七个年级水平组织。该流程结合LLM驱动的源调查、特征整合、题目生成与质量控制，并借鉴项目反应理论设计题目选择步骤，最后进行专家评审。我们在基斯瓦希里语（肯尼亚；135题）和英语（英国；137题）中进行了实例化，并分别评估了121个和119个模型。前沿模型在两种语言中的准确率均超过90%，但小型和低成本模型的准确率急剧下降，尤其在基斯瓦希里语中更为明显。参数量低于100亿的两个最佳模型在英语中达到91.7%和73.0%的准确率，但在基斯瓦希里语中仅为51.9%和40.7%；可在移动和平板设备部署的模型平均准确率为英语47%、基斯瓦希里语24%。因此，最适用于低资源课堂的模型恰恰是其服务语言中最不擅长的。我们发布了每种语言20个样本题目、所有流程提示、开源代码和公开排行榜。这是衡量并生成可解码文本以支持基础阅读能力的三阶段评估阶梯的第一步。
👤 Maxime Lelièvre, Alasdair Mackintosh, Dominic Andrews, Tobias Murray, Moritz Boos et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

VulnSafe: Measuring Vulnerability-Aware Relational Safety in Large Language Models
VulnSafe：测量大语言模型中的脆弱性感知关系安全性
Emotionally supportive interactions require large language models not only to avoid overtly harmful content, but also to recognize vulnerability-related risk, regulate their relational stance, preserve user agency, and provide context-appropriate support. Existing safety benchmarks often measure isolated violations, leaving unclear what underlying response competence their scores represent. We introduce VulnSafe, a Chinese capability assessment centered on Vulnerability-Aware Response Competence (VARC). VARC organizes relational safety into five complementary constructs: Risk Perception, Safety Preservation, Autonomy Protection, Supportive Response, and Boundary Management. Following an evidence-centered design, 41 scenarios elicit construct-relevant behavior through 2,050 balanced prompts and scenario-specific scoring rubrics. The assessment further separates proactive should-do behavior from prohibitive should-not behavior. Across eight representative LLMs, results show distinct competence profiles and larger disparities than prohibitive requirements. VulnSafe therefore makes relational safety interpretable as a multidimensional model capability rather than as refusal compliance alone.
中文翻译
情感支持性互动要求大语言模型不仅避免明显的有害内容，还需识别与脆弱性相关的风险，调节自身关系立场，保护用户自主权，并提供情境适配的支持。现有安全基准通常仅衡量孤立违规行为，难以明确其评分反映的底层响应能力。我们引入VulnSafe，一个聚焦于脆弱性感知响应能力（VARC）的中文能力评估体系。VARC将关系安全性划分为五个互补维度：风险感知、安全维持、自主权保护、支持性回应和边界管理。采用证据中心设计，41个场景通过2050个平衡提示和针对具体场景的评分标准，激发相关行为表现。评估还区分了应主动采取的行为与应禁止的行为。在八种代表性大语言模型上的测试结果显示，各模型表现出不同的能力图谱，且积极行为的差异远大于禁止性要求的差异。因此，VulnSafe使关系安全性可被理解为多维模型能力，而非仅限于拒绝合规。
👤 Jiaming Luo, Jiacheng Ji, Haoan Jin, Xiaoning Sun, Qiuzhi Xiong et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

PoVi: Agentic Poetry-to-Video Generation with Knowledge Grounding and Figurative Understanding
PoVi：基于知识锚定与隐喻理解的智能体诗歌转视频生成
Poetry-to-video generation requires more than translating text into visual scenes. Poetic language is highly compressed, often depends on historical and cultural knowledge, and frequently conveys meaning through metaphor, imagery, and other non-literal expressions. We introduce PoVi, a benchmark and agentic framework for grounded poetry-to-video generation, evaluating semantic grounding, discourse coherence, and audiovisual realization. PoVi reconstructs poem semantics into an evidence-bounded representation and progressively transforms them into narration, interpretation, character assets, and shot-level visual plans, followed by narration-aligned video generation. Across three planning backbones and four video generators, PoVi consistently improves over single-call generation, with the largest gains on implicit background knowledge and metaphorical relations. PoVi provides a testbed for studying knowledge-grounded, long-horizon multimodal generation beyond direct text-to-video synthesis.
中文翻译
诗歌转视频生成不仅需要将文本翻译为视觉场景，还需处理诗意语言的高度压缩性、对历史与文化知识的依赖性，以及通过隐喻、意象等非字面表达传递意义的特点。我们提出了PoVi，一个基准测试与智能体框架，用于评估语义锚定、话语连贯性和音视频实现能力。PoVi将诗歌语义重构为受证据约束的表示形式，并逐步转化为叙述、解释、角色资产和逐镜头视觉计划，随后生成与叙述对齐的视频。在三种规划骨干和四种视频生成器上，PoVi始终优于单次调用生成方法，尤其在隐含背景知识和隐喻关系方面提升显著。PoVi为研究超越直接文本到视频合成的知识锚定型、长时程多模态生成提供了实验平台。
👤 Yuxia Zhu, Shiying Zhang, Lingzhuang Sun, Song Yang, Siheng Wu et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34529]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base
AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base
We present AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base, a mixture-of-experts language model with 80 billion total parameters and approximately 3 billion active parameters per token, trained from scratch at Yandex and released under the Apache 2.0 license. The model combines Kimi Delta Attention with full attention, uses Attention Residuals, and is trained with Muon. Training comprises general pretraining on 17.5 trillion tokens, two context-extension stages, and a dedicated stage for reasoning and agentic interaction. We describe the data collection and augmentation pipelines, evaluation protocols, architectural ablations, and scaling-law experiments. We identify the limitations of held-out training loss as a proxy for downstream performance and introduce an auxiliary loss on mixture-of-experts outputs to stabilize training at the predicted learning rate. Domain-specific data selection and augmentation improve factual knowledge, mathematics, and programming skills, while agent trajectories introduced during pretraining improve performance on agentic benchmarks after supervised fine-tuning. In zero-shot reasoning evaluation, the model achieves leading results among open base models of comparable size, with 88.7% pass@8 on IMO-AnswerBench and 82.9% pass@8 on LiveCodeBench v5-v6. To evaluate Russian-language factual knowledge, we introduce and release two benchmarks with their evaluation protocols: WikiWebFacts, which tests short factual questions drawn from encyclopedic sources and search queries, and HardMultiQA, which tests more demanding factual questions inspired by user interactions. Our model scores 86.5% on WikiWebFacts and 67.9% on HardMultiQA, compared with 83.2% and 65.4% for DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Base (284B total and 13B active parameters) and 72.8% and 54.5% for Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base (120B total and 12B active parameters), outperforming both larger models on these benchmarks.
中文翻译
我们提出了 AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base，这是一个具有800亿总参数、每token约30亿活跃参数的专家混合语言模型，由Yandex从头训练并以Apache 2.0许可证发布。该模型结合了Kimi Delta Attention与全注意力机制，采用注意力残差，并使用Muon进行训练。训练包括在17.5万亿token上的通用预训练、两个上下文扩展阶段，以及一个专门用于推理和代理交互的阶段。我们描述了数据收集与增强流程、评估协议、架构消融实验和缩放定律实验。我们指出保留训练损失作为下游性能代理的局限性，并引入一种针对专家混合输出的辅助损失，以在预测学习率下稳定训练。特定领域数据选择与增强提升了事实知识、数学和编程能力；预训练期间引入的代理轨迹在监督微调后提升了代理基准表现。在零样本推理评估中，该模型在同规模开源基础模型中表现领先，在IMO-AnswerBench上达到88.7%的pass@8，在LiveCodeBench v5-v6上达到82.9%的pass@8。为评估俄语事实知识，我们引入并发布了两个基准及其评估协议：WikiWebFacts（测试来自百科来源和搜索查询的简短事实问题）和HardMultiQA（测试更具挑战性的事实问题，灵感来自用户交互）。我们的模型在WikiWebFacts上得分为86.5%，在HardMultiQA上得分为67.9%，优于DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Base（284B总参数，13B活跃参数）的83.2%和65.4%，也优于Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base（120B总参数，12B活跃参数）的72.8%和54.5%，在这些基准上超越了更大模型的表现。
👤 Nikolay Skachkov, Ekaterina Redina, Maksim Konoplev, Evgenii Uskov, Veronika Zykova et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34398]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
When Skills Fail to Compose: Compatibility and Retention in Language Models
当技能无法组合时：语言模型中的兼容性与保留性
Skill acquisition alone does not establish a reusable skill library: task proficiency does not specify a compositional interface. Motivated by agents that acquire and refine skills, we study independently learned soft-prompt skills on frozen language models, with skill identities and execution order supplied. Across four digit-task bases and three record-editing models, mean composition accuracy in one call is at most 7.6%, compared with 88.2% to 100% over two calls that add computation and an explicit intermediate output. Neutral-insertion training improves robustness but leaves substantial interference, which block length and per-token norm alone cannot explain on two of four digit bases. Blocks of the same operation need not be interchangeable: after training next to frozen learned partners, a post hoc test on two Qwen bases shows that replacing those partners with independently learned blocks of the same operations lowers median composition accuracy from 98.0% and 95.3% to 12.7% and 0.5%, although every replacement scores at least 90% alone. Repair can improve a composition while impairing the original skill, and rehearsal improves retention while transfer remains limited on Qwen2.5-0.5B. Internal interventions constrain explanations without identifying a shared failure mechanism. These findings motivate evaluating compatibility, retention and transfer alongside acquisition when building skill libraries for self-evolving agents. Code is available at https://github.com/YanLin-Quinne/SkillCompatibility.
中文翻译
仅掌握技能并不能建立可复用的技能库：任务熟练度并未定义可组合的接口。受能够获取并优化技能的智能体启发，我们研究了在冻结的语言模型上独立学习的软提示技能，其中技能身份和执行顺序由外部提供。在四个数字任务基准和三个记录编辑模型上，单次调用的平均组合准确率最高仅为7.6%，而两次调用（增加计算量并显式输出中间结果）的准确率则达到88.2%至100%。中性插入训练提升了鲁棒性，但仍存在显著干扰，这种干扰无法仅通过块长度或每标记归一化来解释。相同操作的块并不一定可互换：在两个Qwen基线模型上，经过与冻结学习伙伴共同训练后，事后测试显示，用独立学习的同操作块替换这些伙伴，会使中位组合准确率从98.0%和95.3%降至12.7%和0.5%，尽管每个替换块单独测试得分均不低于90%。修复可能改善组合性能，但会损害原始技能；重练能提升保留性，但在Qwen2.5-0.5B上迁移能力仍有限。内部干预虽限制了解释范围，但未能识别出共享的失败机制。这些发现表明，在构建自演化智能体的技能库时，应同时评估兼容性、保留性和迁移性。代码已公开于 https://github.com/YanLin-Quinne/SkillCompatibility。
👤 Yan Lin, Zhongliang Guo, Jingyu Sun, Qiyan Zhao, Ruihong Zeng et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Learning from Unreliable Operator References: Claim-Level Rewards for Customer Support Agents
从不可靠的操作员引用中学习：面向客户支持智能体的声明级奖励
Even a correct operator reply can be an unreliable training target for a customer-support agent. About a quarter of the factual claims in these replies lacked full support in the agent's knowledge base and tool records. We treat operator replies as claims to verify rather than answers to imitate. Unverified claims guide revisions to the knowledge base and tools. Verified claims serve as coverage targets in a claim-level GRPO reward that scores factual precision against the evidence the agent retrieved for its answer. On an offline benchmark, 8.6% of the GRPO agent's text answers were flagged for hallucinations, compared with 13.6% for the supervised fine-tuning baseline. In a randomized A/B test against the existing procedural bot, the deployed system increased the share of conversations closed without a human operator from 63.3% to 80.8%.
中文翻译
即使操作员的回答正确，也可能成为客户支持智能体不可靠的训练目标。约四分之一的回复中的事实声明在智能体的知识库和工具记录中缺乏完整支持。我们不将操作员回复视为需要模仿的答案，而是将其作为需验证的声明。未验证的声明用于指导知识库和工具的修订；已验证的声明则作为声明级GRPO奖励中的覆盖目标，该奖励根据智能体检索到的证据评估事实精确性。在离线基准测试中，GRPO智能体的文本回答中有8.6%被标记为幻觉，而监督微调基线为13.6%。在与现有流程机器人进行的随机A/B测试中，部署系统使无需人工介入即可关闭的对话比例从63.3%提升至80.8%。

Puzzling-Bench: Evaluating Language Models on 23,777 Human-Written Puzzles with Community-Verified Answers
Puzzling-Bench：在23,777个真人编写的谜题上评估语言模型，答案经社区验证
We introduce Puzzling-Bench, a benchmark of 23,777 puzzles with community-verified answers drawn from the Puzzling Stack Exchange archive (2014-2026): riddles, ciphers, logic grids, lateral-thinking problems, wordplay and recreational mathematics, a third of them with images. Every puzzle carries the answer accepted by its author, a short reference answer taken from it (LLM-extracted for the evaluation sets and checked on a 50-puzzle human review), the author's own hints, community tags and a creation date, so that the benchmark supports hint ablations, category analysis and a rolling post-cutoff slice (254 puzzles posted after the training cutoff of six of the nine models we evaluate, including the best one) as a contamination check. We evaluate nine frontier and open models with a two-tier scorer (normalized exact match, then an LLM judge) and validate the judge with three judges from two model families: they agree on 95-97% of verdicts (kappa 0.82-0.96), move no model by more than 1.6 points, and show no own-family preference. The best model, GPT-6.1, reaches 74.8% on a shared 255-puzzle set; a hard core of 17 puzzles defeats all nine models; model failures are strongly correlated, and the second-best model contributes a single puzzle to a greedy ensemble, yet an oracle over all nine reaches 91%. On a 100-puzzle multimodal pilot, withholding the question's image costs the two best models 24 and 35 points. We release the dataset, the scoring pipeline and all per-row provenance.
中文翻译
我们推出了Puzzling-Bench，一个包含23,777个谜题的基准测试集，数据源自Puzzling Stack Exchange档案（2014–2026年）：包括谜语、密码、逻辑网格、横向思维题、文字游戏和休闲数学问题，其中三分之一配有图像。每个谜题均包含作者接受的答案、简短参考答案（LLM提取并经50题人工审核）、作者提供的提示、社区标签和创建日期，因此该基准支持提示消融、类别分析以及滚动的截止后切片（六个被评估模型中有九个在训练截止后发布了254道新谜题，包括表现最佳的模型），以进行污染检测。我们使用双层评分器（标准化精确匹配，随后由LLM裁判）评估九个前沿及开源模型，并通过来自两个模型家族的三位裁判验证裁判一致性：他们对95%-97%的判断达成一致（kappa值0.82–0.96），无模型得分变动超过1.6分，且无家族偏好。表现最佳的模型GPT-6.1在共享的255题集上达到74.8%准确率；一个由17道难题构成的高难度核心挑战击败所有九个模型；模型失败高度相关，第二名模型仅贡献一道题给贪婪集成，而所有九个模型的最优组合（oracle）可达91%。在100题多模态试点中，若隐藏问题图像，前两名模型分别损失24和35分。我们已发布数据集、评分流水线及每行溯源信息。

IRTIQA: Evolving in English, Evaluated in Urdu -- Does Agent Self-Evolution Transfer from English to Urdu and Code-Switched Input?
IRTIQA：在英语中进化，在乌尔都语中评估——智能体自演化能否从英语迁移到乌尔都语及混合语言输入？
As LLM agents increasingly learn from their own experience, the tasks they learn from and are evaluated on are almost always in English. We ask whether self-evolution transfers from English to Urdu, and release IRTIQA, a benchmark of aligned English, Urdu-translated, and Urdu code-switched variants of DROP, SNIPS, AppWorld, and SkillsBench, built with a matrix-language code-switching pipeline and human verification. Using IRTIQA, we evaluate three self-evolving frameworks, Tool-R0 (self-play RL), AgentEvolver (experience-guided RL), and Autogenesis (protocol-level evolution), against their unevolved backbones. Self-evolution often raises capability: AgentEvolver lifts AppWorld goal completion from near zero to 20-36% across all conditions, and Autogenesis improves SkillsBench pass rate from 16.48% to 22.22%. But it rarely closes the language gap. Evolved systems remain stronger in English, by up to 0.26 DROP F1, the gap is largest on multi-step tasks, and code-switching behaves as a distinct condition rather than degraded translation. On a shared Gemma-4 31B backbone, the gap exists before evolution, and RL-based evolution can hurt Urdu reasoning more than English. Language parity must therefore be an explicit design target, as it does not emerge from self-improvement alone.
中文翻译
随着大语言模型智能体越来越多地从自身经验中学习，它们所学习和评估的任务几乎始终以英语为主。我们探讨自演化是否能从英语迁移到乌尔都语，并发布IRTIQA基准，该基准包含DROP、SNIPS、AppWorld和SkillsBench的对齐英文、乌尔都语翻译版及乌尔都语混杂版本，采用矩阵语言混杂管道构建并经人工验证。利用IRTIQA，我们评估三种自演化框架（Tool-R0，自对弈强化学习；AgentEvolver，经验引导强化学习；Autogenesis，协议级演化）与其未演化的基础模型之间的差异。自演化通常能提升能力：AgentEvolver使AppWorld的目标完成率从接近零提升至20%-36%；Autogenesis将SkillsBench通过率从16.48%提升至22.22%。但很少能弥合语言差距。演化后的系统在英语中仍更强，最多相差0.26的DROP F1，差距在多步任务中最大，且语言混杂表现为独立条件而非劣化翻译。在共享的Gemma-4 31B基础模型上，差距在演化前即存在，且基于RL的演化反而可能比英语更损害乌尔都语推理能力。因此，语言平等必须作为明确的设计目标，因为其不会仅通过自改进自然产生。

MINE: A Training-Free Defense against Indirect Prompt Injection via Mutual Information and Embedding Variance
MINE：通过互信息与嵌入方差实现无需训练的间接提示注入防御
Existing defenses against indirect prompt injection in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) are inherently reactive: prompt-engineering methods are often overridden by the injected instructions, while supervised detectors must be retrained for each new attack style. We propose a different paradigm: defend by maximizing relevance, not by detecting attacks. Our method, MINE, is training-free and attack-agnostic, and it also serves as a query-conditioned reranker. To evaluate defenses under realistic conditions, we introduce LC-Inj-SQuAD, a long-context benchmark with an average of 4,032 words per context. We evaluate on four datasets spanning short-context QA (Inj-SQuAD, Inj-TriviaQA), long-context QA (LC-Inj-SQuAD), and agent tool-call injection (InjecAgent). Across all four, MINE achieves the best accuracy--security trade-off among training-free defenses. It is also the only defense that stays usable against a fully adaptive attacker, generalizes to agent tool-call injection, and achieves the lowest inference latency on long contexts.
中文翻译
现有的针对检索增强生成（RAG）中间接提示注入的防御方法本质上是被动的：提示工程方法常被注入指令覆盖，而监督检测器必须为每种新攻击风格重新训练。我们提出一种新范式：通过最大化相关性进行防御，而非检测攻击。我们的方法MINE无需训练且对攻击风格无感，同时可作为查询条件重排序器。为在真实条件下评估防御效果，我们引入LC-Inj-SQuAD，一个平均上下文长度达4,032词的长上下文基准。我们在四个数据集上进行评估，涵盖短上下文问答（Inj-SQuAD、Inj-TriviaQA）、长上下文问答（LC-Inj-SQuAD）以及智能体工具调用注入（InjecAgent）。在所有四个数据集中，MINE在无需训练的防御方法中实现了最佳的准确率-安全性权衡。它也是唯一能在面对完全自适应攻击者时仍保持可用性、泛化至智能体工具调用注入，并在长上下文上实现最低推理延迟的防御方法。
👤 Truong Phan, Ngoc-Quan Pham, Dat Quoc Nguyen 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Familiar Words, Unfamiliar Minds: Conventionalization without Calibration in the Vocabulary of Mind
熟悉词汇，陌生心智：心智词汇中的常规化而不需校准
Speakers describe new technologies by stretching old words, letting experience of the new referents sort out which inferences carry over. I argue that stretching the vocabulary of mind to language models pulls apart two processes that theories of metaphor and linguistic change tend to conflate: conventionalization, whereby a stretched word ceases to be marked as such, and calibration, whereby the inferences it invites are fitted to what its new referents bear out. As a gene variant can reach fixation without being an adaptation, a word can become conventional without being calibrated. A corpus study of "hallucinate" in 8,568 abstracts and 31,866 forum comments shows conventionalization running most of its course within three years. Calibration need not keep pace: these models are organized so unlike human minds that it is hard to tell when mental words apply and what they imply. Even warranted attributions can mislead: mechanistic analysis shows that a transformer trained to navigate Manhattan has and uses a faithful street map, yet loses track of its position on long, diverted journeys. Nor is the gap likely to close by itself. The usual routes to calibration (direct experience, designers' knowledge, experiment) presuppose a referent that is accessible, stable, and independent of the discourse about it. But language models are opaque, fast-changing, and looped into the discourse: they are trained on past uses of mental vocabulary and on the experimental literature about them, and join the discourse themselves. Calibration must therefore be organized, through a reciprocal, not merely deferential, division of linguistic labour. This yields a norm of targeted qualification (flag the specific inferences an attribution invites without support), a reply to the worry that meaning change eludes deliberate control, and three testable predictions about when qualifications calibrate expectations.
中文翻译
说话者通过延伸旧词来描述新技术，让对新指称的经验自行决定哪些推论可以延续。我认为，将心智词汇扩展至语言模型会分离两种在隐喻和语言演变理论中常被混淆的过程：常规化，即延伸词不再被视为特殊；以及校准，即其引发的推论与新指称的实际属性相匹配。如同基因变异可在非适应情况下达到固定，一个词也可成为常规词而不必经过校准。对8,568篇摘要和31,866条论坛评论中“hallucinate”一词的语料库研究表明，常规化过程在三年内基本完成。但校准未必能跟上：这些模型的组织方式与人类心智截然不同，难以判断心理词汇何时适用及其含义。即使合理的归因也可能误导：机制分析显示，一个训练用于导航曼哈顿的Transformer模型拥有并使用了忠实的街道地图，但在长距离偏移旅程中会迷失位置。这种差距不太可能自行弥合。通常的校准路径（直接经验、设计者知识、实验）预设了可访问、稳定且独立于话语的指称对象，但语言模型具有不可见性、快速变化性和话语循环性：它们基于过去对心智词汇的使用及关于它们的实验文献进行训练，并自身参与话语。因此，校准必须通过相互的、而非单向的言语劳动分工来组织。这带来一种目标明确的限定规范（标记未获支持的具体推论），回应了意义变化无法主动控制的担忧，并提出了三个可检验的预测，关于限定如何校准预期。
👤 Matthieu Queloz 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34044]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Reliable User Simulation in Complex Multi-Step Workflows
复杂多步骤工作流中的可靠用户模拟
LLM-based user simulators are increasingly used to evaluate conversational assistants. Yet, these simulators can drift from their assigned goals or fail to exhaustively explore all specified options, causing evaluation errors incorrectly attributed to the assistant. Prior work has examined goal alignment in single-task settings, but many practical scenarios require simulators to execute multi-step workflows with sequential tasks, fallback logic, and cross-task dependencies, where simulator fidelity has not been measured. To close this gap, we introduce a benchmark of 500 workflows represented using the Hierarchical Task Network (HTN) abstraction. Our experiments reveal that the simulator's fidelity degrades sharply with workflow complexity, and that goal drift accounts for the majority of failures. Fidelity also depends on the assistant's response style, with the same simulator scoring substantially better or worse across styles. To address this, we propose ReUST (Reasoning + User State Tracking), a simple prompting strategy to maintain state information across dialogue turns. ReUST improves goal success rate by more than 12 points for mid-sized and large models, and reduces simulator sensitivity to the assistant's response style.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型的用户模拟器正被广泛用于评估对话助手。然而，这些模拟器可能偏离既定目标或未能充分探索所有指定选项，导致评估误差被错误归因于助手本身。先前研究关注单任务场景下的目标对齐，但许多实际场景要求模拟器执行包含顺序任务、回退逻辑和跨任务依赖的多步骤工作流，而此类场景下的模拟器保真度尚未得到测量。为填补这一空白，我们引入了一个由500个工作流组成的基准，采用层次任务网络（HTN）抽象表示。实验表明，模拟器保真度随工作流复杂度急剧下降，且目标漂移是失败的主要原因。保真度还取决于助手的响应风格，同一模拟器在不同风格下表现差异显著。为此，我们提出ReUST（推理+用户状态追踪），一种简单的提示策略，以在对话轮次间维持状态信息。ReUST使中大型模型的目标成功率提升超过12个百分点，并显著降低模拟器对助手响应风格的敏感性。
👤 Pusala Pavan Teja, Jayasimha Talur, Saab Mansour, Marcello Federico 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Too Smart for Its Own Good? Cognitive Fidelity and Competence Leakage in LLM Simulations of Young Learners
聪明过头？大语言模型在年轻学习者模拟中的认知保真度与能力泄露
LLM-based interactive learner simulators are increasingly used in educational applications, including teacher training and tutoring system development. A critical requirement for those simulations is cognitive fidelity: the simulated learner should maintain the assigned knowledge, misconceptions, and reasoning procedures throughout interaction. However, LLMs may express knowledge or problem-solving skills beyond the assigned learner's cognitive status, a phenomenon we refer to as competence leakage. In this exploratory study, we examine 3,339 simulated learner utterances from 130 teacher-learner simulator transcripts across three learning scenarios and identify over-competence as a prominent direction of cognitive-profile misalignment during interaction. We then investigate the recurrence of previously observed competence leakage cases under four matched-trigger evaluation protocols, ranging from isolated questioning to multi-turn interactions, across five model configurations. Overall, we observe higher violation recurrence under multi-turn protocols than under isolated question answering. More capable models generally show lower recurrence, whereas increased reasoning effort does not consistently improve cognitive fidelity and is even associated with slightly higher recurrence under multi-turn protocols in our matched-model comparison. These findings provide exploratory evidence of competence leakage phenomena in interactive learner simulation and suggest that single-turn question-answering type of evaluations alone may be insufficient, motivating the development of more systematic and reliable multi-turn protocols for evaluating cognitive fidelity in learner simulation.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型的交互式学习者模拟器正日益应用于教育领域，包括教师培训和辅导系统开发。这类模拟的关键要求是认知保真度：模拟的学习者应在交互过程中保持设定的知识、误解和推理方式。然而，大语言模型可能表现出超出设定认知水平的知识或解题能力，我们称之为能力泄露。在本探索性研究中，我们分析了三个学习场景下130个师生模拟对话中的3,339条学习者语句，识别出过度能力是交互过程中认知画像错位的主要方向。随后，我们在四种匹配触发的评估协议下（从孤立提问到多轮交互）考察了此前观察到的能力泄露案例的重复性，涵盖五种模型配置。总体而言，多轮协议下的违规重复率高于孤立问答。更强大的模型通常表现出更低的重复率，而增加推理努力并未一致提升认知保真度，在多轮协议下甚至与略高的重复率相关。这些发现为交互式学习者模拟中的能力泄露现象提供了探索性证据，提示仅依赖单轮问答型评估可能不足，亟需发展更系统、更可靠的多轮评估协议以有效评估学习者模拟的认知保真度。
👤 Zhen Xu, Beata Beigman Klebanov, Jamie N. Mikeska 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

JHealthBench-L: Clinician-Reviewed Localization of HealthBench for Rubric-Based Medical LLM Evaluation in Japan
JHealthBench-L：基于日本临床标准的健康基准局部化评估框架
Rubric-based benchmarks such as HealthBench evaluate large language models (LLMs) on open-ended health consultations using case-specific physician criteria, many of which presuppose the healthcare institutions, medications, and care pathways of their source region. Under machine translation alone, these regional assumptions remain in the rubric, leaving responses appropriate in Japan uncredited or falsely penalized under foreign clinical rules. To adapt established clinical evaluation perspectives to Japan without authoring rubrics from scratch, we construct JHealthBench-L (79 patient-facing consultations, 1,022 criteria) through source-cited LLM drafting, single-specialist selection and revision, independent dual-physician review, and meta-review. Specialists rewrote only 4.4% of criteria from scratch, and independent reviewers approved 93.6% of Stage-2 criteria without issue. Comparing JHealthBench-L with a machine-translated baseline across 18 LLMs reveals that translation-only evaluation preserves coarse model rankings ($τ= 0.817$, mean shift +0.027) yet flips verdicts on 17.7% of modified criteria (vs. 7.3% re-grading noise) and reduces false penalties on Japan-adapted negative criteria by 7.1 percentage points, masking item-level clinical mismeasurement behind stable leaderboards. Axis-level and paired analyses further show that context awareness and Japan-specific clinical criteria remain primary bottlenecks, whereas Japanese continual pretraining and reasoning post-training substantially improve locally grounded performance. Datasets, physician revision logs, and evaluation code are available at https://github.com/horshohei/JHealthBench-L.
中文翻译
基于评分量表的基准测试（如HealthBench）使用特定病例的医生标准对大语言模型（LLMs）在开放式医疗咨询中的表现进行评估，但这些标准往往依赖于其来源地区的医疗机构、药物和护理路径。仅通过机器翻译，这些区域假设仍保留在评分量表中，导致在日本适用的回应在外国临床规则下被错误扣分或未获认可。为在不从零构建评分量表的前提下将现有临床评估视角适配日本，我们通过引用源文献的LLM起草、单专业专家筛选与修订、独立双医生评审及元评审，构建了JHealthBench-L（79个患者导向咨询，1,022项评估标准）。专家仅重写4.4%的标准，独立评审者对93.6%的第二阶段标准无异议通过。在18个LLM上对比JHealthBench-L与纯机器翻译基线发现，翻译评估虽保留粗粒度模型排名（τ=0.817，平均偏移+0.027），但改变了17.7%的修改标准的判断结果（vs. 7.3%的重新评分噪声），并将针对日本适应性负面标准的误罚率降低7.1个百分点，掩盖了项目级临床误测问题。轴向分析和配对分析进一步表明，上下文感知能力和日本特异性临床标准仍是主要瓶颈，而日本持续预训练和推理后训练显著提升了本地化表现。数据集、医生修订日志和评估代码已公开于https://github.com/horshohei/JHealthBench-L。

Evaluating Ukrainian Grammatical Competence in Language Models: Probability Versus Prompting
评估语言模型中的乌克兰语语法能力：概率法与提示法比较
Organisations deploying large language models in Ukrainian-language information systems need to know whether a model produces normative Ukrainian. This paper establishes a protocol for measuring that reliably and applies it to 19 models. The instrument is a released set of 1,444 Ukrainian minimal pairs covering the vocative case, numeral-noun and adjective agreement, verb government and calques from russian, with subject-verb agreement as a control; a quarter of the set was checked against the answer key in two independent native-speaker ratings, which agreed at Cohen's $κ$ = 0.963. Every pair is scored twice: by comparing the probabilities a model assigns to the two sentences, and by asking the model directly, with the sentences presented in both orders so that answer-position effects are measured instead of being absorbed into the score. Three results follow. Order consistency (the share of pairs answered identically when the sentences are exchanged) falls below 0.05 for ten of the nineteen models, and what separates the usable models is instruction tuning, not scale. The vocative case is systematically inverted: 0.153 accuracy against 0.951 on the control, with models preferring the non-normative nominative form of address. Ukrainian adaptation substantially reduces this deficit, measured against the exact checkpoints two adapted models were trained from.

在乌克兰语信息系统中部署大语言模型的组织需要了解模型是否生成符合规范的乌克兰语。本文建立了一套可靠的测量协议，并应用于19个模型。该工具包含1,444个乌克兰语最小对立对，涵盖呼格、数词-名词及形容词一致、动词支配关系以及俄语借词，以主谓一致作为对照。四分之一的样本经两名独立母语者评分，Cohen's κ值达0.963。每一对均进行两次评分：一是通过比较模型对两个句子的概率分配，二是直接提问并以两种顺序呈现句子，以测量答案位置效应而非将其吸收进得分。三个结果如下：十种模型的顺序一致性低于0.05，区分可用模型的关键因素是指令微调而非规模；呼格系统性颠倒：准确率为0.153，而对照组为0.951，模型更倾向非规范的主格称呼形式；乌克兰语适配显著缩小这一差距，与两个适配模型所基于的原始检查点相比可量化衡量。

ChemDeduce: Benchmarking LLMs for Unknown Chemical Identification through Interactive Simulated Experiments
ChemDeduce：通过交互式模拟实验基准测试LLM在未知化学物质识别中的表现
Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in chemical knowledge and reasoning, yet it remains unclear whether they can solve problems whose answers must be discovered through experimentation. We investigate this question through Unknown Chemical Identification, where a model must actively design experiments, interpret observations, update hypotheses, and ultimately identify a hidden substance. We introduce ChemDeduce, an interactive evaluation framework for assessing LLMs through simulated experiments. ChemDeduce formulates chemical identification as a sequential decision process and provides a stateful simulated laboratory with explicit instrument and operational constraints, enabling scalable and reproducible evaluation of iterative experimental reasoning. We benchmark eight frontier LLMs on 200 chemicals, yielding 1600 model--target pairs and 4800 episodes over three independent repetitions, and evaluate identification accuracy, operational compliance, interaction efficiency, and trajectory-level reasoning quality. Despite generating seemingly plausible experimental plans, current models achieve an average identification accuracy ranging from 23.83% to 67.33%, revealing substantial limitations in experiment selection, evidence interpretation, and sequential hypothesis refinement.

Tells of a Lying Machine: Analyzing LLM-Generated Strategic Dialogue in Chinese Werewolf
说谎机器的线索：分析中文狼人杀中大模型生成的战略对话
Social-deduction games give models opposing objectives, but they also give roles different information and speaking obligations. We analyze 5,404 public utterances from 150 Chinese Werewolf games played by 12 models, comparing ordinary wolves with ordinary villagers so that special roles are excluded. Private knowledge and goals still differ, and no utterance is verified as true or false. After model and length adjustment, wolf utterances score higher on both certainty and uncertainty (coefficients +0.074 and +0.072). Emotional intensification has the largest rank-biserial effect (r = .145), but it depends on a single degree marker and correlates weakly with human-rated affect. Three annotators' ratings of 392 utterances show higher wolf scores on four behavioral dimensions, and five LLM judges reach 60.8%-70.2% accuracy on a shared evaluation set. In a generation probe that prompts six models with a wolf identity but a villager speaking goal under fixed public context, certainty (+0.164) and emotional intensification (+0.095) increase. These findings describe role-associated language rather than verified lying, and they argue for explicit comparison groups and lexicon sensitivity checks in game-based deception evaluation. Data and code are available at https://github.com/justinchoi929/Tells-of-a-Lying-Machine

Sports Agent: A Self-Evolving System for Conversational Data Analytics
体育智能体：一种用于对话数据分析的自演化系统
Conversational sports analytics requires accurate, evidence-based responses with low latency. Changes in query distributions, data resources, and model behavior can degrade static agent configurations, motivating controlled evolution guided by runtime evidence. We present Sports Agent, a self-evolving framework integrating an Agentic SQL Model for structured-data reasoning, a harness for planning and tool orchestration, and a unified Feedback Engine connecting online execution with offline evolution. The engine assesses responses and execution trajectories, verifies factual claims, diagnoses failures, and generates evolution signals. Model evolution combines SQL-to-Question Scaling for coverage expansion with Graph-Guided Scaling for error-targeted synthesis. Generated questions undergo trajectory distillation and quality filtering for iterative supervised fine-tuning, followed by a separate Post-RL stage. Harness evolution translates recurrent failures into bounded prompt and skill updates, validated through independent audits, execution replay, and regression testing. Performance-tolerance checks guard against unacceptable degradation during simplification. The resulting Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-based model outperforms Qwen3.8-Max on sports question answering and analysis while achieving approximately three times the inference efficiency. Across 30 harness-evolution rounds, the aggregate performance score improves by 10.8 points. In a live sports analytics product, the evolved system improves factual correctness by 10.5 points and average response length, a proxy for answer richness, by 158.1%, while reducing average latency by 41.3% relative to the baseline.

对话式体育分析需要低延迟、基于证据的准确响应。查询分布、数据资源和模型行为的变化会降低静态智能体配置的性能，因此需要基于运行时证据的受控演化。我们提出了Sports Agent，一个集成代理SQL模型（用于结构化数据推理）、规划与工具编排框架以及统一反馈引擎的自演化框架。该引擎评估响应和执行轨迹，验证事实声明，诊断失败，并生成演化信号。模型演化结合了SQL到问题的扩展以扩大覆盖范围，以及基于图的扩展以针对性合成错误修复。生成的问题经过轨迹提炼和质量过滤，用于迭代监督微调，随后进入独立的后强化学习阶段。框架演化将重复失败转化为有界提示和技能更新，通过独立审计、执行回放和回归测试进行验证。性能容差检查防止简化过程中的不可接受性能下降。最终基于Qwen3.6-35B-A3B的模型在体育问答与分析任务上优于Qwen3.8-Max，同时推理效率提升约三倍。在30轮框架演化中，综合性能得分提升了10.8分。在实际体育分析产品中，演化后的系统使事实正确性提升10.5分，平均响应长度（作为答案丰富度的代理）提升158.1%，平均延迟相比基线降低41.3%。

LogToPersona: New Benchmark and Efficient Persona Prediction through Semantic Log Filtering
LogToPersona：新基准与通过语义日志过滤实现的高效人格预测
Detailed knowledge of a user's persona is valuable for personalizing generative AI models and agents, yet such details are difficult to obtain and change over time. We address the task of inferring persona attributes from a single day of mobile activity logs. No suitable publicly available benchmark exists for this task, so we introduce a synthetic benchmark of 1,600 persona-log pairs. We target on-device inference so that raw logs never leave the phone, which makes memory rather than speed the limiting constraint: a day of activity yields roughly 30K tokens, whose key-value cache alone exceeds the INT4 weights of a 4B-parameter model. We therefore propose a log filtering method that retains only behaviorally distinct entries. Filtering reduces the memory footprint by up to 39% across nine models from four families and more than halves prefill time, while maintaining or improving prediction quality. For Qwen3-4B it removes 2.5GB, bringing the model from well beyond a realistic per-process budget to a manageable one. Within our benchmark, these results indicate that preserving diverse behavioral events matters more for persona prediction than retaining every semantically similar log entry.

Using AI to Simulate the Behaviour of European Populations
利用AI模拟欧洲人口的行为
Language models can stand in for survey respondents, but leading approaches build their simulated people from interviews or individual records. We show that a model can learn how European population groups respond from published statistics, without interviews and without training on individual records. We fine-tune an open-weight language model on more than 12 million published group-level answer distributions from about 40 European countries, setting it up to predict each group's departure from a reference distribution. On opinion surveys from its training families it calls the direction of group differences right 84% of the time, ahead of a demographic statistical model (77%, paired test) and level with Claude Opus 5 and 5.5, which it also ties on accuracy; the frontier models are more accurate on new questions. On 278 held-out questions, almost all from the survey families seen in training, its error is 12% lower than that statistical model. It runs on a single 32 GB GPU or a high-memory laptop, and in a test with an earlier model, an evidence gate calibrated on half of a survey's questions fell back to the reference where the model's adjustments did not help. Coupled to a transport simulation of a planned metro closure, with a conventional elasticity as the reference, it adds a testable age gradient to the response, which we freeze for later scoring.

Evaluating Large Language Models on conversational African American English: Feature classification and next-turn generation
在对话式非裔美国人英语上的大语言模型评估：特征分类与下一回合生成
Large Language Models (LLMs) are known to misinterpret and misrepresent regional and social varieties of English other than Mainstream American English (MAE), such as African American English (AAE), yet most evaluations of these biases have relied on synthetic, stylized or decontextualized data. This paper instead evaluates LLMs on authentic, conversational AAE to more closely reflect how such models may encounter AAE when deployed as chatbots. Concretely, we perform two complementary evaluation tasks on 100 six-turn segments randomly sampled from the Corpus of Regional African American Language (CORAAL; Kendall and Farrington, 2024), comparing three closed-weight LLM configurations: Gemini 2.5 Flash with and without reasoning, and GPT-4.1 mini. The first task compares the models against gold-standard manual annotations for five AAE morphosyntactic features, while the second assesses their ability to generate contextually appropriate next turns, evaluating outputs for AAE feature frequency, diversity (Self-BLEU), and pragmatic quality. Reasoning-enabled Gemini is the strongest feature detector by a clear margin (macro F1 of 0.91), outperforming both other LLMs as well as a finetuned BERT-based baseline (Masis et al., 2022). In generation, however, all LLMs underproduce AAE relative to the human speakers and produce relatively uniform continuations, with reasoning-enabled Gemini producing the least AAE and the least diverse output. We conclude that a model's ability to detect AAE does not straightforwardly translate into its spontaneous use of AAE in generation, and argue that addressing this gap requires changes upstream, in how pretraining and alignment data represent dialectal variation.

👤 Romeo De Timmerman, Thomas Demeester, Els Lefever 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

AI-human hybrid conversations: Can AI Agents Improve Discourse Quality?
AI-人类混合对话：AI代理能否提升话语质量？
Conversations are central to how people exchange information, share experiences, and develop collective understanding. Yet, across conversational settings, discussions often suffer from uneven participation, unanswered contributions, and a narrow range of perspectives. As generative AI agents increasingly participate alongside humans, hybrid human-AI conversations present a new opportunity to address these challenges. We investigate whether AI agents can serve as catalysts for human interaction, improving conversational engagement and broadening collective discourse. We develop a framework for introducing generic and persona-conditioned AI agents into evolving discussions and evaluate it through two complementary studies. First, using agent-based simulations grounded in Reddit discussions across four domains, we find that AI-generated conversations are more positive and less toxic than human conversations, but substantially less semantically diverse. Persona conditioning produces more human-like contributions and narrows this diversity gap, while increasing the representation of underrepresented personas can shift which perspectives emerge without necessarily increasing overall diversity. Second, we conduct a pre-registered experiment with 221 participants across nine discussion threads, comparing human-only conversations with those involving generic or persona-conditioned AI agents. We find that AI participation increases responsiveness, reduces unanswered contributions, and broadens the range of ideas expressed by human participants, even though the AI agents themselves do not fully reproduce human conversational diversity. These findings suggest that the value of hybrid human-AI conversations lies not simply in the content AI agents generate, but also in their potential to stimulate more responsive and diverse interactions among humans.

对话是人们交流信息、分享经验并形成集体理解的核心方式。然而，在各类对话场景中，讨论常面临参与不均、回应缺失以及视角狭窄等问题。随着生成式AI代理越来越多地与人类共同参与对话，人机混合对话为解决这些问题提供了新机遇。我们探究AI代理是否能作为人类互动的催化剂，提升对话参与度并拓宽集体话语范围。我们构建了一个框架，将通用型和人格化AI代理引入动态讨论，并通过两项互补研究进行评估。首先，基于Reddit四个领域的讨论进行基于代理的模拟发现，AI生成的对话更积极且毒性更低，但语义多样性显著不足。人格化条件可使AI贡献更接近人类风格，缩小多样性差距；同时增加代表性不足的人格类型可改变浮现的观点，而不一定提高整体多样性。其次，我们在九个讨论线程中对221名参与者进行了注册实验，比较纯人类对话与包含通用或人格化AI代理的对话。结果表明，AI参与提高了响应性，减少了未回应内容，并拓宽了人类参与者表达的观点范围，尽管AI代理自身并未完全复现人类对话的多样性。这些发现表明，人机混合对话的价值不仅在于AI生成的内容，更在于其激发人类之间更具响应性和多样性的互动潜力。

When the Knowledge Runs Out: A Part-Level Pilot Evaluation of How Language Models Handle Partially Covered Questions
当知识耗尽时：语言模型处理部分覆盖问题的局部级初步评估
Assistants that answer from supplied reference material often receive material that covers some parts of a user's question and not others. Existing evaluations of insufficient context mostly score one answer per question, and often treat partially sufficient context as simply insufficient. We describe a small, open evaluation that scores each part of a three-part question separately, with a verdict for parts that are answered correctly from the material but carry an added unsupported claim. On 30 cases (five scenarios, six coverage patterns, five samples each) we test claude-haiku-4-5 and llama3.1:8b. Without an explicit instruction to stay within the material, both models fill most gaps from their own knowledge (unsupported-claim rates of 0.71 and 0.90). One added sentence instructing them to answer only from the material brings these rates to 0.00 and 0.06, consistent with prior work. What remains is less visible: answers that strengthen the material, for example turning "many job sites prohibit bots" into a flat "no". We also report on the judge. Three rounds of blind hand labelling show that this added-claim verdict is the hardest to apply, for the judge and the author alike; RAGAS faithfulness is unsuitable for this task because it penalises correct withholding; and a difference between the two models that is clear under one judge disappears when the judge model is replaced. The dataset, code, hand labels and per-run figures are public.
中文翻译
依赖所提供参考材料回答问题的助手经常遇到部分覆盖用户问题的情况。现有对上下文不足的评估大多对每个问题仅给出一个答案评分，且常将部分充分的上下文视为完全不足。我们描述了一项小型开放评估，对三部分问题中的每一部分分别评分，并对那些虽从材料中正确回答但附加了未经支持主张的部分给出判断。在30个案例（五个情景、六种覆盖模式、每种五次样本）中测试claude-haiku-4-5和llama3.1:8b。在无明确指令要求仅基于材料作答时，两个模型均从自身知识填补大部分空白（未经支持主张率分别为0.71和0.90）。仅添加一句指令要求仅依据材料作答，便将该比率降至0.00和0.06，与以往研究一致。但更隐蔽的问题仍存在：如将“许多职位网站禁止机器人”强化为“禁止”。我们还报告了人工标注情况。三轮盲评显示，该附加主张判断最难应用，对标注者和作者均具挑战；RAGAS忠实度指标不适用于此任务，因其会惩罚正确的保留行为；且在一位标注者下明显存在的模型差异，在更换标注模型后消失。数据集、代码、人工标注及每轮结果均已公开。

Who Attributes a Mind to Whom? Mutual Theory of Mind in Simulated User-Assistant Conversation
谁赋予谁心智？模拟用户-助手对话中的相互心智理论
When users assume that an AI chatbot has mental states, they are more likely to develop over-reliance, emotional dependence, and in extreme cases, psychological harm or self-harm. However, how these mind attributions arise in conversations and how human or AI factors influence this is still unknown. We study how users attribute minds using a minimal framework: a simulated user with different characteristics chats with assistants that differ in social style and memory. We also introduce a message-level measure of who attributes a mind to whom in four directions, checked with trained coders and applied by a fine-tuned encoder that reproduces an LLM judge. Our results show that, on average, nine out of thirteen characteristics raised simulated users' attribution to the assistant. We found that relational needs such as attachment anxiety raised it as theory predicts, some general personality traits raised it as well, and AI-specific characteristics the least. By contrast, the assistant's social style barely changed simulated users' mind attribution. Instead, sycophantic instructions raised the assistant's attribution to itself. Memory raised users' attribution only with some user models. The model playing the user changed overall attribution and the effects of characteristics, style, and memory. Our framework measures how persona descriptions affect mind attribution in simulation. It gives concrete hypotheses for human studies and shows the need for more consistent user simulators.
中文翻译
当用户认为AI聊天机器人具有心理状态时，更容易产生过度依赖、情感依附，甚至极端情况下导致心理伤害或自残。然而，这种心智归因如何在对话中产生，以及人类或AI因素如何影响这一过程仍不清楚。我们采用最小化框架研究用户心智归因：具有不同特征的模拟用户与具有不同社交风格和记忆能力的助手进行对话。我们还引入一种消息级别的双向心智归因测量方法，经训练编码员验证，并由微调后的编码器模型复现LLM裁判。结果显示，平均而言，十三种特征中有九种提升了模拟用户对助手的心智归因。符合理论预测的是，关系需求如依恋焦虑提升了归因，部分一般人格特质也如此，而AI特定特征影响最小。相反，助手的社交风格对心智归因影响甚微；但奉承性指令却提升了助手对自己的心智归因。记忆仅在某些用户模型中提升了用户的心智归因。扮演用户的模型改变了整体归因水平及其对特征、风格和记忆的影响。我们的框架衡量了人物设定如何影响模拟中的心智归因，为人类研究提出具体假设，并凸显了需要更一致的用户模拟器。

Lost in Translation, Not in Confidence: Benchmarking and Calibrating Multilingual Decision Models
翻译迷失，信心未失：多语言决策模型的基准测试与校准
Decision models are language models fine-tuned to return a probability for each option of a typed query, which routers and guardrails threshold directly. They are built on multilingual bases but ranked almost entirely in English. We evaluate 25 top-ranked open-weight decision models on 17 tasks in 14 languages, comparing each language with English on parallel items. Every model loses accuracy in every language, from about 2 points in high-resource languages to 8 in Tamil, while confidence falls only half as much: a threshold set for 95\% accuracy in English yields 90% in Tamil. The size of the loss is set by the language and the base model rather than by leaderboard rank. In 81 controlled fine-tuning runs, task skill learned in English transfers to other languages and a few thousand target-language items narrow large losses, but neither more data nor full-parameter tuning closes them. Finally, the same test items are answered more accurately in all 23 benchmark--language pairs we compare when an LLM rather than a person translated them, so LLM-translated test sets understate the gap.
中文翻译
决策模型是经过微调的语言模型，可为类型化查询的每个选项返回概率，路由器和防护机制直接使用这些概率进行阈值判断。这些模型基于多语言基础，但排名几乎完全以英语为主。我们评估了25个排名靠前的开源权重决策模型在14种语言中的17项任务表现，并将每种语言与英语在平行任务上进行对比。每个模型在所有语言中均出现准确率下降，从高资源语言约2个百分点到泰米尔语的8个百分点不等，而置信度仅下降一半：针对英语设定95%准确率的阈值在泰米尔语中仍可达到90%。损失大小由语言和基础模型决定，而非排行榜排名。在81次受控微调实验中，英语中学到的任务技能可迁移到其他语言，少量目标语言数据可缩小大范围损失，但无论增加数据量还是全参数微调都无法彻底消除。最后，在所有23个基准-语言对中，当测试题由大语言模型而非人类翻译时，回答准确率更高，说明由LLM翻译的测试集低估了实际差距。

Focus On Facts: Stylistically Invariant and Factually Sensitive Text Embeddings
聚焦事实：风格不变且事实敏感的文本嵌入
Text embedding models can conflate surface-form similarity with factual identity. Given an altered near-copy with one corrupted fact and a differently worded fact-preserving rewrite, the base model prefers the altered near-copy. We test whether hard-negative contrastive supervision can reduce this surface-similarity shortcut. We construct FOF-Bench, an 83,263-triplet benchmark spanning five domains. Each anchor is paired with an LLM-generated, fact-preserving stylistic rewrite and a matched fact-altered near-copy. We fine-tune FOF-80M (Focus On Facts), an 80M-parameter embedding model, with a triplet contrastive objective that ranks the rewrite above the factual alteration. On 8,327 held-out test triplets, the base model chooses the factually altered text in 99.58% of cases (0.42% factual preference rate; mean separation -0.1650). After fine-tuning, mean positive--negative separation is +0.0329, 98.5% of individual margins move in the intended direction, and the preference rate reaches 29.37%. On DiSC, an external style-transfer corpus, mean cross-style similarity increases from 0.7905 to 0.9559. The model also penalizes omission and transfers poorly to directional tasks such as NLI and summarization. We characterize FOF-80M as a soft factual fingerprint for studying factual retention under stylistic variation, not as a general-purpose factuality metric.
中文翻译
文本嵌入模型可能混淆表面形式相似性与事实同一性。给定一个包含一处事实错误的近似复制版本和一个措辞不同但保留事实的改写版本，基础模型更倾向于选择前者。我们检验硬负样本对比监督是否能减少这种表面相似性捷径。我们构建了FOF-Bench，一个涵盖五个领域的83,263组三元组基准测试。每个锚点配对一个由大语言模型生成的事实保留型风格改写版本和一个匹配的事实篡改近似复制版本。我们对一个8000万参数的嵌入模型FOF-80M进行微调，采用三元组对比目标，使改写版本得分高于事实篡改版本。在8,327个保留测试三元组上，基础模型在99.58%的情况下选择了事实被篡改的文本（事实偏好率为0.42%；平均分离度为-0.1650）。微调后，正负样本平均分离度提升至+0.0329，98.5%的个体边际方向正确，偏好率上升至29.37%。在DiSC这一外部风格迁移语料库上，跨风格相似度从0.7905提升至0.9559。该模型对遗漏也具有惩罚作用，但在方向性任务如自然语言推理和摘要生成中迁移能力较差。我们将其视为一种软事实指纹，用于研究风格变化下的事实保留情况，而非通用的事实性度量工具。

Extraneous Cognitive Load in Large Language Models
大型语言模型中的额外认知负荷
Cognitive Load Theory (CLT) shows that human problem-solving is affected by both intrinsic load, arising from task difficulty, and extraneous load, arising from how task information is presented. Large language models (LLMs) show analogous effects: presentation alone can affect model performance, but these effects have not yet been systematically isolated from task difficulty. To address this gap, we introduce CogLoadBench, a controlled benchmark that varies five CLT-inspired presentation factors across three reasoning task families. Across models, we find that higher extraneous load conditions often reduce accuracy by either adding competition or complicating the reasoning path. To support diagnosis and mitigation of load-related effects, we propose the Extraneous Load Score (ELS), a prompt-time metric computed from activations that estimates a model's extraneous load, and show that it generalizes across reasoning task types and load levels. We further show that shifting model representations toward lower-load directions at inference time can improve average model performance without changing prompt content.
中文翻译
认知负荷理论（CLT）表明，人类解决问题的表现受到内在负荷（源于任务难度）和额外负荷（源于任务信息呈现方式）的影响。大型语言模型（LLMs）表现出类似效应：仅信息呈现方式就会影响模型性能，但这些影响尚未与任务难度系统地区分开来。为填补这一空白，我们提出了CogLoadBench，一个受控基准测试，通过在三个推理任务类别中变化五个受CLT启发的呈现因素。在多个模型中，我们发现更高的额外负荷条件通常会降低准确率，原因在于增加了竞争或复杂化了推理路径。为支持对负荷相关效应的诊断与缓解，我们提出了一种额外负荷评分（ELS），这是一种在提示阶段计算的激活指标，可估计模型的额外负荷，并证明其在不同推理任务类型和负荷水平间具有泛化能力。我们进一步表明，在推理时将模型表示向低负荷方向调整，可在不改变提示内容的前提下提升平均模型性能。

Verified experience improves materials prediction with large language models
经验证据提升大语言模型在材料预测中的表现
Materials discovery is an iterative loop: experiments and calculations verify predictions, and each outcome can inform the next. Yet most predictors are specialized single-task models fixed after training. Large language models have recently emerged as predictors across heterogeneous tasks but generally underperform specialized models, and retrieval-augmented variants discard their own outcomes. Benchmarks also score queries in isolation, leaving improvement from outcomes untested. Here we construct MatMemBench, a sequential benchmark of 22 materials tasks that reveals outcomes only after prediction and tests online adaptation, memory retention and cross-task transfer. We present MatRecall, a framework that lets large language models store verified predictions, with evidence, outcome and error, as episodes and recall them without retraining. MatRecall improved median performance by 62% over a memory-free model, exceeded specialized models on 11 tasks, remained useful after feedback stopped and transferred between related tasks. Verified experience could become a reusable resource for self-improving discovery loops.

材料发现是一个迭代循环：实验和计算验证预测，每次结果均可指导下一次预测。然而，大多数预测模型是训练完成后固定的单任务专用模型。大语言模型最近作为跨异构任务的预测器出现，但通常表现不如专用模型，且检索增强型变体会丢弃自身的预测结果。基准测试也孤立地评估查询，未测试结果带来的改进。本文构建了MatMemBench，一个包含22个材料任务的序列化基准测试，仅在预测后揭示结果，用于测试在线适应、记忆保持和跨任务迁移。我们提出MatRecall框架，使大语言模型能够以“事件”形式存储经验证的预测结果，包括证据、结果和误差，并在无需重新训练的情况下进行回忆。MatRecall使中位性能相比无记忆模型提升了62%，在11个任务上超越了专用模型，即使反馈停止后仍具实用性，并能在相关任务间实现迁移。经验证据可能成为自改进发现循环中的可复用资源。

When Every Rollout Fails: Splicing Learner-Conditioned Options into Long-Horizon Agent RL
当每次轨迹都失败时：将学习者条件化选项拼接进长时程智能体强化学习
Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often yields all-fail groups on long-horizon agent tasks: every sampled trajectory receives zero reward, leaving group-relative methods without a reward-based policy gradient. A common remedy supplies complete solutions from a stronger model, but full trajectories are expensive and expose the learner to long off-policy histories. We introduce SPLICE, which constructs local training targets from failed current-policy trajectories online. For each all-fail group, a proposal model selects a splice point in a current-policy trajectory and generates several bounded options from that point. We estimate each option's value using repeated current-policy continuations, convert positive estimates into a return-weighted local target, and fit the current policy on the options. We evaluate SPLICE across two policy families on two long-horizon agent tasks, Software Engineering with SWE-bench Verified and Deep Research with BrowseComp-Plus. SPLICE outperforms GRPO and full-trajectory guidance baselines in all four settings, improving over GRPO by 2.8--9.8 percentage points. On initially failed SWE tasks, it raises GRPO's graduation rate by 10.8 percentage points. Further experiments show that SPLICE improves performance while reducing costly proposal-model computation.
中文翻译
基于群体的强化学习在长时程智能体任务中常出现全失败群体：所有采样轨迹均获得零奖励，导致基于奖励的群体相对方法无法生成策略梯度。常见解决方案是从更强模型中提供完整解法，但完整轨迹成本高且使学习者暴露于长期离策略历史中。我们提出SPLICE，通过在线构建失败当前策略轨迹的局部训练目标。对于每个全失败群体，提议模型在当前策略轨迹中选择一个拼接点，并从该点生成多个有界选项。我们通过重复当前策略延续来估计每个选项的价值，将正向估计转换为返回加权的局部目标，并在这些选项上拟合当前策略。我们在两个策略族和两个长时程智能体任务（软件工程中的SWE-bench Verified和深度研究中的BrowseComp-Plus）上评估SPLICE，结果表明其在全部四个设置中均优于GRPO和完整轨迹引导基线，性能提升2.8至9.8个百分点。在初始失败的SWE任务上，其将GRPO的毕业率提升10.8个百分点。进一步实验显示，SPLICE在提升性能的同时减少了昂贵的提议模型计算量。
👤 Haowen Hou, Yicheng Liu, Zhen Huang, Jiaqi Wang 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Neural Rendering Compiler: Generative Video Models as Programmable Renderers
神经渲染编译器：可编程渲染的生成式视频模型
Generative video models are becoming powerful renderers, yet lack the predictable control interfaces of conventional systems such as HTML/CSS engines or Blender. Their capabilities emerge implicitly from training, making it difficult to determine what they can reliably execute. We present Neural Rendering Compiler (NeurCo), which automatically probes frozen video models to construct model-specific capability manuals describing which semantic requirements they can reliably satisfy. NeurCo represents user intent in a model-agnostic intermediate representation of objects, constraints, events, and cameras, then compiles it into model-specific execution strategies. Reliable requirements remain unchanged, while unreliable ones are selectively rewritten or compiled into alternative execution strategies. We introduce VComBench, a benchmark of 380 compositional, multi-requirement tasks spanning text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video generation. NeurCo improves requirement satisfaction from 46.8% to 52.3% on Wan and from 66.5% to 69.3% on Minimax H3, and achieves further gains when combined with prompt enhancers, including on Seedance 2.5. Our analysis reveals model-specific differences in control capabilities and trade-offs between controllability and photorealism, highlighting capability-guided compilation as a promising approach to programmable generative video rendering.

生成式视频模型正变得越来越强大，但缺乏传统系统（如 HTML/CSS 引擎或 Blender）那样的可预测控制接口。其能力隐含于训练过程中，难以确定它们能可靠执行的内容。我们提出神经渲染编译器（NeurCo），通过自动探测冻结的视频模型，构建描述其可可靠满足的语义需求的特定模型手册。NeurCo 将用户意图以模型无关的中间表示（对象、约束、事件、相机）表达，然后编译为特定模型的执行策略。可靠的请求保持不变，不可靠的请求则被选择性重写或编译为替代执行策略。我们引入 VComBench，一个包含 380 个组合性、多需求任务的基准，涵盖文本到视频、图像到视频以及参考到视频生成。NeurCo 在 Wan 上将需求满足率从 46.8% 提升至 52.3%，在 Minimax H3 上从 66.5% 提升至 69.3%，并与提示增强技术结合后在 Seedance 2.5 上进一步提升。我们的分析揭示了模型间控制能力的差异以及可控性与逼真度之间的权衡，凸显了基于能力引导的编译在可编程生成式视频渲染中的前景。

Single-Database Rewards Are Hackable: Counterfactual Execution for Trustworthy Text-to-SQL RL
单数据库奖励可被攻破：用于可信文本到SQL强化学习的反事实执行
Reinforcement learning (RL) for text-to-SQL agents pays a query when its result matches the reference on one database. However, a wrong query can match by luck when the data lack the rows that expose its bug. We study a counterfactual-database (CF) reward that also runs the final query on a few hidden, code-generated copies of the database and pays only if all results match (strict form) or by the share matched (mean form). On 1,302 synthetic audit questions, the strict form lowers the share of buggy queries paid in full from 29.5% to 16.8% and of reward hacks from 54.1% to 1.9%, rejecting 0 of 2,847 correct queries; copies from a family-blind generator do about as well. In a controlled GRPO comparison on a 3.35B model with three seeds per arm, training on the mean form did not improve in-domain accuracy (8.4% vs 11.7%), because the two rewards disagreed on only 5-7% of rollouts; on Spider it gained 1.6 points. One seed reverses both signs, so single-seed reward comparisons mislead. Code: https://github.com/RishiiGamer2201/counterfactual-sql-rewards ; data, models, demo: https://huggingface.co/counterfactual-sql
中文翻译
针对文本到SQL智能体的强化学习（RL）在查询结果与参考答案匹配时支付奖励，但错误查询可能因数据缺乏暴露其缺陷的行而偶然匹配。我们研究了一种反事实数据库（CF）奖励机制，该机制在几个隐藏的、由代码生成的数据库副本上运行最终查询，并仅在所有结果一致（严格形式）或匹配比例一致（均值形式）时才支付奖励。在1,302个合成审计问题上，严格形式将完全支付的错误查询比例从29.5%降至16.8%，奖励欺骗比例从54.1%降至1.9%，同时拒绝了0个正确查询；来自无家族感知生成器的副本效果相当。在3.35B模型上进行受控GRPO对比实验（每臂三个种子），使用均值形式训练并未提升域内准确率（8.4% vs 11.7%），因为两种奖励在仅5-7%的轨迹上存在分歧；但在Spider数据集上提升了1.6分。一个种子反转了两个指标的方向，表明单种子奖励比较具有误导性。代码：https://github.com/RishiiGamer2201/counterfactual-sql-rewards；数据、模型、演示：https://huggingface.co/counterfactual-sql
👤 Shreshth Rai, Rishii Kumar Singh, Aksh Aggarwal 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

MedSkill-E$^{2}$: Evaluating and Evolving Skills for Medical Agents
MedSkill-E$^{2}$：评估与演化医疗智能体技能
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use skills to access domain knowledge and complete real-world tasks. We observe that even when a skill specifies the rules and procedures needed for a task, agents may fail to apply them effectively during autonomous execution, a phenomenon we call skill Enable But Underuse (EBU). This issue is particularly consequential in medical tasks, where overlooking a single critical constraint can yield a plausible but incorrect result. To evaluate and address EBU, we introduce MedSkill-E$^{2}$, a framework for evaluating and evolving skills for medical agents. We construct EBU-Bench, a challenging benchmark covering 12 medically relevant task groups and associated skills, to evaluate agents' ability to use available skills autonomously. We quantify the EBU gap as the difference in skill-use accuracy between autonomous use and explicitly guided reading and application of the same skill. On EBU-Bench, all evaluated model-harness combinations exhibit EBU, with a mean gap of 43.94 percentage points (pp). For the same model, skill-use accuracy under autonomous use differs by up to 38.96 pp across harnesses. Motivated by these observations, we propose EBU-Evolve, which uses execution feedback from autonomous and explicitly guided skill use to distill effective guidance into reusable skill instructions. Relative to their pre-evolution versions, the revised skills improve skill-use accuracy during autonomous execution by 33.33 to 55.17 pp and continue to yield improvements when transferred across multiple models and harnesses. Our results show that autonomous skill-use accuracy varies across harnesses with the model and skill held fixed, and model rankings can change. EBU can persist even after a skill is opened. EBU-Evolve improves autonomous skill-use accuracy on unseen tasks within the same skill, with gains transferring across models and harnesses.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地使用技能来访问领域知识并完成现实任务。我们观察到，即使技能明确指定了任务所需规则和流程，智能体在自主执行过程中仍可能无法有效应用，这种现象称为‘技能启用但使用不足’（EBU）。该问题在医疗任务中尤为严重，忽略单一关键约束可能导致看似合理但错误的结果。为评估和解决EBU问题，我们提出MedSkill-E$^{2}$，一个用于评估和演化医疗智能体技能的框架。我们构建了EBU-Bench，一个涵盖12个医学相关任务组及其对应技能的挑战性基准，用于评估智能体自主使用可用技能的能力。我们量化EBU差距为自主使用与显式引导阅读和应用同一技能之间的技能使用准确率差异。在EBU-Bench上，所有评估的模型-工具组合均表现出EBU，平均差距达43.94个百分点（pp）。对于同一模型，不同工具组合下的自主技能使用准确率差异可达38.96 pp。基于这些观察，我们提出EBU-Evolve，利用自主与显式引导技能使用中的执行反馈，将有效指导提炼为可复用的技能指令。相较于进化前版本，改进后的技能在自主执行中提升了33.33至55.17 pp的技能使用准确率，并在跨多个模型和工具组合迁移时仍保持提升。结果表明，在固定模型和技能条件下，自主技能使用准确率随工具变化而波动，模型排名也可能改变。EBU即使在技能开放后仍可能持续存在。EBU-Evolve在未见过的任务中提升了同一技能的自主使用准确率，且提升效果可在模型和工具间迁移。
👤 Jingjing Hua, Shaocong Wei, Jianghan Shen, Junzhi Ning, Min Cen et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Out of Sight, Not Out of Clues: Can Video LLMs Guess from Partial Evidence?
看不见，但并非无线索：视频大模型能否从部分证据中推断？
In real-world applications like egocentric recording, visual accessibility, and surveillance review, visual evidence for Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) is often only partially captured. A reliable model should match its response to the evidence: answering when sufficient, guessing from visible clues when partial, and abstaining when absent. Yet existing benchmarks enforce a binary answerable-or-unanswerable split, leaving partial evidence unmeasured and obscuring whether failures arise from missing evidence or misjudging sufficiency. We introduce ClueProbe, a diagnostic benchmark to (1) separate perceiving evidence from judging sufficiency, and (2) validate our diagnosis via targeted fine-tuning. ClueProbe comprises 1,721 human-annotated questions across 598 natural videos, labeled Answerable, Guessable, or Unanswerable, alongside controlled perturbations altering wording or visual input. Evaluating 10 open-source and proprietary Video LLMs reveals that failures stem from judgment rather than perception: models perceive the evidence yet lack graded sufficiency awareness, answering or refusing outright while rarely guessing from partial clues. Targeted fine-tuning shows that this reflects missing supervision rather than insufficient capacity: supervising the guess level lifts appropriate guessing from 1.6% to 40.4% without extra false refusals, whereas conventional refusal tuning rejects 92% of guessable questions. Data and code will be released at https://github.com/suat-llm/ClueProbe.
中文翻译
在如第一人称记录、视觉可访问性及监控审查等真实应用场景中，视频大语言模型（Video LLMs）所获取的视觉证据往往仅部分捕获。一个可靠的模型应根据证据情况作出相应响应：证据充分时作答，部分可见时基于可见线索猜测，证据缺失时则放弃回答。然而，现有基准强制执行“可回答”或“不可回答”的二元划分，导致部分证据未被测量，也掩盖了失败是源于证据缺失还是对充分性的误判。我们引入ClueProbe，一种诊断性基准，旨在(1) 区分感知证据与判断充分性，(2) 通过针对性微调验证诊断。ClueProbe包含598段自然视频上的1,721个由人工标注的问题，标注为“可回答”、“可猜测”或“不可回答”，并附有控制性扰动（改变措辞或视觉输入）。评估10个开源和专有Video LLMs发现，失败源于判断而非感知：模型能感知证据，但缺乏对充分性的分级认知，倾向于直接回答或拒绝，很少基于部分线索进行猜测。针对性微调表明，这反映的是监督缺失而非容量不足：通过监督猜测层级，适当猜测率从1.6%提升至40.4%，且未增加误拒率；而传统拒绝微调则拒绝了92%的可猜测问题。数据与代码将发布于：https://github.com/suat-llm/ClueProbe。
👤 Luoxi Zhang, Jiawei Liu, Yanlin Luo, Peiyang He, Junlin Zhu et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Budget, Readout or Reasoning? Where Small Reasoning Models Lose Value in Auction Bidding
预算、读出还是推理？小型推理模型在拍卖竞价中的价值损失来源
Ad platforms and marketplaces show bidders a bid landscape, the estimated chance of winning at each candidate bid, and increasingly let agents built on reasoning language models choose the bid. We give six open reasoning models of 4B to 14B parameters such landscapes, with win curves from eBay closing prices and from a simulated advertising market (AuctionNet), and score their bids on held-out auctions against an exact calculator. Because the agent is handed the estimates, the lost value can be split by cause. Two failures belong to the models. They pass over the best option even when the values written in their own trace already rank it first, and their wrong answers lean toward the highest bid on the menu. The lean is about the bid's size, not its place in the list, and it survives sampling. At 768 forced tokens on eBay, under pay-your-bid pricing, the wrong answers cost bidders 7-17% of the surplus the calculator's bids earn. Arithmetic errors are rare. Two further losses come from operator choices. A token cap cuts traces short: allowed to stop on their own within 4,096 tokens, five models recover 91-100% of the calculator's lead over a model that does no reasoning. Asking for the answer while the reasoning block is still open misreads it: closing the block first lifts the weakest model from 42% to 84% on eBay and from 24% to 81% on AuctionNet. Finally, an audit that flags bids disagreeing with the trace's own numbers, and overrides them with the calculator's bid, raises the share of the calculator's lead recovered at 768 forced tokens from 42-85% to 84-100% on eBay under nested cross-validation, and the same reader settings transfer from eBay to AuctionNet.
中文翻译
广告平台和市场向竞标者展示出价格局，即在每个候选出价下的中标概率，并越来越多地允许基于推理型语言模型构建的智能体自主选择出价。我们向六个参数规模为4B至14B的开放推理模型提供了来自eBay成交价格和模拟广告市场（AuctionNet）的胜率曲线，并将其出价在保留拍卖中与精确计算器的结果进行评分。由于代理已获得估计值，可将价值损失按原因拆分。其中两个失败源于模型自身：即使其自身轨迹中已将最优选项排在首位，仍会忽略该选项；且错误答案倾向于选择菜单中最高金额的出价。这种倾向是出价金额本身所致，而非列表中的位置，且在采样后依然存在。在eBay上强制768个token条件下，采用支付自己出价定价机制时，错误答案导致竞标者损失计算器出价所获盈余的7%-17%。算术错误罕见。另有两项损失来自操作选择：令牌上限截断了推理轨迹；允许自主停止在4096个token内，五个模型恢复了计算器领先优势的91%-100%，而无推理模型则无法做到。在推理块仍开放时要求答案会导致误读：先关闭推理块可使最弱模型在eBay上的表现从42%提升至84%，在AuctionNet上从24%提升至81%。最后，一项审计机制会标记与自身轨迹数值不一致的出价，并以计算器出价覆盖之，使在768个强制token条件下，eBay上保留验证中恢复的计算器领先份额从42%-85%提升至84%-100%；相同设置在eBay与AuctionNet之间具有可迁移性。
👤 Mahbod Khaleti, Seyed Reza Tavakoli, Baran Hoseini, Alireza Noori, Amir Amini et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

What Does Skill Lift Actually Measure? A Source-Code-Level Audit and Noise-Aware Reanalysis of SkillsBench
技能提升实际测量的是什么？SkillsBench的源码级审计与噪声感知再分析
Agent skills are directories of instructions, reference materials, and scripts loaded on demand by coding agents. According to SkillsBench, although human-curated skills can raise the passing rate by about 16.6 percentage points (pp) on average, they have actually reduced the results for some tasks. Later research has started to gate or eliminate skills according to the above per-task gains. We have audited the above benchmark at the source-code level and analysed 7,554 public trials to form 1,421 paired "(configuration, task) cells". Source-code audit identified two controlled-verified cases of grader dependence and one operational definition gap; the average lift on these three tasks was 74.6 pp, and the overall average lift for the full set was 18.7 pp. The audit also found that four tasks contained placeholder skill directories, and a certain execution harness additionally added 48 built-in skills in most runs. No task-level reduction achieved statistical significance in the pooled reanalysis with multiple-testing correction. As there were at most three runs per condition for each cell, the observed negative differences are very susceptible to run-sampling noise, and arithmetically, 58% of the cells could not have shown a decrease. Therefore, the raw numbers of negative cells cannot show that skills cause harm. A gating strategy based on the skill-free score seems to improve by 2-3 pp in the logs; however, even after shuffling condition labels to remove any skill effect, it still achieves an increase of 4-7 pp. On the held-out runs not used for fitting and decision-making, there was no statistically significant improvement. The audit ledger and analysis scripts have been published by us.

智能体技能是编码智能体按需加载的指令、参考材料和脚本目录。根据SkillsBench，尽管人工标注的技能平均可将通过率提高约16.6个百分点（pp），但某些任务的实际结果反而下降。后续研究开始根据任务级增益对技能进行筛选或剔除。我们对上述基准进行了源码级审计，并分析了7,554次公开试验，形成1,421个“(配置, 任务)单元”。源码审计发现两例受控验证的评分依赖案例和一例操作定义缺口；这三个任务的平均提升达74.6 pp，全集平均提升为18.7 pp。审计还发现四个任务包含占位符技能目录，且特定执行框架在多数运行中额外添加了48个内置技能。在经过多重检验校正的合并再分析中，未发现任何任务级削减具有统计显著性。由于每个单元最多仅有三轮运行，观察到的负差异极易受运行抽样噪声影响，且从算术上看，58%的单元不可能出现下降。因此，原始负单元数量无法证明技能造成损害。基于无技能得分的筛选策略在日志中看似提升2-3 pp；然而，即使在打乱条件标签以消除技能效应后，仍能实现4-7 pp的提升。在未用于拟合和决策的保留运行中，也未见统计显著改善。审计记录与分析脚本已由我们发布。

Aligned Machine: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Embedding Similarity via Human Comparative Judgements
对齐机器：通过人类比较判断评估多模态嵌入相似性的基准
Embedding models increasingly inform how people search, recommend, and retrieve information, yet we lack credible ways to test whether these similarity judgments reflect how humans understand meaning. Existing benchmarks typically rely on static, expert-labeled pairs with coarse Likert-style annotations, limiting scalability and making it difficult to obtain consistent, high-confidence annotations. We introduce Aligned Machine, an interactive, public-facing platform that transforms broad participation into a continuously growing Human-Aligned Similarity Benchmark (HASB) for evaluating semantic alignment in embedding-based systems. Participants complete a cognitively-natural, forced-choice task, comparing two pairs of items (text or images) and selecting which pair is more similar. This comparative design is accessible to non-experts, yields clean preference data, and supports robust aggregation. We report results from an initial deployment with 115 participants contributing 2,875 total labeled pairs and 872 unique pairs. Using HASB, we evaluated 12 widely used embedding models and found only partial alignment between embedding similarity and human judgments. Text-only models consistently achieve higher overall agreement than multimodal models, while multimodal performance varies systematically by task type: alignment is strongest for image-image comparisons, weaker for image-text comparisons, and lowest for text-text comparisons. Aligned Machine is designed to evolve over time as participation grows, supporting longitudinal analysis, domain-specific studies, bias detection, and measurement of concept drift across model versions. By combining accessible public participation with rigorous comparative evaluation, HASB provides a scalable and accountable framework for auditing semantic alignment in embedding models.
中文翻译
嵌入模型越来越多地影响人们搜索、推荐和检索信息的方式，但我们缺乏可信的方法来检验这些相似性判断是否反映了人类对意义的理解。现有基准通常依赖静态、专家标注的成对数据，采用粗略的李克特量表注释，限制了可扩展性，且难以获得一致、高置信度的标注。我们提出了“对齐机器”，一个交互式、面向公众的平台，将广泛参与转化为持续增长的人类对齐相似性基准（HASB），用于评估基于嵌入系统的语义对齐。参与者完成一种认知自然的强制选择任务，比较两组项目（文本或图像）并选择哪一组更相似。这种比较设计对非专家也易于理解，产生清晰的偏好数据，并支持稳健聚合。我们报告了首次部署的结果，115名参与者贡献了2,875个标注对和872个唯一对。利用HASB，我们评估了12个广泛使用的嵌入模型，发现嵌入相似性与人类判断之间仅有部分对齐。纯文本模型总体一致性高于多模态模型，而多模态性能系统性地随任务类型变化：图像-图像比较对齐最强，图像-文本比较较弱，文本-文本比较最低。对齐机器设计为随参与度增长而不断演进，支持纵向分析、领域特定研究、偏见检测以及跨模型版本的概念漂移测量。通过结合可访问的公众参与与严谨的比较评估，HASB为审计嵌入模型的语义对齐提供了一个可扩展且可问责的框架。

PeerReveal: Learning to Generalize in AI Peer Review Detection
PeerReveal：在AI同行评审检测中学习泛化
Can we distinguish fully AI-generated reviews from those using AI only to rewrite human judgement? Furthermore, can we do so reliably when reviews are generated from diverse AI models and workflows across scientific domains? To this end, we introduce PeerReveal, a new detector that grounds its decisions in learned relationships between a review's claims and reference claims drawn from AI-generated reviews of the same paper, complemented by review-level textual signals. We evaluate PeerReveal on PeerShift, a new, challenging test set of 5,430 reviews spanning multiple domains, including machine learning, life sciences, and social sciences, combining variation in disciplinary review conventions with diverse AI review-production mechanisms. With regular training, PeerReveal achieves 70.52% pooled Macro-F1 on this benchmark, improving over the strongest baseline by 11.48 points. We develop an effective and practical adversarial training framework that further improves the detector's generalization using a small local generator optimized to produce challenging reviews, raising pooled Macro-F1 from 70.52% to 82.40%. These results show that robust AI-review detection benefits from richer review-level evidence and adversarial training on more challenging generation processes.

我们能否可靠地区分完全由AI生成的评审与仅使用AI重写人类判断的评审？此外，当评审来自不同科学领域的多种AI模型和工作流程时，我们能否做到这一点？为此，我们引入PeerReveal，一种基于评审主张与同一论文的AI生成评审中引用主张之间学习到的关系做出决策的新检测器，辅以评审级别的文本信号。我们在PeerShift上评估PeerReveal，这是一个新的、具有挑战性的测试集，包含5,430篇跨越多个领域（包括机器学习、生命科学和社会科学）的评审，结合了学科评审惯例的变化与多样化的AI评审生成机制。在常规训练下，PeerReveal在该基准上取得70.52%的合并宏F1分数，比最强基线提升11.48个百分点。我们开发了一种有效且实用的对抗训练框架，通过一个小规模本地生成器优化以生成更具挑战性的评审，进一步提升检测器泛化能力，将合并宏F1从70.52%提升至82.40%。结果表明，鲁棒的AI评审检测受益于更丰富的评审级别证据以及在更具挑战性的生成过程上的对抗训练。
👤 Zi Li, André V. Duarte, Lei Li 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
