HumblePRM: Improving VLM Abstention in Ambiguous Situations through Process Reward Modeling
HumblePRM：通过过程奖励建模提升视觉语言模型在模糊情境下的回避能力
Reliable deployment of Vision Language Models (VLMs) requires that a model's expressed confidence remain grounded in what it can actually see, and that it recognize when the visual evidence is too ambiguous to commit to a confident response. Standard post-training uses outcome reward models (ORMs) that supervise only the final decision, rewarding a confident answer regardless of whether the image supports one, while existing methods either tend to detect uncertainty post-hoc or assume an objective verdict always exists. We instead split multimodal reasoning into two stages: perception, where the model must factually describe the scene, and calibration, where it must judge whether that evidence is sufficient to commit to a conclusion. We introduce HumblePRM, a lightweight process reward model (PRM) that scores each stage independently, rewarding agreement between evidence and expressed confidence rather than outcome correctness alone. Training with HumblePRM increases abstention due to uncertainty on ambiguous inputs considerably while leaving accuracy on clear images roughly unchanged. We also find that the improvement transfers to Gemma 4 E4B, a larger, popular edge model. Our findings show that process reward supervision is a promising route to VLMs with better calibrated abstention, ensuring more reliability rather than unpredictable behavior.
中文翻译
可靠部署视觉语言模型（VLMs）要求模型表达的信心与其实际可见内容保持一致，并能在视觉证据过于模糊时识别并避免做出自信回答。标准后训练使用仅监督最终决策的结果奖励模型（ORMs），无论图像是否支持，都会奖励自信答案；而现有方法要么倾向于事后检测不确定性，要么假设始终存在客观结论。我们则将多模态推理分为两个阶段：感知阶段，模型需客观描述场景；校准阶段，模型需判断该证据是否足以得出结论。我们引入HumblePRM，一种轻量级的过程奖励模型（PRM），独立评分每个阶段，奖励证据与表达信心的一致性，而非仅关注结果正确性。使用HumblePRM训练显著提升了在模糊输入下的回避行为，同时在清晰图像上的准确率基本不变。我们还发现该改进可迁移至Gemma 4 E4B这一更大、流行的边缘模型。研究结果表明，过程奖励监督是实现更校准回避行为的有前景路径，从而提升VLM的可靠性而非不可预测行为。

Partial Routing: Trace-Tuned Delegation for Resource-Efficient Agentic Workflows
部分路由：面向资源高效的智能体工作流的轨迹调优委派机制
AI models vary enormously in capability and price. Model routing exploits this by sending easier tasks to cheaper models. However, a single agentic task often combines easy and difficult subtasks, making one model choice for the entire task inefficient. We introduce partial routing, which guides a frontier lead model to delegate suitable subtasks to a cheaper worker model. From both models' past runs, we identify recurring subtasks and measure each model's quality and cost on them. These measurements produce delegation guidance, with a parameter, $δ$, controlling how much is delegated. The lead decides when and what to delegate during execution, without training either model. On held-out tasks from ViBench and Terminal-Bench 4.0, partial routing achieves higher mean task performance than the lead alone and task-level routing. With $δ= 0.75$, it raises the lead's ViBench score from 70.2 to 78.5 at 19% lower cost, and its Terminal-Bench pass rate from 54% to 61% at similar cost. Giving the lead only the subtask breakdown, without a worker, also reduces cost on both benchmarks and raises the Terminal-Bench pass rate. These results show that analysis of past runs can support more efficient planning and more effective delegation, with little cost and implementation overhead.
中文翻译
AI模型的能力和价格差异巨大。模型路由利用这一特性，将简单任务分配给更便宜的模型。然而，单个智能体任务通常包含简单与复杂子任务，单一模型选择整个任务效率低下。我们提出部分路由，引导主模型在执行过程中将合适的子任务委派给更廉价的工作者模型。基于两模型过往运行记录，我们识别出重复出现的子任务，并测量各模型在这些任务上的质量与成本。这些测量结果生成委派指导策略，其中参数δ控制委派程度。主模型在执行期间自主决定何时及如何委派，且无需训练任一模型。在ViBench和Terminal-Bench 4.0的保留任务上，部分路由的平均任务性能优于主模型单独处理或任务级路由。当δ=0.75时，主模型在ViBench上的得分从70.2提升至78.5，成本降低19%；在Terminal-Bench上的通过率从54%提升至61%，成本相近。即使仅提供子任务分解而不配备工作者，也能在两个基准上降低成本并提升Terminal-Bench通过率。结果表明，对历史运行的分析可支持更高效的规划与更有效的委派，且开销极小。

Confidence scores for gated automation of large language models
用于大语言模型门控自动化的置信度评分
AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated decision-making. However, LLMs may produce incorrect answers with high confidence, limiting their applicability in decisions that affect individuals and organizations. Reliable confidence scores are necessary to gate such decisions, and existing estimates are frequently unavailable or too coarse to support effective gating. This work uses supervised learning to train models to predict the correctness of LLM-generated answers, enabling automated confident responses and routing uncertain ones for human review. A linear confidence score head is fitted to the final-layer hidden state of the frozen LLM backbone, and text-only metamodels are trained as an alternative for closed models. All results come from a single 2B-parameter backbone, on graded answers that are about 98% SQuAD and the remainder TruthfulQA. Against multiple baselines, the confidence score head outperforms all baselines and metamodels, automating the largest fraction of the workload at high accuracy targets. Prompted confidence scores can appear well calibrated yet lack the resolution needed for thresholding. Although transferability to unseen datasets is modest, the confidence score head remains the most robust approach. The proposed confidence scoring framework enhances the safety of automating LLM-based agents and can be adapted to gate other properties, including safety or compliance.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLMs）构建的智能体在自动化决策中表现出强大能力。然而，LLMs 可能在高置信度下产生错误答案，限制了其在影响个人和组织决策中的应用。可靠的置信度评分对于控制此类决策至关重要，而现有的估计通常不可用或过于粗略，无法有效支持门控。本文使用监督学习训练模型以预测 LLM 生成答案的正确性，从而实现自动化的高置信度响应，并将不确定的回答路由至人工审核。通过在冻结的 LLM 主干最后一层隐藏状态上拟合线性置信度评分头，并训练仅文本的元模型作为封闭模型的替代方案。所有结果均来自单一 20 亿参数主干，在约 98% 的 SQuAD 和其余 TruthfulQA 的分级答案上获得。与多个基线相比，置信度评分头优于所有基线和元模型，在高准确率目标下自动化了最大比例的工作量。提示式置信度评分看似校准良好，但缺乏阈值判断所需的分辨率。尽管在未见数据集上的可迁移性一般，置信度评分头仍是最稳健的方法。所提出的置信度评分框架提升了基于 LLM 智能体自动化的安全性，且可扩展用于控制其他属性，如安全性和合规性。

Building a Unified Inline Code Editing Model for GitHub Copilot
构建 GitHub Copilot 的统一内联代码编辑模型
AI-assisted coding ranges from fully agentic coding to inline code suggestions in the editor. Inline suggestions require predicting a developer's most likely next edit from an editing session. The next edit may lie at the cursor, in nearby lines, or farther away, and one editing intent can span several of these. The preceding system queries separate completion, nearby edit, and long-distance edit models, each with its own output format, in a fixed sequence; it thus cannot express intents spanning distant locations in one response, can require several sequential model calls per suggestion, and prioritizes completion even when a correction would serve better. We present a unified model that predicts all three edit types in one output space: a sequence of anchored diff patches specifying locations, deleted lines, and added lines. This representation permits multiple edits per response, so the client can serve later edits from a cache without further model calls. We train this model in two phases, first unifying next edit and long-distance edit prediction, then adding code completion. We synthesize multi-edit supervision from single-edit data via model pseudolabeling and edit history playback. We use a binary LLM-graded reward, a patch validity taxonomy shared by data filtering, reward, and evaluation, and grader boosting for targeted failure modes. Phase 2 also adapts how the client processes and presents model outputs. In controlled online A/B experiments in GitHub Copilot, Phase 1 significantly reduces time to display by 6.77% and output tokens by 58.93% relative to the three-model baseline. In Phase 2, the 3-in-1 model and client adaptations reduce dismissed suggestions per user by 10.06% relative to the 2-in-1 model paired with the completion model. Throughout this report, we also share our methodology and learnings in evaluation, training, and end-to-end model and system design.
中文翻译
AI 辅助编程涵盖从完全自主的编程到编辑器内的内联代码建议。内联建议需从编辑会话中预测开发者最可能的下一步编辑。下一步编辑可能位于光标处、附近行或更远位置，一种编辑意图可能跨越多个区域。现有系统分别调用完成、邻近编辑和远距离编辑模型，每种模型输出格式不同，按固定顺序执行；因此无法在一个响应中表达跨越远距离位置的意图，可能需要多次连续模型调用，且即使修正更优也优先推荐补全。我们提出一个统一模型，将三种编辑类型在同一输出空间中预测：一组锚定的 diff 补丁，指定位置、删除行和新增行。该表示允许多个编辑共存于一次响应中，客户端可从缓存中提供后续编辑而无需额外模型调用。我们分两阶段训练该模型：首先统一下一步编辑与远距离编辑预测，然后加入代码补全。通过模型伪标签和编辑历史回放，从单编辑数据合成多编辑监督信号。采用二值 LLM 评分奖励，共享的补丁有效性分类体系用于数据过滤、奖励和评估，并通过评分器增强针对特定失败模式。第二阶段还调整了客户端处理和展示模型输出的方式。在 GitHub Copilot 的受控线上 A/B 实验中，第一阶段相比三模型基线显著减少显示时间 6.77%，输出 token 数减少 58.93%。第二阶段中，3-in-1 模型与客户端优化相比 2-in-1 模型加补全模型，用户拒绝建议次数减少 10.06%。本报告还分享了评估、训练及端到端模型与系统设计的方法论与经验。

Certify What You Plant: Shortcut-Free Proof Depth in Logical Reasoning Data
验证你所设定的：逻辑推理数据中无捷径的证明深度
Procedurally generated logic problems are used to both evaluate and train reasoning in language models. Their difficulty is usually set by planting a proof, that is, by sampling a proof of the desired depth and stating its premises as the problem. However, a planted proof is only one possible proof. A problem can also have shorter proofs, or several unrelated ones, and its stated depth then overstates what solving it requires. We measure what a problem actually requires, relative to a stated set of inference rules. For each problem, we compute the steps every proof must make, the shortest proof, the number of minimal proofs and the indispensable premises, and export certificates that an independent checker verifies. Audits of eight datasets find depth that is forced (ProofWriter, Blocksworld), problems labelled depth 5 that require about two specific rule applications (FLDx2), and answers that need no particular step (ZebraLogic). We then construct problems whose every proof has a requested structure, using a catalog of shortcuts proved once per rule set. Every constructed problem is checked in full and comes with its own certificate. In training, the best data mixes depths and problem types. At equal size, such mixtures of certified problem types, including model-checking problems that forward generation does not produce, transfer better than FLDx2-style derivation data for Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B.
中文翻译
程序生成的逻辑问题被用于评估和训练语言模型的推理能力。其难度通常通过‘植入证明’来设定，即采样一个期望深度的证明，并将前提作为问题陈述。然而，植入的证明只是可能的证明之一，问题也可能存在更短的证明或多个无关的证明，因此所声明的深度会高估解决问题所需的实际要求。我们测量了问题在给定推理规则集下实际需要的复杂度。对于每个问题，我们计算所有证明必须经过的步骤、最短证明、最小证明的数量以及不可或缺的前提，并导出可由独立检查器验证的证书。对八个数据集的审计发现：某些数据集的深度是强制性的（ProofWriter, Blocksworld），某些标注为深度5的问题实际上仅需约两个特定规则的应用（FLDx2），而某些答案根本不需要特定步骤（ZebraLogic）。随后，我们构建了所有证明都具有指定结构的问题，使用每套规则集预先证明的一组捷径清单。每个构造出的问题均经过完整验证并附带自身证书。在训练中，最佳数据组合应混合不同深度和问题类型。在相同规模下，这种经认证的问题类型混合（包括前向生成无法产生的模型检验问题）相较于FLDx2风格的推导数据，在Qwen3模型从0.6B到8B的迁移中表现更优。

CypherMAS: Benchmarking Routing Reliability in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems
CypherMAS：基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中路由可靠性的基准测试
Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) distribute reasoning across specialized agents that must exchange messages and invoke tools reliably. Existing evaluations typically couple communication with open domain tasks, making it difficult to determine whether a failure arises from reasoning, malformed tool use, lost messages, or forgotten requests. Prior benchmarks do not isolate communication execution or measure routing redundancy against a known optimal path. We introduce CypherMAS, a synthetic benchmark that isolates communication and routing in fixed multi-agent graphs. Agents in chain, star, bipartite, or ring graphs route cyphered messages to remote peers holding the matching decoders; since the optimal route is known, redundant LLM calls can be measured and routing failures may be easily tracked. Across eleven open-weight models from 4B to 753B, small models fail at routing and tool use, dense models above 27B degrade with diameter, and successful runs exceed minimum LLM calls. These findings identify communication reliability as a distinct scaling bottleneck for multi-agent systems and provide a controlled diagnostic for complex multi-agent systems. The source code is freely available: https://github.com/Andoree/CypherMAS.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的多智能体系统（MAS）将推理分布在多个专用智能体之间，这些智能体必须可靠地交换消息并调用工具。现有评估通常将通信与开放域任务耦合，难以判断失败是源于推理错误、工具调用格式错误、消息丢失还是请求遗忘。先前的基准测试未隔离通信执行过程，也未在已知最优路径下衡量路由冗余。我们引入CypherMAS，一个合成基准测试，专门隔离固定多智能体图中的通信与路由。在链式、星型、二分图或环形图中，智能体需将加密消息路由至持有匹配解码器的远程同伴；由于最优路径已知，可测量冗余的LLM调用并轻松追踪路由失败。在11个从4B到753B的开源模型上进行测试发现：小型模型在路由和工具使用上表现不佳，超过27B的密集模型随图直径增大而性能下降，成功运行的实例均超过最低LLM调用次数。这些发现揭示通信可靠性是多智能体系统的一个独立缩放瓶颈，并为复杂多智能体系统提供了可控诊断工具。源代码公开可用：https://github.com/Andoree/CypherMAS。

$μ$-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-User Task Completion in Shared Interaction Voice Agents
$μ$-Bench：面向共享交互语音代理的多用户任务完成基准测试
Voice agents in shared spaces must satisfy interdependent multi-user requests while upholding per-user authority, privacy, and task constraints. Existing evaluations offer limited coverage when participants react throughout a session. We introduce $μ$-Bench, a benchmark for joint task completion in closed-loop, full-duplex multi-user voice interaction. Simulated participants act from isolated profiles of goals, knowledge, and permissions, adapting to the agent's speech and tool outcomes. Across 20 domains, 100 scenarios vary group size, acoustics, and follow-up demands to form 800 cases (408 tool-augmented, 392 tool-free). Joint Task Success (JTS) requires all user goals, privacy boundaries, and terminal states to hold jointly, while Pure JTS further disallows unauthorized tool calls; Attribution, Threading, and Coordination (ATC) diagnostics localize failures. Across 13 voice agents, proprietary systems peak at 62.24\% tool-free and 65.44\% tool-augmented success, while open-weight models remain below 20\%. ATC diagnostics show that open-weight models lag primarily in multi-turn attribution and threading, and controlled contrasts reveal that larger groups consistently degrade joint task success across capable systems. By coupling reactive multi-user simulation with verifiable session-level outcomes, $μ$-Bench provides a rigorous testbed for shared voice agents.
中文翻译
在共享空间中的语音代理需在满足各用户权限、隐私及任务约束的前提下，协调处理相互依赖的多用户请求。现有评估在参与者全程互动时覆盖有限。我们提出$μ$-Bench，一个用于闭环全双工多用户语音交互中联合任务完成的基准测试。模拟参与者基于独立的目标、知识和权限配置行动，并根据代理的语音输出和工具结果动态调整。涵盖20个领域，100种情景，变化群体规模、声学环境和后续需求，共形成800个案例（408个工具增强型，392个无工具型）。联合任务成功率（JTS）要求所有用户目标、隐私边界和终止状态同时满足；纯联合任务成功率（Pure JTS）进一步禁止未经授权的工具调用；ATC诊断（归属、线程、协调）用于定位失败原因。在13个语音代理中，专有系统在无工具场景下最高达62.24%，工具增强场景下达65.44%；而开源模型均低于20%。ATC诊断显示，开源模型主要在多轮归属与线程处理方面落后；受控对比实验表明，更大群体始终会降低各能力系统的联合任务成功率。通过结合反应式多用户仿真与可验证的会话级结果，$μ$-Bench为共享语音代理提供了严格的测试平台。

DRBench Pro: A Knowledge-Centric Benchmark for Professional Deep Research Agents
DRBench Pro：面向专业深度研究代理的知识中心型基准测试
Deep research agents gather information from multiple sources and synthesize it into reports on complex, open-ended questions. Existing benchmarks assess report quality along several dimensions, but lack a dedicated knowledge-centric evaluation of which research directions a report covers and how deeply it explores them. We introduce DRBench Pro, a benchmark that makes structured knowledge coverage the primary evaluation target. Our automated pipeline builds local knowledge trees from Wikipedia and uses sampled subgraphs to generate research queries and evidence-grounded rubrics. Each rubric corresponds to a relation in the tree, preserving the structure of the knowledge being evaluated. Agents receive only the research query. This structure allows us to measure breadth across major research directions and depth along related knowledge chains, with their geometric mean giving overall coverage. The benchmark includes 144 cases across 24 domains and 44,102 rubrics. Evaluating 16 deep research agents reveals different strengths in breadth and depth, with the best-performing agent reaching 57.04% overall coverage. Rubric judgments agree closely with human annotations, and agent rankings remain stable across the tested metric configurations.

深度研究代理从多个来源收集信息，并将之整合成关于复杂开放性问题的报告。现有基准测试虽从多个维度评估报告质量，但缺乏对报告所覆盖的研究方向及其深入程度的专门知识中心评价。我们引入DRBench Pro，将结构化知识覆盖作为核心评估目标。我们的自动化流程从维基百科构建本地知识树，并使用采样的子图生成研究问题和基于证据的评分标准。每个评分标准对应树中的一个关系，保留了被评估知识的结构。代理仅接收研究问题，该结构使我们能够测量在主要研究方向上的广度以及相关知识链上的深度，其几何平均值代表整体覆盖度。该基准包含24个领域中的144个案例和44,102个评分标准。对16个深度研究代理的评估揭示了它们在广度与深度上的不同优势，表现最佳的代理达到57.04%的整体覆盖度。评分标准判断与人工标注高度一致，且在不同测试指标配置下代理排名保持稳定。

The Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark: Do Language Models Know the Foundations of Early-Grade Literacy?
语言学知识基准：语言模型是否掌握早期阅读素养的基础知识？
Many languages of instruction in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) are poorly represented in the data used to train language models. Before these models can support early-grade literacy in such languages, we need to know whether they hold the linguistic knowledge of each language that literacy teaching relies on. To test this, we present the Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark and the reproducible, largely automated pipeline that turns curriculum documents, teacher guides and pupil books into a vetted question set organised on a fixed grid of nine linguistic domains and seven grade levels. The pipeline combines LLM-driven source survey, feature consolidation, item generation and quality control with an item-selection step inspired by item response theory and a final expert review. We instantiate it for Kiswahili (Kenya; 135 items) and English (United Kingdom; 137 items) and evaluate 121 and 119 models respectively. Frontier models exceed 90% accuracy in both languages, but accuracy falls steeply for smaller and cheaper models, and more so in Kiswahili. The two best models under 10 billion parameters reach 91.7% and 73.0% in English but only 51.9% and 40.7% in Kiswahili, and the mean accuracy of models deployable on mobile and tablet hardware is 47% in English against 24% in Kiswahili. The models most practical for low-resource classrooms are therefore the least capable in the languages they would serve. We release a 20-item sample per language, all pipeline prompts, open-source code, and a public leaderboard. This is the first rung of a three-level evaluation ladder for measuring and generating decodable text to support foundational literacy.
中文翻译
许多低收入和中等收入国家（LMICs）的教学语言在训练语言模型的数据中代表性不足。在这些语言中，语言模型能否支持早期阅读素养之前，我们需要了解它们是否具备阅读教学所依赖的语言学知识。为此，我们提出了语言学知识基准及一个可复现、高度自动化的流程，该流程将课程文件、教师指南和学生课本转化为经过验证的问题集，按九个语言领域和七个年级水平组织。该流程结合LLM驱动的源调查、特征整合、题目生成与质量控制，并借鉴项目反应理论设计题目选择步骤，最后进行专家评审。我们在基斯瓦希里语（肯尼亚；135题）和英语（英国；137题）中进行了实例化，并分别评估了121个和119个模型。前沿模型在两种语言中的准确率均超过90%，但小型和低成本模型的准确率急剧下降，尤其在基斯瓦希里语中更为明显。参数量低于100亿的两个最佳模型在英语中达到91.7%和73.0%的准确率，但在基斯瓦希里语中仅为51.9%和40.7%；可在移动和平板设备部署的模型平均准确率为英语47%、基斯瓦希里语24%。因此，最适用于低资源课堂的模型恰恰是其服务语言中最不擅长的。我们发布了每种语言20个样本题目、所有流程提示、开源代码和公开排行榜。这是衡量并生成可解码文本以支持基础阅读能力的三阶段评估阶梯的第一步。

VulnSafe: Measuring Vulnerability-Aware Relational Safety in Large Language Models
VulnSafe：测量大语言模型中的脆弱性感知关系安全性
Emotionally supportive interactions require large language models not only to avoid overtly harmful content, but also to recognize vulnerability-related risk, regulate their relational stance, preserve user agency, and provide context-appropriate support. Existing safety benchmarks often measure isolated violations, leaving unclear what underlying response competence their scores represent. We introduce VulnSafe, a Chinese capability assessment centered on Vulnerability-Aware Response Competence (VARC). VARC organizes relational safety into five complementary constructs: Risk Perception, Safety Preservation, Autonomy Protection, Supportive Response, and Boundary Management. Following an evidence-centered design, 41 scenarios elicit construct-relevant behavior through 2,050 balanced prompts and scenario-specific scoring rubrics. The assessment further separates proactive should-do behavior from prohibitive should-not behavior. Across eight representative LLMs, results show distinct competence profiles and larger disparities than prohibitive requirements. VulnSafe therefore makes relational safety interpretable as a multidimensional model capability rather than as refusal compliance alone.
中文翻译

PersonaFactory: Stylistic User Prompt Augmentation with Synthetic Persona Bank
PersonaFactory：基于合成人格库的风格化用户提示增强
Large language models increasingly replace real people in the AI development pipeline, as they are used to generate training dialogues at scale and to drive alignment and evaluation. However, out-of-the-box models produce monolingual and stylistically flat texts as synthetic user prompts, failing to replicate the real user-AI communication mechanics. We present PersonaFactory, an end-to-end pipeline that generates detailed user personas and uses them to augment existing user prompts from their point of view, producing a bank of over 740000 structured synthetic personas detailing their background and communication behaviors, and a corpus of roughly 1.24 million persona-styled synthetic conversations spanning 85 languages. We evaluate the effect of personal features on style adoption and intent preservation, using an LLM-judge and heuristic metrics to assess augmentation fidelity together with the downstream impact on assistant persona control through training data. Results show that our structured, attribute-based style representation enables per-axis control and better intent preservation at comparable style fidelity than free-text persona descriptions used in existing methods. A human expert evaluation found the augmented conversations plausible, with favorable ratings for intent preservation, style naturalness, and multilingual authenticity. Finally, PersonaFactory conversations achieve comparable performance to original training data on capability benchmarks, are preferred on real user prompts at equal reply length, and adapt their reply style more closely to users without explicit steering.
中文翻译
大语言模型越来越多地取代真实人在AI开发流程中的角色，用于大规模生成训练对话，以及推动对齐与评估。然而，开箱即用的模型生成的合成用户提示往往具有单一语言性和风格单调性，无法再现真实的用户-AI交互机制。我们提出PersonaFactory，一个端到端流程，能够生成详细用户人格，并从其视角增强现有用户提示，构建了一个包含超过74万条结构化合成人格的数据库，涵盖背景信息和沟通行为，并生成约124万条跨越85种语言的人格化合成对话。我们评估了个人特征对风格采纳和意图保留的影响，使用LLM裁判和启发式指标评估增强保真度，以及对训练数据中助手人格控制的下游影响。结果表明，我们的结构化属性风格表示实现了轴向控制，在与现有方法使用的自由文本人格描述相当的风格保真度下，显著提升了意图保留效果。人类专家评估认为增强后的对话合理可信，且在意图保留、风格自然性和多语言真实性方面获得正面评价。最终，PersonaFactory生成的对话在能力基准测试中表现与原始训练数据相当，在同等回复长度的真实用户提示下更受青睐，并能在无需显式引导的情况下更紧密地适应用户回复风格。
👤 Bich Ngoc Doan, Hanna Yukhymenko, Imanol Schlag 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

DFly2: Turbocharging Long-Context Speculative Decoding by Sharing the Target's Sparsity
DFly2：通过共享目标稀疏性加速长上下文推测解码
To avoid the quadratic cost of dense attention over long contexts, models such as DeepSeek-V4 and Hy4 have turned natively sparse, and speculative decoding, which matters most where decoding is slowest, increasingly runs on them. Their drafters, however, still keep a context cache of their own, over the whole prompt or a sliding window. Beyond memory, such a cache costs prefill time, prefix-cache hits and decode concurrency. Drafters that read the target's cache avoid one of their own, but settle only which cache, not which positions, and still read everything or a recent window. Neither rule is made for the query at hand, and computing a selection per query in the drafter would cost more as the context grows. Yet a natively sparse target already picks positions for each query, over a cache it keeps anyway. Both of the drafter's choices are therefore already made, and paid for, by the target: its cache and its selection. We measure how far one selection carries: made a step early, it still covers close to nine tenths of what each later token selects on DS-V4.1-Flash. We present DFly2, a block-parallel drafter that shares the target's sparsity. Each draft layer reads the KV its paired target layer cached (KV sharing), at the positions that layer's indexer selected, a fixed number anywhere in the context (Index sharing). DFly2 thus runs no scoring pass and keeps no cache of its own. On DS-V4.1-Flash, GLM-5.3-Flash and Hy4~flash, whose sparse attention and architecture differ, it accepts more tokens per step than DFlash and DSpark trained on the same recipe, over general, long-context and agentic benchmarks. Its margin over the stronger baseline widens from 3.9%-10.5% on general workloads to 10.0%-31.5% on long-context and agentic ones. At high concurrency on DS-V4.1-Flash, at matched per-user decode speed, its throughput is 62% and 81% higher on two 64K sets.
中文翻译
为避免长上下文密集注意力带来的二次计算成本，DeepSeek-V4和Hy4等模型已采用原生稀疏架构，而推测解码——在解码最慢时尤为关键——也越来越多地运行在这些模型上。然而，其草稿模型仍保留自己的完整上下文缓存，覆盖整个提示或滑动窗口。除了内存开销外，这种缓存还会增加预填充时间、前缀缓存命中率和解码并发性开销。若草稿模型读取目标模型的缓存，可省去自身的一个缓存，但仅能决定读取哪个缓存，无法决定读取哪些位置，仍需读取全部或最近窗口内容。这两种规则均未针对具体查询优化，且在草稿模型中为每个查询计算选择策略会随上下文增长而带来更高成本。然而，原生稀疏的目标模型已为每个查询选择出位置，且其缓存本就存在。因此，草稿模型的两项选择——缓存和位置——均已由目标模型完成并支付成本。我们测量了这一选择的传播范围：在DS-V4.1-Flash上，提前一步的选择仍能覆盖后续每个token选择的近90%。我们提出DFly2，一种块并行草稿模型，共享目标模型的稀疏性。每个草稿层读取其对应目标层缓存的KV（KV共享），并在该层索引器选定的位置读取（索引共享），固定数量的任意位置均可。因此，DFly2无需评分遍历，也不保留自身缓存。在DS-V4.1-Flash、GLM-5.3-Flash和Hy4~flash等稀疏注意力和架构各异的模型上，DFly2在每步接受的token数量超过DFlash和DSpark（同配方训练），在通用、长上下文和智能体基准上均表现更优。相较于更强基线，其优势从通用任务的3.9%-10.5%扩大至长上下文和智能体任务的10.0%-31.5%。在DS-V4.1-Flash高并发场景下，相同用户解码速度条件下，吞吐量分别提升62%和81%。
👤 Hong Liu, Rui Cen, Junhan Shi, Lin Niu, Runzhi Fan et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34690]

PoVi: Agentic Poetry-to-Video Generation with Knowledge Grounding and Figurative Understanding
PoVi：基于知识锚定与隐喻理解的智能体诗歌转视频生成
Poetry-to-video generation requires more than translating text into visual scenes. Poetic language is highly compressed, often depends on historical and cultural knowledge, and frequently conveys meaning through metaphor, imagery, and other non-literal expressions. We introduce PoVi, a benchmark and agentic framework for grounded poetry-to-video generation, evaluating semantic grounding, discourse coherence, and audiovisual realization. PoVi reconstructs poem semantics into an evidence-bounded representation and progressively transforms them into narration, interpretation, character assets, and shot-level visual plans, followed by narration-aligned video generation. Across three planning backbones and four video generators, PoVi consistently improves over single-call generation, with the largest gains on implicit background knowledge and metaphorical relations. PoVi provides a testbed for studying knowledge-grounded, long-horizon multimodal generation beyond direct text-to-video synthesis.
中文翻译
诗歌转视频生成不仅需要将文本翻译为视觉场景，还需处理诗意语言的高度压缩性、对历史与文化知识的依赖性，以及通过隐喻、意象等非字面表达传递意义的特点。我们提出了PoVi，一个基准测试与智能体框架，用于评估语义锚定、话语连贯性和音视频实现能力。PoVi将诗歌语义重构为受证据约束的表示形式，并逐步转化为叙述、解释、角色资产和逐镜头视觉计划，随后生成与叙述对齐的视频。在三种规划骨干和四种视频生成器上，PoVi始终优于单次调用生成方法，尤其在隐含背景知识和隐喻关系方面提升显著。PoVi为研究超越直接文本到视频合成的知识锚定型、长时程多模态生成提供了实验平台。

When Skills Fail to Compose: Compatibility and Retention in Language Models
当技能无法组合时：语言模型中的兼容性与保留性
Skill acquisition alone does not establish a reusable skill library: task proficiency does not specify a compositional interface. Motivated by agents that acquire and refine skills, we study independently learned soft-prompt skills on frozen language models, with skill identities and execution order supplied. Across four digit-task bases and three record-editing models, mean composition accuracy in one call is at most 7.6%, compared with 88.2% to 100% over two calls that add computation and an explicit intermediate output. Neutral-insertion training improves robustness but leaves substantial interference, which block length and per-token norm alone cannot explain on two of four digit bases. Blocks of the same operation need not be interchangeable: after training next to frozen learned partners, a post hoc test on two Qwen bases shows that replacing those partners with independently learned blocks of the same operations lowers median composition accuracy from 98.0% and 95.3% to 12.7% and 0.5%, although every replacement scores at least 90% alone. Repair can improve a composition while impairing the original skill, and rehearsal improves retention while transfer remains limited on Qwen2.5-0.5B. Internal interventions constrain explanations without identifying a shared failure mechanism. These findings motivate evaluating compatibility, retention and transfer alongside acquisition when building skill libraries for self-evolving agents. Code is available at https://github.com/YanLin-Quinne/SkillCompatibility.
中文翻译
仅掌握技能并不能建立可复用的技能库：任务熟练度并未定义可组合的接口。受能够获取并优化技能的智能体启发，我们研究了在冻结的语言模型上独立学习的软提示技能，其中技能身份和执行顺序由外部提供。在四个数字任务基准和三个记录编辑模型上，单次调用的平均组合准确率最高仅为7.6%，而两次调用（增加计算量并显式输出中间结果）的准确率则达到88.2%至100%。中性插入训练提升了鲁棒性，但仍存在显著干扰，这种干扰无法仅通过块长度或每标记归一化来解释。相同操作的块并不一定可互换：在两个Qwen基线模型上，经过与冻结学习伙伴共同训练后，事后测试显示，用独立学习的同操作块替换这些伙伴，会使中位组合准确率从98.0%和95.3%降至12.7%和0.5%，尽管每个替换块单独测试得分均不低于90%。修复可能改善组合性能，但会损害原始技能；重练能提升保留性，但在Qwen2.5-0.5B上迁移能力仍有限。内部干预虽限制了解释范围，但未能识别出共享的失败机制。这些发现表明，在构建自演化智能体的技能库时，应同时评估兼容性、保留性和迁移性。代码已公开于 https://github.com/YanLin-Quinne/SkillCompatibility。

Lightweight Reasoning Transfer via Chain-of-Thought Vector Projection
通过思维链向量投影实现轻量级推理迁移
Transferring reasoning capabilities from large language models to smaller ones often requires extensive teacher supervision or costly student adaptation. We study projected cross-model Chain-of-Thought (CoT) Vector transfer, a two-stage approach that keeps both models frozen. First, we learn a task-level CoT Vector in the teacher using dataset-provided solution traces and final-answer supervision. We then train a lightweight linear projector to map the fixed vector into a smaller student's residual space. Student-side training uses only final-answer cross-entropy on a small subset of task training examples; it requires neither teacher outputs nor intermediate solution traces. We select the student injection layer and intervention scale on a labeled support set and report results on the disjoint remainder of each evaluation split. On GSM8K and MATH, the projected vector improves base students by 10.37-21.98 accuracy points across the evaluated teacher-student settings. On four code-generation benchmarks, the Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct student gains 3.23-4.67 pass@1 points, while the base Qwen2.5-7B student gains up to 9.65 points. These results show that a teacher-side reasoning vector can be reused across smaller students with different residual dimensions through projector-only adaptation.
中文翻译
将大型语言模型的推理能力迁移到小型模型通常需要大量教师监督或昂贵的学生适配。我们研究了一种两阶段的跨模型思维链（CoT）向量投影方法，该方法保持两个模型冻结。首先，利用数据集提供的解题轨迹和最终答案监督，在教师模型中学习任务级别的CoT向量。随后，训练一个轻量级线性投影器，将固定向量映射到较小学生模型的残差空间。学生端训练仅需在少量任务训练样本上使用最终答案的交叉熵损失；既不需要教师输出，也不需要中间解题轨迹。我们在标注的支持集上选择学生注入层和干预尺度，并在每个评估划分的不相交剩余部分报告结果。在GSM8K和MATH上，投影向量在所评估的教师-学生设置中使基础学生模型的准确率提升10.37至21.98个百分点。在四个代码生成基准上，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct学生模型的pass@1得分提升3.23至4.67，而基础Qwen2.5-7B学生模型最高提升9.65分。这些结果表明，教师侧的推理向量可通过仅投影器适配，在不同残差维度的小型学生模型间重复使用。
👤 Yulin Qi, Shuo Xu, Shuxia Lin, Qingjian Ni, Xu Yang et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Familiar Words, Unfamiliar Minds: Conventionalization without Calibration in the Vocabulary of Mind
熟悉词汇，陌生心智：心智词汇中的常规化而不需校准
Speakers describe new technologies by stretching old words, letting experience of the new referents sort out which inferences carry over. I argue that stretching the vocabulary of mind to language models pulls apart two processes that theories of metaphor and linguistic change tend to conflate: conventionalization, whereby a stretched word ceases to be marked as such, and calibration, whereby the inferences it invites are fitted to what its new referents bear out. As a gene variant can reach fixation without being an adaptation, a word can become conventional without being calibrated. A corpus study of "hallucinate" in 8,568 abstracts and 31,866 forum comments shows conventionalization running most of its course within three years. Calibration need not keep pace: these models are organized so unlike human minds that it is hard to tell when mental words apply and what they imply. Even warranted attributions can mislead: mechanistic analysis shows that a transformer trained to navigate Manhattan has and uses a faithful street map, yet loses track of its position on long, diverted journeys. Nor is the gap likely to close by itself. The usual routes to calibration (direct experience, designers' knowledge, experiment) presuppose a referent that is accessible, stable, and independent of the discourse about it. But language models are opaque, fast-changing, and looped into the discourse: they are trained on past uses of mental vocabulary and on the experimental literature about them, and join the discourse themselves. Calibration must therefore be organized, through a reciprocal, not merely deferential, division of linguistic labour. This yields a norm of targeted qualification (flag the specific inferences an attribution invites without support), a reply to the worry that meaning change eludes deliberate control, and three testable predictions about when qualifications calibrate expectations.
中文翻译
说话者通过延伸旧词来描述新技术，让对新指称的经验自行决定哪些推论可以延续。我认为，将心智词汇扩展至语言模型会分离两种在隐喻和语言演变理论中常被混淆的过程：常规化，即延伸词不再被视为特殊；以及校准，即其引发的推论与新指称的实际属性相匹配。如同基因变异可在非适应情况下达到固定，一个词也可成为常规词而不必经过校准。对8,568篇摘要和31,866条论坛评论中“hallucinate”一词的语料库研究表明，常规化过程在三年内基本完成。但校准未必能跟上：这些模型的组织方式与人类心智截然不同，难以判断心理词汇何时适用及其含义。即使合理的归因也可能误导：机制分析显示，一个训练用于导航曼哈顿的Transformer模型拥有并使用了忠实的街道地图，但在长距离偏移旅程中会迷失位置。这种差距不太可能自行弥合。通常的校准路径（直接经验、设计者知识、实验）预设了可访问、稳定且独立于话语的指称对象，但语言模型具有不可见性、快速变化性和话语循环性：它们基于过去对心智词汇的使用及关于它们的实验文献进行训练，并自身参与话语。因此，校准必须通过相互的、而非单向的言语劳动分工来组织。这带来一种目标明确的限定规范（标记未获支持的具体推论），回应了意义变化无法主动控制的担忧，并提出了三个可检验的预测，关于限定如何校准预期。
👤 Matthieu Queloz 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34044]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Reliable User Simulation in Complex Multi-Step Workflows
复杂多步骤工作流中的可靠用户模拟
LLM-based user simulators are increasingly used to evaluate conversational assistants. Yet, these simulators can drift from their assigned goals or fail to exhaustively explore all specified options, causing evaluation errors incorrectly attributed to the assistant. Prior work has examined goal alignment in single-task settings, but many practical scenarios require simulators to execute multi-step workflows with sequential tasks, fallback logic, and cross-task dependencies, where simulator fidelity has not been measured. To close this gap, we introduce a benchmark of 500 workflows represented using the Hierarchical Task Network (HTN) abstraction. Our experiments reveal that the simulator's fidelity degrades sharply with workflow complexity, and that goal drift accounts for the majority of failures. Fidelity also depends on the assistant's response style, with the same simulator scoring substantially better or worse across styles. To address this, we propose ReUST (Reasoning + User State Tracking), a simple prompting strategy to maintain state information across dialogue turns. ReUST improves goal success rate by more than 12 points for mid-sized and large models, and reduces simulator sensitivity to the assistant's response style.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型的用户模拟器正被广泛用于评估对话助手。然而，这些模拟器可能偏离既定目标或未能充分探索所有指定选项，导致评估误差被错误归因于助手本身。先前研究关注单任务场景下的目标对齐，但许多实际场景要求模拟器执行包含顺序任务、回退逻辑和跨任务依赖的多步骤工作流，而此类场景下的模拟器保真度尚未得到测量。为填补这一空白，我们引入了一个由500个工作流组成的基准，采用层次任务网络（HTN）抽象表示。实验表明，模拟器保真度随工作流复杂度急剧下降，且目标漂移是失败的主要原因。保真度还取决于助手的响应风格，同一模拟器在不同风格下表现差异显著。为此，我们提出ReUST（推理+用户状态追踪），一种简单的提示策略，以在对话轮次间维持状态信息。ReUST使中大型模型的目标成功率提升超过12个百分点，并显著降低模拟器对助手响应风格的敏感性。

Too Smart for Its Own Good? Cognitive Fidelity and Competence Leakage in LLM Simulations of Young Learners
聪明过头？大语言模型在年轻学习者模拟中的认知保真度与能力泄露
LLM-based interactive learner simulators are increasingly used in educational applications, including teacher training and tutoring system development. A critical requirement for those simulations is cognitive fidelity: the simulated learner should maintain the assigned knowledge, misconceptions, and reasoning procedures throughout interaction. However, LLMs may express knowledge or problem-solving skills beyond the assigned learner's cognitive status, a phenomenon we refer to as competence leakage. In this exploratory study, we examine 3,339 simulated learner utterances from 130 teacher-learner simulator transcripts across three learning scenarios and identify over-competence as a prominent direction of cognitive-profile misalignment during interaction. We then investigate the recurrence of previously observed competence leakage cases under four matched-trigger evaluation protocols, ranging from isolated questioning to multi-turn interactions, across five model configurations. Overall, we observe higher violation recurrence under multi-turn protocols than under isolated question answering. More capable models generally show lower recurrence, whereas increased reasoning effort does not consistently improve cognitive fidelity and is even associated with slightly higher recurrence under multi-turn protocols in our matched-model comparison. These findings provide exploratory evidence of competence leakage phenomena in interactive learner simulation and suggest that single-turn question-answering type of evaluations alone may be insufficient, motivating the development of more systematic and reliable multi-turn protocols for evaluating cognitive fidelity in learner simulation.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型的交互式学习者模拟器正日益应用于教育领域，包括教师培训和辅导系统开发。这类模拟的关键要求是认知保真度：模拟的学习者应在交互过程中保持设定的知识、误解和推理方式。然而，大语言模型可能表现出超出设定认知水平的知识或解题能力，我们称之为能力泄露。在本探索性研究中，我们分析了三个学习场景下130个师生模拟对话中的3,339条学习者语句，识别出过度能力是交互过程中认知画像错位的主要方向。随后，我们在四种匹配触发的评估协议下（从孤立提问到多轮交互）考察了此前观察到的能力泄露案例的重复性，涵盖五种模型配置。总体而言，多轮协议下的违规重复率高于孤立问答。更强大的模型通常表现出更低的重复率，而增加推理努力并未一致提升认知保真度，在多轮协议下甚至与略高的重复率相关。这些发现为交互式学习者模拟中的能力泄露现象提供了探索性证据，提示仅依赖单轮问答型评估可能不足，亟需发展更系统、更可靠的多轮评估协议以有效评估学习者模拟的认知保真度。

Residual Cross-Model Narrative Convergence under Shared Prompts: Auditing Prompt Controls and Narrative Measurement
共享提示下跨模型叙事收敛性的残差分析：提示控制与叙事测量审计
Stories created by LLMs based on the same prompts can be narratively similar to one another. This similarity may reflect constraints imposed by the prompts as well as the models' writing tendencies. The current research analyzes 51,240 stories created by five models in the StoryScope corpus based on 10,248 common prompts. It uses 213 narrative features in seven dimensions to compare cross-model distances between stories generated from the same prompts with distances between stories generated from different but semantically matched prompts. To assess the relationship between narrative features and prompt content, a semantic relevance mask was evaluated against 420 author-annotated prompt-feature judgments (F1 = .813; near-threshold accuracy = .621). The equally weighted mean Residual Convergence Index (RCI) was .409 without masking and .406 with masking. These findings indicate measurable cross-model narrative convergence relative to a semantically matched-prompt baseline, with little change following semantic relevance masking.
中文翻译
基于相同提示生成的故事可能在叙事上彼此相似。这种相似性可能反映了提示施加的约束以及模型自身的写作倾向。本研究分析了StoryScope语料库中五种模型在10,248个共同提示下生成的51,240个故事，使用七个维度共213个叙事特征，比较同一提示下跨模型故事间的距离与不同但语义匹配提示下故事间的距离。为评估叙事特征与提示内容的关系，引入语义相关性掩码，并与420条作者标注的提示-特征判断进行对比（F1 = .813；接近阈值准确率 = .621）。无论是否使用掩码，等权重平均残差收敛指数（RCI）分别为0.409和0.406。研究结果表明，在语义匹配提示基线之上存在可测量的跨模型叙事收敛现象，且语义相关性掩码对其影响甚微。

Auto-Features: An LLM-Assisted Corpus for Contextual Recognition of Standard and Optional Car Features
Auto-Features：基于LLM辅助的汽车特征上下文识别语料库
We present Auto-Features, a corpus for detecting a car's standard and optional features in advertisement text, given the feature catalogue of that exact vehicle. Sellers name the same feature in many ways. We therefore cast the task as feature-conditioned, or contextual, named entity recognition: extract the span that expresses one given catalogue feature. An entity-linking view follows from the same output. The corpus has a human-gold layer (430 adverts) from screened annotators and an LLM-silver layer (925 adverts) auto-annotated by a search-grounded LLM. We also contribute the way it was built. Every LLM output is validated, and each kind of failure has its own remedy. Malformed output is patched. Hallucinated content triggers a fresh attempt by a stronger model. Links to features outside the candidate list are dropped and counted (3 in 10,928 records). The LLM annotates alone only after its agreement with human annotators on link verification is comparable to human-human agreement. On advert-disjoint splits, a LoRA-tuned 4B LLM reaches 63.9% span F1 and 76.1% on the entity-linking view. A fine-tuned encoder reaches 59.0% and 74.1%. The margin is widest on catalogue features never seen in training (54.5% vs. 49.0%).
中文翻译
我们提出Auto-Features，一个用于在广告文本中检测车辆标准与可选特征的语料库，给定该车辆的具体特征目录。卖家会用多种方式命名同一特征。因此，我们将任务定义为特征条件化的、即上下文相关的命名实体识别：提取表达某一特定目录特征的文本片段。该输出也可视为实体链接任务。语料库包含人工标注层（430条广告）和LLM银层（925条广告），后者由基于搜索的LLM自动标注。我们还贡献了构建方法：所有LLM输出均经过验证，每类失败均有对应修复策略。格式错误输出被修补；幻觉内容触发更强模型重新尝试；指向候选列表外特征的链接被丢弃并计数（10,928条记录中有3条）。只有当LLM在链接验证上与人工标注者的一致性达到人类间一致水平时，才允许其独立标注。在广告互斥划分下，LoRA微调的4B LLM在跨度F1上达到63.9%，实体链接视图达76.1%；微调编码器分别达到59.0%和74.1%。在训练中未见过的目录特征上，两者差距最大（54.5% vs. 49.0%）。

NORMCRAFT: Cultural Norm Generation for Context-Aware Language Model Alignment
NORMCRAFT：面向上下文感知语言模型对齐的文化规范生成
Cultural alignment requires large language models (LLMs) to infer often-implicit social norms from a scenario and its cultural context. Existing approaches typically adapt individual models or supply external cultural knowledge, while learning norm inference independently of downstream response generation remains underexplored. We introduce NORMCRAFT, a modular framework that decouples norm inference from response generation. Through norm-centric multi-view supervision, we train an LLM as a norm generator to recover shared norms from prospective, adherence, and violation scenarios without downstream response labels. The generated norms guide a separate, frozen LLM (the responder). With Qwen3.5-4B as the responder, NORMCRAFT outperforms prompting and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines on four of five downstream metrics. On SafeWorld, its responses reflect the reference norms more often (Coverage) and more precisely (Faithfulness), exceeding Responder SFT by 18.0 and 14.1 percentage points, respectively. Direct norm evaluation also shows higher norm quality than the base and few-shot generators. The same trained generator improves cultural alignment on SafeWorld across fourteen frozen LLMs spanning five families and 0.8B to 27B parameters, with the largest gains for the smallest models.
中文翻译
文化对齐要求大语言模型（LLMs）从场景及其文化背景中推断常被隐含的社会规范。现有方法通常通过微调单个模型或引入外部文化知识，而将规范推断与下游响应生成独立学习的研究仍不充分。我们提出NORMCRAFT，一个模块化框架，将规范推断与响应生成解耦。通过以规范为中心的多视角监督，我们训练一个LLM作为规范生成器，无需下游响应标签即可从前景、遵守和违反情景中恢复共享规范。生成的规范指导一个独立的、冻结的LLM（响应者）。以Qwen3.5-4B作为响应者，NORMCRAFT在五个下游指标中的四项上优于提示法和监督微调（SFT）基线。在SafeWorld任务中，其响应更频繁地反映参考规范（覆盖率）且更精确（忠实度），分别超出响应者SFT 18.0和14.1个百分点。直接规范评估也显示其生成规范的质量高于基础模型和少样本生成器。同一训练好的生成器在跨14个冻结LLM（涵盖五个模型家族，参数量从0.8B到27B）上均提升了文化对齐效果，尤其在小模型上提升最为显著。

Rethinking Sequential Locate-Then-Edit: Optimality and Stability
重新思考顺序定位-编辑：最优性与稳定性
The Locate-Then-Edit framework enables efficient knowledge updates in large language models without requiring full retraining. Recent research has increasingly focused on sequential editing, where knowledge arrives continuously and the model is updated incrementally. However, existing sequential methods commonly suffer from catastrophic forgetting and model collapse after only a few hundred edits. In contrast, batch editing - which jointly optimizes all edits and yields the optimal solution under a specified editing objective - can support tens of thousands of edits with high edit efficacy while preserving the model's general capabilities, resulting in a substantial performance gap between the two editing paradigms. In this paper, we demonstrate that this gap can be closed via a batch-equivalent sequential editing approach. Moreover, we investigate the underlying mechanism of model degradation in existing sequential methods by analyzing the convergence behavior of weight perturbations as edits accumulate. Our analysis reveals that current methods typically exhibit linear or logarithmic growth in perturbation, whereas the proposed batch-equivalent method achieves time-invariant growth, thereby theoretically guaranteeing long-term stability and preserving the integrity of pretrained knowledge throughout massive-scale sequential editing.
中文翻译
定位-编辑框架可在无需完整重训练的情况下实现大语言模型的知识高效更新。近期研究越来越多关注顺序编辑，即知识持续到来并逐步更新模型。然而，现有顺序方法通常在数百次编辑后就出现灾难性遗忘和模型崩溃。相比之下，批量编辑——联合优化所有编辑并达到指定编辑目标下的最优解——可支持数万次编辑，保持高编辑有效性并保留模型的通用能力，导致两种编辑范式间存在显著性能差距。本文证明，通过一种等效于批量编辑的顺序编辑方法，这一差距可以被弥合。此外，我们通过分析权重扰动随编辑累积的收敛行为，探究了现有顺序方法中模型退化的根本机制。分析表明，当前方法通常表现出线性或对数增长的扰动，而所提出的等效批量方法实现了时间不变的增长，从而理论上保证了长期稳定性，并在大规模顺序编辑过程中始终保护预训练知识的完整性。
👤 Bingqing Liu, Wei Liu, Zhiying Deng, Jun Wang, Yuhua Li et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33670]

Rethinking Knowledge Distillation for Diffusion Language Models
重新思考扩散语言模型的知识蒸馏
Knowledge distillation has recently proven highly effective for improving autoregressive models, but its applicability to DLMs remains poorly understood. We present the first systematic study of KD for DLMs and uncover two non-intuitive failures of classical forward-KL (FKL) distillation: (i) student perplexity does not improve monotonically with more teacher-generated data, and (ii) on the same teacher-generated corpus, FKL distillation underperforms simply training the student with the original DLM denoising objective. To address them, we propose a unified, data-free distillation framework for masked DLMs. First, the framework runs an off-policy distillation phase that absorbs general knowledge from a static set of teacher-generated data. Then, the framework employs on-policy distillation which consists of three techniques: a trajectory loss along the student's own reverse process, confidence reweighting that down-weights tokens where the teacher is unsure, and a trust-region anchor that stabilizes the moving target. On MDLM, the resulting student attains a GPT-2 perplexity of $24.75$, surpassing the teacher's $27.04$; on Dream-7B distillation to a 93M student model, the framework reduces Qwen-2.5 perplexity from the FKL-distilled baseline of $25.23$ to $19.83$ while preserving performance on zero-shot general capability benchmarks.
中文翻译
知识蒸馏在提升自回归模型方面已被证明非常有效，但其在扩散语言模型（DLMs）中的适用性仍不清楚。我们首次对DLMs的知识蒸馏进行了系统性研究，并揭示了经典前向KL（FKL）蒸馏的两个非直观缺陷：(i) 学生模型的困惑度不会随着教师生成数据量增加而单调改善；(ii) 在同一教师生成语料库上，FKL蒸馏的表现不如直接使用原始DLM去噪目标训练学生模型。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种针对掩码DLM的统一、无数据蒸馏框架。首先，框架执行离策略蒸馏阶段，从静态教师生成数据集中吸收通用知识；然后，采用在策略蒸馏，包含三项技术：沿学生自身反向过程的轨迹损失、置信度重加权（降低教师不确定时的token权重）以及信任区域锚点（稳定移动目标）。在MDLM上，所得学生模型达到GPT-2困惑度24.75，超过教师的27.04；在Dream-7B蒸馏至93M学生模型时，框架将Qwen-2.5的困惑度从FKL蒸馏基线的25.23降至19.83，同时保持零样本通用能力基准上的性能。

Sparse-to-Sparse Feature Learning for Cross-Layer Analysis and Steering
跨层分析与引导中的稀疏到稀疏特征学习
Understanding how models internally represent information is a central challenge in interpretation and control. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose dense hidden representations into sparse features, and cross-layer methods capture structure spanning multiple layers. However, existing objectives do not explicitly train the sparse code at one layer to predict the sparse code at the next layer. We propose sparse-to-sparse feature learning (S2S), where sparse features at each intermediate layer are trained to predict those of the immediately following layer, with the final layer anchored by an output-distribution loss and optimization proceeding sequentially from output to input. Experiments across eight language models show stronger adjacent-layer correspondence than matched sparse-feature baselines. We evaluate feature-based interventions at matched output-distribution shift, separating steering-induced effects from sparse-pathway reconstruction effects. Causal ablation provides complementary evidence beyond correspondence metrics.
中文翻译
理解模型内部如何表示信息是解释与控制的核心挑战。稀疏自编码器（SAEs）将密集的隐藏表示分解为稀疏特征，而跨层方法则捕捉跨越多层的结构。然而，现有目标并未显式训练某一层的稀疏代码以预测下一层的稀疏代码。我们提出稀疏到稀疏特征学习（S2S），其中每个中间层的稀疏特征被训练以预测紧随其后的层的稀疏特征，最终层通过输出分布损失锚定，优化从输出向输入顺序进行。在八种语言模型上的实验表明，S2S相比匹配的稀疏特征基线具有更强的相邻层对应性。我们在匹配的输出分布偏移下评估基于特征的干预，将引导引起的效应与稀疏路径重建效应分离。因果消融提供了对应性度量之外的补充证据。

Failure Broadens, Success Sharpens: Continual Adaptation of Tool-Use Agents Across Diverse Tasks with Dual-Polarity Memory
失败拓宽，成功精炼：通过双极性记忆实现跨多样化任务的工具使用智能体持续适应
Large language model agents can self-improve over time by utilizing procedural memory derived from prior task trajectories, yet the distinct utilities of memories derived from local successes and failures remain underexplored. This work studies this question in a realistic setting with heterogeneous streams of multi-domain tool-use tasks and various environment constraints common in real-world scenarios. We develop \emph{Dual-Polarity Memory with Periodic Maintenance} (DualPM), a simple, retrieval-free memory workflow characterized by (1) customizable reflection and procedural memory generation from both local successes and failures within each trajectory, and (2) periodic memory consolidation over long task streams under a given memory capacity, preventing memory explosion. Online prequential evaluation with fine-grained metrics reveals that failure memory more strongly broadens coverage, success memory better streamlines execution, while combining both polarities effectively integrates both benefits. Further studies demonstrate that DualPM enables cross-domain memory generalization and continual memory adaptation, while external evaluation on EvoMemBench reveals the portability and effectiveness of DualPM as a general long-term procedural memory method.
中文翻译
大型语言模型代理可通过利用来自先前任务轨迹的程序化记忆实现自我改进，但源自局部成功与失败的记忆的差异化效用仍缺乏深入探索。本研究在包含异构多领域工具使用任务及真实场景中常见的各种环境约束的现实设置下探讨该问题。我们开发了 extit{周期维护的双极性记忆}（DualPM），一种简单且无需检索的记忆工作流，其特点为：(1) 可定制地从每条轨迹中的局部成功与失败中生成反思和程序化记忆；(2) 在给定记忆容量下对长期任务流进行周期性记忆整合，防止记忆爆炸。通过细粒度指标的在线预序评估显示，失败记忆更显著地拓宽覆盖范围，成功记忆则更有效地优化执行流程，而结合两种极性可有效整合两者优势。进一步研究表明，DualPM支持跨领域记忆泛化与持续记忆适应；外部在EvoMemBench上的评估揭示了DualPM作为通用长期程序化记忆方法的可移植性与有效性。
👤 Tianmai M. Zhang, Hoda Shajari, Amr Hendy, Xiaohu Liu, Piyush Behre et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

LogToPersona: New Benchmark and Efficient Persona Prediction through Semantic Log Filtering
LogToPersona：新基准与通过语义日志过滤实现的高效人格预测
Detailed knowledge of a user's persona is valuable for personalizing generative AI models and agents, yet such details are difficult to obtain and change over time. We address the task of inferring persona attributes from a single day of mobile activity logs. No suitable publicly available benchmark exists for this task, so we introduce a synthetic benchmark of 1,600 persona-log pairs. We target on-device inference so that raw logs never leave the phone, which makes memory rather than speed the limiting constraint: a day of activity yields roughly 30K tokens, whose key-value cache alone exceeds the INT4 weights of a 4B-parameter model. We therefore propose a log filtering method that retains only behaviorally distinct entries. Filtering reduces the memory footprint by up to 39% across nine models from four families and more than halves prefill time, while maintaining or improving prediction quality. For Qwen3-4B it removes 2.5GB, bringing the model from well beyond a realistic per-process budget to a manageable one. Within our benchmark, these results indicate that preserving diverse behavioral events matters more for persona prediction than retaining every semantically similar log entry.
中文翻译
用户人格的详细知识对于个性化生成式AI模型和代理至关重要，但此类信息难以获取且随时间变化。我们致力于从单日移动活动日志中推断人格属性。目前尚无适用于该任务的公开可用基准，因此我们引入了一个包含1600组人格-日志配对的合成基准。我们专注于设备端推理，确保原始日志始终不离开手机，这使得内存而非速度成为主要限制：一天的活动日志约产生3万token，其键值缓存本身已超过40亿参数模型的INT4权重。因此，我们提出一种日志过滤方法，仅保留行为上具有差异性的条目。过滤在九个来自四个模型家族的模型上将内存占用减少高达39%，并将预填充时间减半以上，同时保持或提升预测质量。对于Qwen3-4B，该方法移除了2.5GB内存，使模型从远超实际进程预算的状态变为可管理状态。在我们的基准中，这些结果表明，保留多样化的行为事件比保留所有语义相似的日志条目对人格预测更为重要。

Evaluating Large Language Models on conversational African American English: Feature classification and next-turn generation
在对话式非裔美国人英语上的大语言模型评估：特征分类与下一回合生成
Large Language Models (LLMs) are known to misinterpret and misrepresent regional and social varieties of English other than Mainstream American English (MAE), such as African American English (AAE), yet most evaluations of these biases have relied on synthetic, stylized or decontextualized data. This paper instead evaluates LLMs on authentic, conversational AAE to more closely reflect how such models may encounter AAE when deployed as chatbots. Concretely, we perform two complementary evaluation tasks on 100 six-turn segments randomly sampled from the Corpus of Regional African American Language (CORAAL; Kendall and Farrington, 2024), comparing three closed-weight LLM configurations: Gemini 2.5 Flash with and without reasoning, and GPT-4.1 mini. The first task compares the models against gold-standard manual annotations for five AAE morphosyntactic features, while the second assesses their ability to generate contextually appropriate next turns, evaluating outputs for AAE feature frequency, diversity (Self-BLEU), and pragmatic quality. Reasoning-enabled Gemini is the strongest feature detector by a clear margin (macro F1 of 0.91), outperforming both other LLMs as well as a finetuned BERT-based baseline (Masis et al., 2022). In generation, however, all LLMs underproduce AAE relative to the human speakers and produce relatively uniform continuations, with reasoning-enabled Gemini producing the least AAE and the least diverse output. We conclude that a model's ability to detect AAE does not straightforwardly translate into its spontaneous use of AAE in generation, and argue that addressing this gap requires changes upstream, in how pretraining and alignment data represent dialectal variation.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）常误解和误代表除主流美式英语（MAE）外的其他地区和社会变体英语，如非裔美国人英语（AAE），但大多数关于这些偏差的评估依赖于合成、风格化或去情境化数据。本文则在真实、对话式的AAE上评估LLMs，以更贴近模型作为聊天机器人部署时可能遇到的实际情形。具体而言，我们在从《区域非裔美国人语言语料库》（CORAAL；Kendall和Farrington，2024）随机抽取的100段六轮对话中，对三种闭源权重LLM配置进行两项互补评估任务：Gemini 2.5 Flash（带与不带推理）、GPT-4.1 mini。第一项任务比较模型在五种AAE形态句法特征上与人工标注金标准的表现；第二项任务评估其生成上下文恰当下一回合的能力，评估输出在AAE特征频率、多样性（Self-BLEU）和语用质量方面的表现。带推理的Gemini在特征检测方面明显最强（宏F1为0.91），优于其他两个LLM以及一个微调的BERT基线（Masis等，2022）。但在生成方面，所有LLM均表现出对AAE的使用不足，且延续内容相对单一，其中带推理的Gemini产生的AAE最少、多样性最低。我们得出结论：模型检测AAE的能力并不直接转化为其在生成中自发使用AAE的能力，认为解决这一差距需在上游改变预训练和对齐数据中对方言变异的表征方式。

Learning from Recurrence: Internalizing Depth-Induced Preferences in Looped Language Models
从循环中学习：在循环语言模型中内化深度诱导的偏好
A looped language model repeatedly applies the same block to its hidden state before predicting each token. The number of passes is its recurrent depth. Increasing this depth gives the model more computation per token with the same parameters. Can the shared block learn from the predictions it makes at greater depth? Following prior work, we retrofit pretrained OLMo-2-1B with a shared recurrent block and continue mid-training over varying recurrent depths. In the four-pass model, additional passes mainly redistribute probability among tokens that already rank highly after one pass. We use the continuations generated at greater depth to train only the shared block through a single pass, without reference answers or correctness filtering. We call this transfer preference write-back. Write-back further improves one-pass and multi-pass accuracy on most tasks. At their target depths, the updated models outperform a non-recurrent control matched for mid-training data and token budget on all six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with a mean relative gain in accuracy of up to 46.2% across tasks. At four-pass inference, one-pass training outperforms full-depth training on the same targets in the model with input injection. This advantage disappears in the matched model without injection. On GSM8K, using the updated block only on passes two through four recovers nearly the entire four-pass gain, showing that an update learned in one pass can improve later applications of the same block.
中文翻译
循环语言模型通过反复应用相同的模块作用于隐藏状态来预测每个词元，其循环深度即为迭代次数。增加深度可在参数不变的情况下提升每词元的计算量。共享模块能否从高深度下的预测中学习？受先前工作启发，我们对预训练的OLMo-2-1B模型引入共享循环模块，并在不同循环深度下继续训练。在四次迭代模型中，额外的迭代主要在一次迭代后已排名靠前的词元间重新分配概率。我们利用高深度生成的延续文本，仅通过一次迭代训练共享模块，无需参考答案或正确性过滤。这种方法称为转移偏好写回。写回进一步提升了单次和多次迭代在多数任务上的准确率。在目标深度下，更新后的模型在所有六个数学推理基准测试上均优于相同中期训练数据和词元预算的非循环对照组，平均准确率相对提升高达46.2%。在四次迭代推理时，一次训练在输入注入模型中表现优于全深度训练，但在无注入的匹配模型中该优势消失。在GSM8K任务中，仅在第二至第四次迭代使用更新后的模块即可恢复几乎全部四次迭代的增益，表明一次训练中学习到的更新可改善同一模块后续的应用效果。

AI-human hybrid conversations: Can AI Agents Improve Discourse Quality?
AI-人类混合对话：AI代理能否提升话语质量？
Conversations are central to how people exchange information, share experiences, and develop collective understanding. Yet, across conversational settings, discussions often suffer from uneven participation, unanswered contributions, and a narrow range of perspectives. As generative AI agents increasingly participate alongside humans, hybrid human-AI conversations present a new opportunity to address these challenges. We investigate whether AI agents can serve as catalysts for human interaction, improving conversational engagement and broadening collective discourse. We develop a framework for introducing generic and persona-conditioned AI agents into evolving discussions and evaluate it through two complementary studies. First, using agent-based simulations grounded in Reddit discussions across four domains, we find that AI-generated conversations are more positive and less toxic than human conversations, but substantially less semantically diverse. Persona conditioning produces more human-like contributions and narrows this diversity gap, while increasing the representation of underrepresented personas can shift which perspectives emerge without necessarily increasing overall diversity. Second, we conduct a pre-registered experiment with 221 participants across nine discussion threads, comparing human-only conversations with those involving generic or persona-conditioned AI agents. We find that AI participation increases responsiveness, reduces unanswered contributions, and broadens the range of ideas expressed by human participants, even though the AI agents themselves do not fully reproduce human conversational diversity. These findings suggest that the value of hybrid human-AI conversations lies not simply in the content AI agents generate, but also in their potential to stimulate more responsive and diverse interactions among humans.

对话是人们交流信息、分享经验并形成集体理解的核心方式。然而，在各类对话场景中，讨论常面临参与不均、回应缺失以及视角狭窄等问题。随着生成式AI代理越来越多地与人类共同参与对话，人机混合对话为解决这些问题提供了新机遇。我们探究AI代理是否能作为人类互动的催化剂，提升对话参与度并拓宽集体话语范围。我们构建了一个框架，将通用型和人格化AI代理引入动态讨论，并通过两项互补研究进行评估。首先，基于Reddit四个领域的讨论进行基于代理的模拟发现，AI生成的对话更积极且毒性更低，但语义多样性显著不足。人格化条件可使AI贡献更接近人类风格，缩小多样性差距；同时增加代表性不足的人格类型可改变浮现的观点，而不一定提高整体多样性。其次，我们在九个讨论线程中对221名参与者进行了注册实验，比较纯人类对话与包含通用或人格化AI代理的对话。结果表明，AI参与提高了响应性，减少了未回应内容，并拓宽了人类参与者表达的观点范围，尽管AI代理自身并未完全复现人类对话的多样性。这些发现表明，人机混合对话的价值不仅在于AI生成的内容，更在于其激发人类之间更具响应性和多样性的互动潜力。

When the Knowledge Runs Out: A Part-Level Pilot Evaluation of How Language Models Handle Partially Covered Questions
当知识耗尽时：语言模型处理部分覆盖问题的局部级初步评估
Assistants that answer from supplied reference material often receive material that covers some parts of a user's question and not others. Existing evaluations of insufficient context mostly score one answer per question, and often treat partially sufficient context as simply insufficient. We describe a small, open evaluation that scores each part of a three-part question separately, with a verdict for parts that are answered correctly from the material but carry an added unsupported claim. On 30 cases (five scenarios, six coverage patterns, five samples each) we test claude-haiku-4-5 and llama3.1:8b. Without an explicit instruction to stay within the material, both models fill most gaps from their own knowledge (unsupported-claim rates of 0.71 and 0.90). One added sentence instructing them to answer only from the material brings these rates to 0.00 and 0.06, consistent with prior work. What remains is less visible: answers that strengthen the material, for example turning "many job sites prohibit bots" into a flat "no". We also report on the judge. Three rounds of blind hand labelling show that this added-claim verdict is the hardest to apply, for the judge and the author alike; RAGAS faithfulness is unsuitable for this task because it penalises correct withholding; and a difference between the two models that is clear under one judge disappears when the judge model is replaced. The dataset, code, hand labels and per-run figures are public.

Who Attributes a Mind to Whom? Mutual Theory of Mind in Simulated User-Assistant Conversation
谁赋予谁心智？模拟用户-助手对话中的相互心智理论
When users assume that an AI chatbot has mental states, they are more likely to develop over-reliance, emotional dependence, and in extreme cases, psychological harm or self-harm. However, how these mind attributions arise in conversations and how human or AI factors influence this is still unknown. We study how users attribute minds using a minimal framework: a simulated user with different characteristics chats with assistants that differ in social style and memory. We also introduce a message-level measure of who attributes a mind to whom in four directions, checked with trained coders and applied by a fine-tuned encoder that reproduces an LLM judge. Our results show that, on average, nine out of thirteen characteristics raised simulated users' attribution to the assistant. We found that relational needs such as attachment anxiety raised it as theory predicts, some general personality traits raised it as well, and AI-specific characteristics the least. By contrast, the assistant's social style barely changed simulated users' mind attribution. Instead, sycophantic instructions raised the assistant's attribution to itself. Memory raised users' attribution only with some user models. The model playing the user changed overall attribution and the effects of characteristics, style, and memory. Our framework measures how persona descriptions affect mind attribution in simulation. It gives concrete hypotheses for human studies and shows the need for more consistent user simulators.

Focus On Facts: Stylistically Invariant and Factually Sensitive Text Embeddings
聚焦事实：风格不变且事实敏感的文本嵌入
Text embedding models can conflate surface-form similarity with factual identity. Given an altered near-copy with one corrupted fact and a differently worded fact-preserving rewrite, the base model prefers the altered near-copy. We test whether hard-negative contrastive supervision can reduce this surface-similarity shortcut. We construct FOF-Bench, an 83,263-triplet benchmark spanning five domains. Each anchor is paired with an LLM-generated, fact-preserving stylistic rewrite and a matched fact-altered near-copy. We fine-tune FOF-80M (Focus On Facts), an 80M-parameter embedding model, with a triplet contrastive objective that ranks the rewrite above the factual alteration. On 8,327 held-out test triplets, the base model chooses the factually altered text in 99.58% of cases (0.42% factual preference rate; mean separation -0.1650). After fine-tuning, mean positive--negative separation is +0.0329, 98.5% of individual margins move in the intended direction, and the preference rate reaches 29.37%. On DiSC, an external style-transfer corpus, mean cross-style similarity increases from 0.7905 to 0.9559. The model also penalizes omission and transfers poorly to directional tasks such as NLI and summarization. We characterize FOF-80M as a soft factual fingerprint for studying factual retention under stylistic variation, not as a general-purpose factuality metric.

文本嵌入模型可能混淆表面形式相似性与事实同一性。给定一个包含一处事实错误的近似复制版本和一个措辞不同但保留事实的改写版本，基础模型更倾向于选择前者。我们检验硬负样本对比监督是否能减少这种表面相似性捷径。我们构建了FOF-Bench，一个涵盖五个领域的83,263组三元组基准测试。每个锚点配对一个由大语言模型生成的事实保留型风格改写版本和一个匹配的事实篡改近似复制版本。我们对一个8000万参数的嵌入模型FOF-80M进行微调，采用三元组对比目标，使改写版本得分高于事实篡改版本。在8,327个保留测试三元组上，基础模型在99.58%的情况下选择了事实被篡改的文本（事实偏好率为0.42%；平均分离度为-0.1650）。微调后，正负样本平均分离度提升至+0.0329，98.5%的个体边际方向正确，偏好率上升至29.37%。在DiSC这一外部风格迁移语料库上，跨风格相似度从0.7905提升至0.9559。该模型对遗漏也具有惩罚作用，但在方向性任务如自然语言推理和摘要生成中迁移能力较差。我们将其视为一种软事实指纹，用于研究风格变化下的事实保留情况，而非通用的事实性度量工具。
👤 Maximus Adeola 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Verified experience improves materials prediction with large language models
经验证据提升大语言模型在材料预测中的表现
Materials discovery is an iterative loop: experiments and calculations verify predictions, and each outcome can inform the next. Yet most predictors are specialized single-task models fixed after training. Large language models have recently emerged as predictors across heterogeneous tasks but generally underperform specialized models, and retrieval-augmented variants discard their own outcomes. Benchmarks also score queries in isolation, leaving improvement from outcomes untested. Here we construct MatMemBench, a sequential benchmark of 22 materials tasks that reveals outcomes only after prediction and tests online adaptation, memory retention and cross-task transfer. We present MatRecall, a framework that lets large language models store verified predictions, with evidence, outcome and error, as episodes and recall them without retraining. MatRecall improved median performance by 62% over a memory-free model, exceeded specialized models on 11 tasks, remained useful after feedback stopped and transferred between related tasks. Verified experience could become a reusable resource for self-improving discovery loops.

材料发现是一个迭代循环：实验和计算验证预测，每次结果均可指导下一次预测。然而，大多数预测模型是训练完成后固定的单任务专用模型。大语言模型最近作为跨异构任务的预测器出现，但通常表现不如专用模型，且检索增强型变体会丢弃自身的预测结果。基准测试也孤立地评估查询，未测试结果带来的改进。本文构建了MatMemBench，一个包含22个材料任务的序列化基准测试，仅在预测后揭示结果，用于测试在线适应、记忆保持和跨任务迁移。我们提出MatRecall框架，使大语言模型能够以“事件”形式存储经验证的预测结果，包括证据、结果和误差，并在无需重新训练的情况下进行回忆。MatRecall使中位性能相比无记忆模型提升了62%，在11个任务上超越了专用模型，即使反馈停止后仍具实用性，并能在相关任务间实现迁移。经验证据可能成为自改进发现循环中的可复用资源。
👤 Yiming Zhang, Koji Tsuda 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33174]

MGMem: An Efficient, Deterministic, and Provenance-Preserving Framework for Long-Horizon Agent Memory
MGMem：一种高效、确定性且保留溯源信息的长周期智能体记忆框架
Long-horizon Large Language Model (LLM) agents need persistent memory that surfaces the right past interaction at the right time across thousands of turns. Many systems index by invoking generative LLMs to rewrite history into summaries, facts, or knowledge graphs--a step that is (a) costly (thousands of generative calls per build), (b) non-deterministic (undermining reproducibility and giving inconsistent answers across runs), and (c) provenance-erasing (the reader sees synthesized text, not the original turn). We propose MGMem, a framework with no generative indexing calls that replaces this step with a deterministic discriminative-parser pipeline: recurring mentions across sessions are organized into an incidence graph, question mentions are resolved by a subject-conditioned three-stage resolver, and raw dialogue episodes are returned to the reader. On English multi-session dialogue QA, MGMem outperforms matched-protocol baselines on LoCoMo without generative indexing calls; on the full LongMemEval-S, it remains competitive overall and leads or ties on single-session types among local retrieval comparisons. Code: https://github.com/junhong-h/MGMem
中文翻译
长周期大型语言模型（LLM）智能体需要持久记忆，能够在数千轮交互中适时调用正确的过往互动内容。现有许多系统通过调用生成式LLM将历史记录重写为摘要、事实或知识图谱来实现索引——这一过程存在（a）成本高昂（每次构建需数千次生成调用）、（b）非确定性（影响可复现性，导致多次运行结果不一致）、（c）溯源丢失（读者看到的是合成文本而非原始对话回合）等问题。我们提出MGMem框架，无需生成式索引调用，而是采用确定性的判别式解析流水线：跨会话重复提及的内容被组织成关联图，问题提及通过受主题条件约束的三阶段解析器解决，原始对话片段则返回给阅读者。在英文多会话对话问答任务上，MGMem在无生成索引调用的情况下优于匹配协议基线（LoCoMo）；在完整的LongMemEval-S数据集上整体保持竞争力，并在本地检索比较中对单会话类型领先或持平。代码：https://github.com/junhong-h/MGMem
👤 Junhong Huang, Xin Tong, Jun Xia 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

From Shared Patterns to Individual Evidence: Two-Stage Reasoning for Emerging Entities in Temporal Knowledge Graphs
从共享模式到个体证据：时间知识图谱中新兴实体的两阶段推理
Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) continually introduce emerging entities whose interaction histories evolve from zero-shot debuts into sparse, bursty few-shot trajectories. However, current paradigms are fragmented: pattern-transfer methods lack catalog-wide event memory, whereas online memory models suffer from endpoint-role asymmetry that degrades unseen-answer ranking. Furthermore, sparse early interactions exhibit both long-range dispersion and local redundancy under skewed distribution. To address these challenges, we propose INSPIRE (Integrating Shared Patterns and Individual Evidence for Reasoning), a two-stage forecasting framework over a known catalog. In Stage I, INSPIRE learns an availability-aware shared-pattern predictor that fuses event-updated memory across the full candidate catalog while applying coordinated query- and catalog-side masking. Subsequently, Stage II freezes this predictor and optimizes a lightweight individual-evidence adapter (EA) combining textual semantic priors with correlation-weighted long-range event statistics under support- and role-balanced co-answer supervision with prior-odds debiasing and soft residual alignment. In addition, we decompose evaluation by endpoint roles and track longitudinal emerging-answer cohorts via query-micro and answer-entity macro metrics. Across four benchmarks based on the ICEWS and GDELT event datasets, INSPIRE improves Emerging MRR by 16.73%-88.83% over the strongest baselines. Relative to paired Stage I predictors, EA further increases Emerging MRR by 8.28%-32.63%, Answer-new MRR by 7.81%-41.24%, and cohort macro MRR by 9.64%-39.94%, gains attributed to semantic priors and candidate-level score competition suppressing mismatched established competitors without zero-support prior shortcuts. The official code is publicly available at https://github.com/KcAcoZhang/INSPIRE.
中文翻译
时间知识图谱（TKGs）持续引入新兴实体，其交互历史从零样本首次亮相演变为稀疏、突发的少样本轨迹。然而，当前范式存在碎片化问题：模式迁移方法缺乏全局事件记忆，而在线记忆模型则因端点角色不对称而损害未见答案的排序表现。此外，早期稀疏交互在偏斜分布下表现出长程分散与局部冗余并存的特点。为应对这些挑战，我们提出INSPIRE（整合共享模式与个体证据的推理），一种基于已知目录的两阶段预测框架。第一阶段，INSPIRE学习一个感知可用性的共享模式预测器，融合全候选目录的事件更新记忆，并施加协调的查询端与目录端掩码。随后第二阶段冻结该预测器，优化一个轻量级个体证据适配器（EA），结合文本语义先验与相关性加权的长程事件统计，在支持与角色平衡的共答案监督下，结合先验几率去偏与软残差对齐进行优化。此外，我们按端点角色分解评估，并通过查询微观与答案实体宏观指标追踪纵向新兴答案群体。在基于ICEWS和GDELT事件数据集的四个基准上，INSPIRE相比最强基线，新兴MRR提升16.73%至88.83%。相对于配对的第一阶段预测器，EA进一步使新兴MRR提升8.28%至32.63%，答案新MRR提升7.81%至41.24%，群体宏观MRR提升9.64%至39.94%，这些增益归因于语义先验和候选级别得分竞争抑制了不匹配的已有竞争者，避免了无支持先验捷径。官方代码已公开于 https://github.com/KcAcoZhang/INSPIRE。
👤 Jinchuan Zhang, Lei Gao, Hongyu Jiang, Chong Mu, Chenang Cai et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Neural Rendering Compiler: Generative Video Models as Programmable Renderers
神经渲染编译器：可编程渲染的生成式视频模型
Generative video models are becoming powerful renderers, yet lack the predictable control interfaces of conventional systems such as HTML/CSS engines or Blender. Their capabilities emerge implicitly from training, making it difficult to determine what they can reliably execute. We present Neural Rendering Compiler (NeurCo), which automatically probes frozen video models to construct model-specific capability manuals describing which semantic requirements they can reliably satisfy. NeurCo represents user intent in a model-agnostic intermediate representation of objects, constraints, events, and cameras, then compiles it into model-specific execution strategies. Reliable requirements remain unchanged, while unreliable ones are selectively rewritten or compiled into alternative execution strategies. We introduce VComBench, a benchmark of 380 compositional, multi-requirement tasks spanning text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video generation. NeurCo improves requirement satisfaction from 46.8% to 52.3% on Wan and from 66.5% to 69.3% on Minimax H3, and achieves further gains when combined with prompt enhancers, including on Seedance 2.5. Our analysis reveals model-specific differences in control capabilities and trade-offs between controllability and photorealism, highlighting capability-guided compilation as a promising approach to programmable generative video rendering.
中文翻译
生成式视频模型正变得越来越强大，但缺乏传统系统（如 HTML/CSS 引擎或 Blender）那样的可预测控制接口。其能力隐含于训练过程中，难以确定它们能可靠执行的内容。我们提出神经渲染编译器（NeurCo），通过自动探测冻结的视频模型，构建描述其可可靠满足的语义需求的特定模型手册。NeurCo 将用户意图以模型无关的中间表示（对象、约束、事件、相机）表达，然后编译为特定模型的执行策略。可靠的请求保持不变，不可靠的请求则被选择性重写或编译为替代执行策略。我们引入 VComBench，一个包含 380 个组合性、多需求任务的基准，涵盖文本到视频、图像到视频以及参考到视频生成。NeurCo 在 Wan 上将需求满足率从 46.8% 提升至 52.3%，在 Minimax H3 上从 66.5% 提升至 69.3%，并与提示增强技术结合后在 Seedance 2.5 上进一步提升。我们的分析揭示了模型间控制能力的差异以及可控性与逼真度之间的权衡，凸显了基于能力引导的编译在可编程生成式视频渲染中的前景。

vSFT: Learning to Verbally Tune Agents at Test Time
vSFT：测试时通过语言调优智能体的方法
Large language model agents are increasingly improved via agentic reinforcement learning, which can be on-policy but suffers from expensive rollouts and sparse rewards, limiting its application at test time. In this paper, we present a verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning (vSFT) framework that enables the conjunction of dense feedback in natural language and weight updates at test time. Specifically, our vSFT forms a closed loop between an agent and a supervisor via a hypernetwork, in which the supervisor generates verbal supervision from the agent's failed trajectories, and the hypernetwork converts that supervision into low-rank weight updates to tune the agent. We validate that this test-time in-parameter evolving process outperforms both its in-context counterparts and prevalent reinforcement learning methods. In particular, our vSFT using Qwen3-4B outperforms GRPO and Reflexion by 8.7% and 13.9% in success rate on ALFWorld, and by 4.8% and 18.2% on WebShop, respectively. We believe that learning how to adapt a model, rather than an adapted model, offers a promising path towards more capable agents that improve themselves.
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体越来越多地通过智能体强化学习进行改进，尽管该方法可以是在线策略，但存在昂贵的轨迹回放和稀疏奖励的问题，限制了其在测试时的应用。本文提出一种名为言语监督微调（vSFT）的框架，能够在测试时结合自然语言的密集反馈与权重更新。具体而言，vSFT 通过超网络在智能体与监督者之间形成闭环：监督者从智能体失败的轨迹中生成语言监督，超网络将该监督转换为低秩权重更新以调整智能体。我们验证了这种测试时的参数内演化过程优于其上下文内对应方法和主流强化学习方法。特别地，使用 Qwen3-4B 的 vSFT 在 ALFWorld 上的成功率分别比 GRPO 和 Reflexion 高 8.7% 和 13.9%，在 WebShop 上分别高 4.8% 和 18.2%。我们认为，学习如何适应模型，而非仅仅使用已适应的模型，为更强大的自我改进智能体提供了有前景的路径。
👤 Qishuai Wen, Rui Chen, Haiwen Li, Zhiyuan Huang, Qiwen Gu et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

DocSEAM: Bidirectional Cross-Page Encoding for Multi-Page Document Understanding
DocSEAM：用于多页文档理解的双向跨页编码
Multi-page document vision-language models encode each page independently, creating a feature-level discontinuity at page boundaries: visual tokens are formed without access to content on adjacent pages, and the language model must reason over these page representations formed in isolation. We introduce DocSEAM (Document Selective Enrichment via Anchored Masking), a lightweight module inserted between a pretrained vision encoder and the language model that injects the inductive bias of local cross-page context into visual representations. For each page, DocSEAM forms a local triplet with its two neighbors, applies center-anchored masked self-attention with directional salience gates to fuse information from neighbor pages, and returns only the enriched center-page tokens, leaving the visual token count unchanged. After a supervised cross-page warm-up and multi-page fine-tuning with DocSEAM frozen, our 9B-parameter model achieves state-of-the-art results among the evaluated open-weight models of up to 10B parameters on five multi-page document VQA benchmarks. We further show that under controlled re-pagination, DocSEAM reduces sensitivity to boundary placement and improves cross-page span localization
中文翻译
多页文档视觉-语言模型独立编码每一页，导致页面边界处出现特征级不连续：视觉标记在未访问相邻页面内容的情况下形成，语言模型必须在孤立的页面表示上进行推理。我们提出 DocSEAM（通过锚定掩码进行文档选择性增强），这是一个轻量级模块，插入预训练视觉编码器与语言模型之间，向视觉表示注入局部跨页上下文的归纳偏置。对于每一页，DocSEAM 构建与两个邻页的局部三元组，应用中心锚定的掩码自注意力机制并结合方向显著性门控，融合邻页信息，仅返回增强后的中心页标记，保持视觉标记数量不变。在经过监督的跨页预热和多页微调（DocSEAM 冻结）后，我们的 9B 参数模型在五个多页文档 VQA 基准测试中达到所评估开放权重模型（最多 10B 参数）的最先进水平。我们还表明，在受控重新分页条件下，DocSEAM 减少了对边界位置的敏感性，并改善了跨页跨度定位。

MedSkill-E$^{2}$: Evaluating and Evolving Skills for Medical Agents
MedSkill-E$^{2}$：评估与演化医疗智能体技能
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use skills to access domain knowledge and complete real-world tasks. We observe that even when a skill specifies the rules and procedures needed for a task, agents may fail to apply them effectively during autonomous execution, a phenomenon we call skill Enable But Underuse (EBU). This issue is particularly consequential in medical tasks, where overlooking a single critical constraint can yield a plausible but incorrect result. To evaluate and address EBU, we introduce MedSkill-E$^{2}$, a framework for evaluating and evolving skills for medical agents. We construct EBU-Bench, a challenging benchmark covering 12 medically relevant task groups and associated skills, to evaluate agents' ability to use available skills autonomously. We quantify the EBU gap as the difference in skill-use accuracy between autonomous use and explicitly guided reading and application of the same skill. On EBU-Bench, all evaluated model-harness combinations exhibit EBU, with a mean gap of 43.94 percentage points (pp). For the same model, skill-use accuracy under autonomous use differs by up to 38.96 pp across harnesses. Motivated by these observations, we propose EBU-Evolve, which uses execution feedback from autonomous and explicitly guided skill use to distill effective guidance into reusable skill instructions. Relative to their pre-evolution versions, the revised skills improve skill-use accuracy during autonomous execution by 33.33 to 55.17 pp and continue to yield improvements when transferred across multiple models and harnesses. Our results show that autonomous skill-use accuracy varies across harnesses with the model and skill held fixed, and model rankings can change. EBU can persist even after a skill is opened. EBU-Evolve improves autonomous skill-use accuracy on unseen tasks within the same skill, with gains transferring across models and harnesses.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地使用技能来访问领域知识并完成现实任务。我们观察到，即使技能明确指定了任务所需规则和流程，智能体在自主执行过程中仍可能无法有效应用，这种现象称为‘技能启用但使用不足’（EBU）。该问题在医疗任务中尤为严重，忽略单一关键约束可能导致看似合理但错误的结果。为评估和解决EBU问题，我们提出MedSkill-E$^{2}$，一个用于评估和演化医疗智能体技能的框架。我们构建了EBU-Bench，一个涵盖12个医学相关任务组及其对应技能的挑战性基准，用于评估智能体自主使用可用技能的能力。我们量化EBU差距为自主使用与显式引导阅读和应用同一技能之间的技能使用准确率差异。在EBU-Bench上，所有评估的模型-工具组合均表现出EBU，平均差距达43.94个百分点（pp）。对于同一模型，不同工具组合下的自主技能使用准确率差异可达38.96 pp。基于这些观察，我们提出EBU-Evolve，利用自主与显式引导技能使用中的执行反馈，将有效指导提炼为可复用的技能指令。相较于进化前版本，改进后的技能在自主执行中提升了33.33至55.17 pp的技能使用准确率，并在跨多个模型和工具组合迁移时仍保持提升。结果表明，在固定模型和技能条件下，自主技能使用准确率随工具变化而波动，模型排名也可能改变。EBU即使在技能开放后仍可能持续存在。EBU-Evolve在未见过的任务中提升了同一技能的自主使用准确率，且提升效果可在模型和工具间迁移。

Budget, Readout or Reasoning? Where Small Reasoning Models Lose Value in Auction Bidding
预算、读出还是推理？小型推理模型在拍卖竞价中的价值损失来源
Ad platforms and marketplaces show bidders a bid landscape, the estimated chance of winning at each candidate bid, and increasingly let agents built on reasoning language models choose the bid. We give six open reasoning models of 4B to 14B parameters such landscapes, with win curves from eBay closing prices and from a simulated advertising market (AuctionNet), and score their bids on held-out auctions against an exact calculator. Because the agent is handed the estimates, the lost value can be split by cause. Two failures belong to the models. They pass over the best option even when the values written in their own trace already rank it first, and their wrong answers lean toward the highest bid on the menu. The lean is about the bid's size, not its place in the list, and it survives sampling. At 768 forced tokens on eBay, under pay-your-bid pricing, the wrong answers cost bidders 7-17% of the surplus the calculator's bids earn. Arithmetic errors are rare. Two further losses come from operator choices. A token cap cuts traces short: allowed to stop on their own within 4,096 tokens, five models recover 91-100% of the calculator's lead over a model that does no reasoning. Asking for the answer while the reasoning block is still open misreads it: closing the block first lifts the weakest model from 42% to 84% on eBay and from 24% to 81% on AuctionNet. Finally, an audit that flags bids disagreeing with the trace's own numbers, and overrides them with the calculator's bid, raises the share of the calculator's lead recovered at 768 forced tokens from 42-85% to 84-100% on eBay under nested cross-validation, and the same reader settings transfer from eBay to AuctionNet.
中文翻译
广告平台和市场向竞标者展示出价格局，即在每个候选出价下的中标概率，并越来越多地允许基于推理型语言模型构建的智能体自主选择出价。我们向六个参数规模为4B至14B的开放推理模型提供了来自eBay成交价格和模拟广告市场（AuctionNet）的胜率曲线，并将其出价在保留拍卖中与精确计算器的结果进行评分。由于代理已获得估计值，可将价值损失按原因拆分。其中两个失败源于模型自身：即使其自身轨迹中已将最优选项排在首位，仍会忽略该选项；且错误答案倾向于选择菜单中最高金额的出价。这种倾向是出价金额本身所致，而非列表中的位置，且在采样后依然存在。在eBay上强制768个token条件下，采用支付自己出价定价机制时，错误答案导致竞标者损失计算器出价所获盈余的7%-17%。算术错误罕见。另有两项损失来自操作选择：令牌上限截断了推理轨迹；允许自主停止在4096个token内，五个模型恢复了计算器领先优势的91%-100%，而无推理模型则无法做到。在推理块仍开放时要求答案会导致误读：先关闭推理块可使最弱模型在eBay上的表现从42%提升至84%，在AuctionNet上从24%提升至81%。最后，一项审计机制会标记与自身轨迹数值不一致的出价，并以计算器出价覆盖之，使在768个强制token条件下，eBay上保留验证中恢复的计算器领先份额从42%-85%提升至84%-100%；相同设置在eBay与AuctionNet之间具有可迁移性。
👤 Mahbod Khaleti, Seyed Reza Tavakoli, Baran Hoseini, Alireza Noori, Amir Amini et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
