State Space Attention: Learning Context-Dependent Memory Retention
状态空间注意力：学习上下文相关的记忆保留
Standard attention's key-value cache grows linearly with context length. Bounding it raises the question of what a sequence model should retain for long-context reasoning. We argue this choice should be learned, and introduce State Space Attention (SSA), which learns to keep a bounded set of past representations and reads them with standard attention. A learned scorer rescores every chunk against newly arriving context and the rest of memory. A fixed-context Transformer is then the special case that always keeps the most recent tokens. We train the scorer and reader jointly, either moving gradually from a differentiable soft weighting of memories to hard retention, or with hard retention from the start. To produce signal for the scorer, we generate supervised samples during training, a technique we call recovery. We compare SSA with recurrent models, bounded-memory attention methods, and full-attention Transformers across retrieval, variable tracking, multi-hop reasoning, and language modeling. On our retrieval and reasoning benchmarks, SSA matches or comes within a few points of full attention on most tasks, far outperforms a Transformer with the same readable budget on every long-context retrieval task we evaluate, and matches or outperforms one with about 4 times that budget, while using constant inference memory. The largest remaining gap to full attention at small language model scale is on RULER. SSA makes a fixed number of tokens useful over a longer context, and since it reads memory with standard attention, we propose that it can be stacked with KV cache eviction methods. We also pretrain and post-train 400M-parameter models and measure how SSA's training and inference costs scale with context length and model size. Together, these experiments examine the capabilities and limitations of learned memory retention for long-context sequence modeling.
中文翻译
标准注意力的关键值缓存随上下文长度线性增长。限制其大小引发了序列模型在长上下文推理中应保留什么的问题。我们认为这一选择应当通过学习获得，并提出状态空间注意力（SSA），它学习保留一个有限的过去表示集，并使用标准注意力读取这些记忆。一个学习到的评分器对每个块相对于新到达的上下文和其余记忆进行重评分。固定上下文Transformer是始终保留最近标记的特例。我们联合训练评分器和读取器，或从可微软加权记忆逐渐过渡到硬保留，或从一开始就采用硬保留。为生成评分器信号，我们在训练期间生成有监督样本，这一技术称为恢复。我们在检索、变量跟踪、多跳推理和语言建模任务上对比了SSA与循环模型、有界记忆注意力方法及全注意力Transformer。在我们的检索和推理基准测试中，SSA在大多数任务上达到或接近全注意力性能，远优于具有相同可读预算的Transformer在所有长上下文检索任务上的表现，且在约四倍预算的Transformer上也表现相当或更优，同时推理内存恒定。在小规模语言模型上，与全注意力的差距主要存在于RULER任务。SSA使固定数量的标记在更长上下文中保持有用性，由于其使用标准注意力读取记忆，我们建议可将其与KV缓存淘汰方法堆叠。我们还预训练并后训练了4亿参数模型，测量SSA在上下文长度和模型规模下的训练与推理成本。这些实验共同考察了学习记忆保留在长上下文序列建模中的能力与局限。
👤 Ege Çakar, Cengiz Pehlevan 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

CA-RAG: Constraint-Aligned Retrieval for Structurally Faithful Long-Form Generation
CA-RAG：结构忠实的长文本生成中的约束对齐检索
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically optimises evidence selection for semantic relevance, whereas compliance-sensitive long-form generation also requires evidence supporting explicit structural obligations. Semantically relevant retrieval may therefore omit information needed for mandatory downstream clauses. We propose Constraint-Aligned RAG (CA-RAG), a retrieval-conditioning framework that augments section-specific queries with ontology-derived structural constraints and policy-grounded knowledge-graph relations before evidence selection. Retrieval-only evaluation with manually annotated evidence shows consistent improvements across retrieval budgets. At $k=6$, CA-RAG increases Precision@$k$ from $0.5268$ to $0.5692$, pooled Recall@$k$ from $0.5402$ to $0.5950$, and F1@$k$ from $0.5389$ to $0.5672$ over standard RAG. Generation experiments further show improvements in compliance and reliability in several model--domain settings, while generation-only constraints achieve higher clause coverage but lower overall compliance and reliability. These results indicate that structurally aligned retrieval can improve evidence sufficiency in compliance-sensitive long-form generation.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）通常优化证据选择以实现语义相关性，但在合规敏感的长文本生成中，还要求证据支持明确的结构义务。因此，仅语义相关的检索可能遗漏后续必要条款所需的信息。我们提出约束对齐检索（CA-RAG），一种在证据选择前通过本体导出的结构约束和基于政策的知识图谱关系来增强特定章节查询的检索条件框架。仅使用人工标注证据的检索评估显示，在不同检索预算下均有持续改进。当k=6时，CA-RAG将标准RAG的Precision@k从0.5268提升至0.5692，Pooled Recall@k从0.5402提升至0.5950，F1@k从0.5389提升至0.5672。生成实验进一步表明，在多个模型-领域设置中，合规性和可靠性均有所提升；而仅生成阶段施加约束虽能提高条款覆盖率，但整体合规性和可靠性较低。结果表明，结构对齐的检索可提升合规敏感型长文本生成中的证据充分性。
👤 Vimal Kumar Kumar, Meghana Kshirsagar, Conor Ryan 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

HumblePRM: Improving VLM Abstention in Ambiguous Situations through Process Reward Modeling
HumblePRM：通过过程奖励建模提升视觉语言模型在模糊情境下的回避能力
Reliable deployment of Vision Language Models (VLMs) requires that a model's expressed confidence remain grounded in what it can actually see, and that it recognize when the visual evidence is too ambiguous to commit to a confident response. Standard post-training uses outcome reward models (ORMs) that supervise only the final decision, rewarding a confident answer regardless of whether the image supports one, while existing methods either tend to detect uncertainty post-hoc or assume an objective verdict always exists. We instead split multimodal reasoning into two stages: perception, where the model must factually describe the scene, and calibration, where it must judge whether that evidence is sufficient to commit to a conclusion. We introduce HumblePRM, a lightweight process reward model (PRM) that scores each stage independently, rewarding agreement between evidence and expressed confidence rather than outcome correctness alone. Training with HumblePRM increases abstention due to uncertainty on ambiguous inputs considerably while leaving accuracy on clear images roughly unchanged. We also find that the improvement transfers to Gemma 4 E4B, a larger, popular edge model. Our findings show that process reward supervision is a promising route to VLMs with better calibrated abstention, ensuring more reliability rather than unpredictable behavior.
中文翻译
可靠部署视觉语言模型（VLMs）要求模型表达的信心与其实际可见内容保持一致，并能在视觉证据过于模糊时识别并避免做出自信回答。标准后训练使用仅监督最终决策的结果奖励模型（ORMs），无论图像是否支持，都会奖励自信答案；而现有方法要么倾向于事后检测不确定性，要么假设始终存在客观结论。我们则将多模态推理分为两个阶段：感知阶段，模型需客观描述场景；校准阶段，模型需判断该证据是否足以得出结论。我们引入HumblePRM，一种轻量级的过程奖励模型（PRM），独立评分每个阶段，奖励证据与表达信心的一致性，而非仅关注结果正确性。使用HumblePRM训练显著提升了在模糊输入下的回避行为，同时在清晰图像上的准确率基本不变。我们还发现该改进可迁移至Gemma 4 E4B这一更大、流行的边缘模型。研究结果表明，过程奖励监督是实现更校准回避行为的有前景路径，从而提升VLM的可靠性而非不可预测行为。
👤 Saket Reddy, Ke Yang, ChengXiang Zhai 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Temporal Weight Surgery: Repairing Video Grounding with LLM Editors
时间权重手术：使用 LLM 编辑器修复视频定位
A surgeon first finds what is wrong, then fixes only that part. We use this idea for video grounding, where video-language models often find the right moment in a long video but write its time wrong. In Temporal Weight Surgery (TWS), a general-purpose LLM reads the model's weights and activations, finds where the error arises and repairs it there, without gradients. Separate sessions find the same cause, and this diagnosis of where and why the model fails is our main contribution. Three GPT-6 Astra sessions trace a dropped digit in TimeLens-8B to one MLP layer, and the largest edit gains 7.9 mIoU on 1,215 unseen questions and 18.3 on videos of 30 minutes or more. Two Claude Opus 5.5 sessions trace minutes written as seconds in Qwen3-VL-8B to the separator and gain 2.9 and 1.3. Before any edit, its answers often have the right minute and the wrong separator, and fixing only the separator lifts their mIoU from 13.1 to 19.2. Every improving edit beats a random edit of its size, and the edited models run as fast as before.
中文翻译
外科医生先找出问题所在，再仅修复该部分。我们将这一理念应用于视频定位任务，其中视频-语言模型常能正确识别长视频中的关键时刻，但时间标注错误。在时间权重手术（TWS）中，一个通用的 LLM 读取模型的权重和激活，发现错误来源并直接在该位置进行修复，无需梯度。多次独立会话均定位到相同原因，这种对模型失败“何处”和“为何”的诊断是本文的主要贡献。三次 GPT-6 Astra 会话将 TimeLens-8B 中丢失的数字归因于一个 MLP 层，最大编辑使 1,215 个未见问题的 mIoU 提升 7.9，30 分钟以上视频的 mIoU 提升 18.3。两次 Claude Opus 5.5 会话将 Qwen3-VL-8B 中将分钟误写为秒的问题归因于分隔符，分别带来 2.9 和 1.3 的提升。在任何编辑前，其答案通常有正确的分钟但错误的分隔符，仅修复分隔符即可使 mIoU 从 13.1 提升至 19.2。每次改进的编辑均优于同等规模的随机编辑，且编辑后模型运行速度不变。

Two Timescales of Forgetting in Fine-tuning of Language Models: Fast Behavioral Suppression and Slow Representational Drift
语言模型微调中的遗忘双重时间尺度：快速行为抑制与缓慢表征漂移
Today, a core pipeline of artificial intelligence systems is pretraining on a broad range of text corpora and then fine-tuning on specific tasks or desired behaviors. Yet we still have limited understanding of how fine-tuning on a restricted data distribution modifies representations acquired during pretraining. In particular, it remains unclear whether surface behavioral suppression happens together with the erasure of latent representations that support it. In this work, we study supervised fine-tuning (SFT) by pretraining transformers on a controlled context-free grammar with hierarchical latent structure, the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and fine-tuning with a restricted subset of its generative rules. While during pretraining the model's ability to generate with the correct grammar rules coincides with building the internal representations of the corresponding latent, we find that SFT exhibits a very different dynamics: (i) the generation of deleted rules changes on a fast timescale inversely proportional to the magnitude of the token-latent correlation; meanwhile, their corresponding representations stay intact; (ii) the hidden representations of the deleted rules become forgotten on a much longer timescale, associated with a slow drift of the network parameters. Finally, we observe analogous results in a realistic setup with natural language, suggesting that this timescale separation in forgetting is a more general phenomenon of fine-tuning language models.
中文翻译
当前人工智能系统的核心流程是先在广泛文本语料上进行预训练，再针对特定任务或期望行为进行微调。然而，我们对在有限数据分布上的微调如何改变预训练期间获得的表征仍知之甚少。特别是，表面行为抑制是否伴随支持该行为的潜在表征的消除尚不明确。本研究通过在受控的上下文无关语法（具有层次化潜在结构的随机层次模型RHM）上预训练Transformer，并使用其生成规则的受限子集进行监督微调（SFT），探讨了这一问题。尽管在预训练阶段，模型正确生成语法规则的能力与其内部潜在表征的建立同步，但SFT展现出截然不同的动态：(i) 被删除规则的生成变化发生在快速时间尺度上，且与词元-潜在相关性的大小成反比；而这些规则对应的表征保持完整；(ii) 被删除规则的隐藏表征则在更长的时间尺度上逐渐遗忘，与网络参数的缓慢漂移相关。最后，我们在真实自然语言设置中观察到类似结果，表明这种遗忘的时间尺度分离是语言模型微调中的普遍现象。
👤 Yilin Luo, Jin Hwa Lee, Andrew M. Saxe, Antonio Sclocchi 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

CypherMAS: Benchmarking Routing Reliability in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems
CypherMAS：基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中路由可靠性的基准测试
Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) distribute reasoning across specialized agents that must exchange messages and invoke tools reliably. Existing evaluations typically couple communication with open domain tasks, making it difficult to determine whether a failure arises from reasoning, malformed tool use, lost messages, or forgotten requests. Prior benchmarks do not isolate communication execution or measure routing redundancy against a known optimal path. We introduce CypherMAS, a synthetic benchmark that isolates communication and routing in fixed multi-agent graphs. Agents in chain, star, bipartite, or ring graphs route cyphered messages to remote peers holding the matching decoders; since the optimal route is known, redundant LLM calls can be measured and routing failures may be easily tracked. Across eleven open-weight models from 4B to 753B, small models fail at routing and tool use, dense models above 27B degrade with diameter, and successful runs exceed minimum LLM calls. These findings identify communication reliability as a distinct scaling bottleneck for multi-agent systems and provide a controlled diagnostic for complex multi-agent systems. The source code is freely available: https://github.com/Andoree/CypherMAS.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的多智能体系统（MAS）将推理分布在多个专用智能体之间，这些智能体必须可靠地交换消息并调用工具。现有评估通常将通信与开放域任务耦合，难以判断失败是源于推理错误、工具调用格式错误、消息丢失还是请求遗忘。先前的基准测试未隔离通信执行过程，也未在已知最优路径下衡量路由冗余。我们引入CypherMAS，一个合成基准测试，专门隔离固定多智能体图中的通信与路由。在链式、星型、二分图或环形图中，智能体需将加密消息路由至持有匹配解码器的远程同伴；由于最优路径已知，可测量冗余的LLM调用并轻松追踪路由失败。在11个从4B到753B的开源模型上进行测试发现：小型模型在路由和工具使用上表现不佳，超过27B的密集模型随图直径增大而性能下降，成功运行的实例均超过最低LLM调用次数。这些发现揭示通信可靠性是多智能体系统的一个独立缩放瓶颈，并为复杂多智能体系统提供了可控诊断工具。源代码公开可用：https://github.com/Andoree/CypherMAS。
👤 Danil Kostromin, Andrey Sakhovskiy, Semen Budennyy 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Semantic Density Does Not Predict Agent-Memory Collapse
语义密度无法预测智能体记忆崩溃
Persistent textual memory can improve LLM agents, but repeated updates can erode task performance. We ask whether memory-store statistics warn before held-out accuracy declines. We registered mean pairwise cosine similarity ($ρ$) and 13 additional geometry, retrieval, and graph statistics on 805 snapshots from 13 memory methods across ALFWorld, AppWorld, ScienceWorld, WebShop, and two ARC-AGI settings. These methods store trajectories, rules, workflows, strategies, or linked records, using selective retrieval or whole-store prompting. No such statistic reliably predicted early signs of task accuracy decline, and $ρ$ had zero median lead. With the checkpoint after peak accuracy as a secondary decline point, measures of highly similar pairs and nearest-neighbor crowding changed earlier but did not consistently outperform stored-item count. We trained classifiers on this corpus and applied them unchanged to PropWorld runs of up to 5,000 crafting tasks. They falsely flagged 56.9% of checkpoints from size-limited stores with stable accuracy and detected only 18% of checkpoints after task success fell to zero. Thus, global semantic density did not provide a reliable warning, while local crowding did not transfer consistently across memory designs. In these long-horizon runs, size-limited stores remained stable, while the sole failure arose when unbounded whole-store prompting exceeded model input capacity, which token growth predicted directly.
中文翻译
持久的文本记忆可提升LLM智能体的表现，但重复更新可能导致任务性能下降。我们探究记忆存储的统计特征是否能在任务准确率下降前发出预警。我们在ALFWorld、AppWorld、ScienceWorld、WebShop及两个ARC-AGI设置中，对13种记忆方法共805个快照记录了平均成对余弦相似度（ρ）及其他13项几何、检索和图论统计量。这些方法存储轨迹、规则、工作流、策略或关联记录，采用选择性检索或全存储提示。结果显示，没有任何统计量能可靠预测任务准确率下降的早期迹象，ρ的中位数领先时间为零。以峰值准确率后的检查点作为次级下降点，高度相似对和最近邻拥挤度的变化较早出现，但并未持续优于存储项数量。我们在此数据集上训练分类器，并将其直接应用于PropWorld中长达5000个制造任务的运行。结果发现，该分类器错误标记了56.9%的尺寸受限存储中准确率稳定的检查点，仅检测到18%的任务成功后准确率归零的检查点。因此，全局语义密度无法提供可靠预警，局部拥挤度也无法在不同记忆设计间稳定迁移。在这些长周期运行中，尺寸受限存储保持稳定，唯一失败情况出现在无界全存储提示超出模型输入容量时，而该问题可通过token增长直接预测。

Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces for Hard Math Problems
针对难题数学问题的多样化替代推理路径
Post-training large language models (LLMs) for mathematical reasoning often relies on accepted traces whose final answers pass a verifier. For hard math problems, however, such traces can be rare under independent Monte Carlo (MC) sampling. To address this, we propose Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces (DART), a trace-mining method that starts from a rejected trace, decomposes it into reasoning steps, and ranks them by position-weighted uncertainty. It then generates deliberately distinct alternative candidates for highly ranked steps using reference-answer guidance and a meta-thought before completing the resulting traces. On greedy-unsolved DeepMath-103K problems, DART recovers 37% more problems than MC sampling at a matched number of generated traces. Its estimated generation cost per recovered problem is nearly identical, while its cost per accepted trace is 30% lower. Post-training Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on traces mined by DART achieves the highest point estimate in five of six evaluations across MATH-500 and AIME benchmarks. These results demonstrate that revisiting rejected traces with DART can expand problem coverage and provide effective post-training data.
中文翻译
后训练大型语言模型（LLMs）进行数学推理通常依赖于通过验证器检验正确的推理路径。然而，对于困难的数学问题，独立蒙特卡洛（MC）采样下此类路径可能极为稀少。为解决此问题，我们提出DART（多样化替代推理路径）方法：从被拒绝的路径出发，将其分解为推理步骤，并根据位置加权不确定性进行排序；随后利用参考答案指导和元思考生成高度不同的替代候选路径以完成推理链。在贪婪未解的DeepMath-103K问题上，DART在生成路径数量相同的情况下比MC采样多恢复37%的问题，其每恢复一个问题的估计生成成本几乎相同，而每通过一条路径的成本降低30%。使用DART挖掘的路径对Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct进行后训练，在MATH-500和AIME基准测试的六项评估中，有五项达到最高点估计值。结果表明，通过DART重新审视被拒绝路径可显著扩展问题覆盖范围，并提供有效的后训练数据。

Repairing the Trustworthiness of Distilled Language Models
修复蒸馏语言模型的可信性
Knowledge distillation enables the deployment of large language models through smaller student models, but the compression process can introduce regressions in trustworthiness even when downstream performance is preserved. Repairing such failures is challenging because the original distillation corpus is often unavailable, while direct preference optimization may alter capabilities inherited from the teacher. We propose a targeted repair framework based on the proximal Bregman response function (PBRF) that addresses both limitations. We estimate the curvature required for PBRF using a surrogate proxy dataset, removing dependence on the original transfer corpus, and extend the framework to preference-based supervision through an odds-ratio objective. This enables the use of preferred and dispreferred response pairs while constraining changes to the distilled model. Our results show that the proposed approach improves targeted trustworthiness behaviors for both pre-training and post-training models while largely preserving downstream performance, providing a practical alternative to re-distillation or unconstrained preference optimization. Our experiment results show that across different distilled models, the trustworthy regression can be improved by up to 8% with less than 1% decrement in perplexity.
中文翻译
知识蒸馏使得通过较小的学生模型部署大型语言模型成为可能，但压缩过程可能导致可信性下降，即使下游性能保持不变。修复此类问题具有挑战性，因为原始蒸馏语料库通常不可用，而直接偏好优化可能会改变从教师模型继承的能力。我们提出一种基于近似Bregman响应函数（PBRF）的目标修复框架，解决了这两个局限性。我们使用代理数据集估计PBRF所需的曲率，从而摆脱对原始迁移语料库的依赖，并通过奇数比目标将框架扩展至基于偏好的监督。这使得在约束蒸馏模型变化的前提下，能够使用偏好和不偏好响应对进行训练。实验结果表明，该方法在预训练和后训练模型上均显著提升了特定可信性行为，同时基本保持了下游性能，为重新蒸馏或无约束偏好优化提供了一种实用替代方案。在不同蒸馏模型上，可信性退化可改善高达8%，且困惑度下降不足1%。
👤 Kumar Shubham, Devendra Vyas, Vidushi Agarwal, Jayin Khanna, Prathosh AP 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

PersonaFactory: Stylistic User Prompt Augmentation with Synthetic Persona Bank
PersonaFactory：基于合成人格库的风格化用户提示增强
Large language models increasingly replace real people in the AI development pipeline, as they are used to generate training dialogues at scale and to drive alignment and evaluation. However, out-of-the-box models produce monolingual and stylistically flat texts as synthetic user prompts, failing to replicate the real user-AI communication mechanics. We present PersonaFactory, an end-to-end pipeline that generates detailed user personas and uses them to augment existing user prompts from their point of view, producing a bank of over 740000 structured synthetic personas detailing their background and communication behaviors, and a corpus of roughly 1.24 million persona-styled synthetic conversations spanning 85 languages. We evaluate the effect of personal features on style adoption and intent preservation, using an LLM-judge and heuristic metrics to assess augmentation fidelity together with the downstream impact on assistant persona control through training data. Results show that our structured, attribute-based style representation enables per-axis control and better intent preservation at comparable style fidelity than free-text persona descriptions used in existing methods. A human expert evaluation found the augmented conversations plausible, with favorable ratings for intent preservation, style naturalness, and multilingual authenticity. Finally, PersonaFactory conversations achieve comparable performance to original training data on capability benchmarks, are preferred on real user prompts at equal reply length, and adapt their reply style more closely to users without explicit steering.

大语言模型越来越多地取代真实人在AI开发流程中的角色，用于大规模生成训练对话，以及推动对齐与评估。然而，开箱即用的模型生成的合成用户提示往往具有单一语言性和风格单调性，无法再现真实的用户-AI交互机制。我们提出PersonaFactory，一个端到端流程，能够生成详细用户人格，并从其视角增强现有用户提示，构建了一个包含超过74万条结构化合成人格的数据库，涵盖背景信息和沟通行为，并生成约124万条跨越85种语言的人格化合成对话。我们评估了个人特征对风格采纳和意图保留的影响，使用LLM裁判和启发式指标评估增强保真度，以及对训练数据中助手人格控制的下游影响。结果表明，我们的结构化属性风格表示实现了轴向控制，在与现有方法使用的自由文本人格描述相当的风格保真度下，显著提升了意图保留效果。人类专家评估认为增强后的对话合理可信，且在意图保留、风格自然性和多语言真实性方面获得正面评价。最终，PersonaFactory生成的对话在能力基准测试中表现与原始训练数据相当，在同等回复长度的真实用户提示下更受青睐，并能在无需显式引导的情况下更紧密地适应用户回复风格。

DFly2: Turbocharging Long-Context Speculative Decoding by Sharing the Target's Sparsity
DFly2：通过共享目标稀疏性加速长上下文推测解码
To avoid the quadratic cost of dense attention over long contexts, models such as DeepSeek-V4 and Hy4 have turned natively sparse, and speculative decoding, which matters most where decoding is slowest, increasingly runs on them. Their drafters, however, still keep a context cache of their own, over the whole prompt or a sliding window. Beyond memory, such a cache costs prefill time, prefix-cache hits and decode concurrency. Drafters that read the target's cache avoid one of their own, but settle only which cache, not which positions, and still read everything or a recent window. Neither rule is made for the query at hand, and computing a selection per query in the drafter would cost more as the context grows. Yet a natively sparse target already picks positions for each query, over a cache it keeps anyway. Both of the drafter's choices are therefore already made, and paid for, by the target: its cache and its selection. We measure how far one selection carries: made a step early, it still covers close to nine tenths of what each later token selects on DS-V4.1-Flash. We present DFly2, a block-parallel drafter that shares the target's sparsity. Each draft layer reads the KV its paired target layer cached (KV sharing), at the positions that layer's indexer selected, a fixed number anywhere in the context (Index sharing). DFly2 thus runs no scoring pass and keeps no cache of its own. On DS-V4.1-Flash, GLM-5.3-Flash and Hy4~flash, whose sparse attention and architecture differ, it accepts more tokens per step than DFlash and DSpark trained on the same recipe, over general, long-context and agentic benchmarks. Its margin over the stronger baseline widens from 3.9%-10.5% on general workloads to 10.0%-31.5% on long-context and agentic ones. At high concurrency on DS-V4.1-Flash, at matched per-user decode speed, its throughput is 62% and 81% higher on two 64K sets.
中文翻译
为避免长上下文密集注意力带来的二次计算成本，DeepSeek-V4和Hy4等模型已采用原生稀疏架构，而推测解码——在解码最慢时尤为关键——也越来越多地运行在这些模型上。然而，其草稿模型仍保留自己的完整上下文缓存，覆盖整个提示或滑动窗口。除了内存开销外，这种缓存还会增加预填充时间、前缀缓存命中率和解码并发性开销。若草稿模型读取目标模型的缓存，可省去自身的一个缓存，但仅能决定读取哪个缓存，无法决定读取哪些位置，仍需读取全部或最近窗口内容。这两种规则均未针对具体查询优化，且在草稿模型中为每个查询计算选择策略会随上下文增长而带来更高成本。然而，原生稀疏的目标模型已为每个查询选择出位置，且其缓存本就存在。因此，草稿模型的两项选择——缓存和位置——均已由目标模型完成并支付成本。我们测量了这一选择的传播范围：在DS-V4.1-Flash上，提前一步的选择仍能覆盖后续每个token选择的近90%。我们提出DFly2，一种块并行草稿模型，共享目标模型的稀疏性。每个草稿层读取其对应目标层缓存的KV（KV共享），并在该层索引器选定的位置读取（索引共享），固定数量的任意位置均可。因此，DFly2无需评分遍历，也不保留自身缓存。在DS-V4.1-Flash、GLM-5.3-Flash和Hy4~flash等稀疏注意力和架构各异的模型上，DFly2在每步接受的token数量超过DFlash和DSpark（同配方训练），在通用、长上下文和智能体基准上均表现更优。相较于更强基线，其优势从通用任务的3.9%-10.5%扩大至长上下文和智能体任务的10.0%-31.5%。在DS-V4.1-Flash高并发场景下，相同用户解码速度条件下，吞吐量分别提升62%和81%。
👤 Hong Liu, Rui Cen, Junhan Shi, Lin Niu, Runzhi Fan et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34756]

Collect for Coverage, Replay with Evidence: Adaptive and Efficient On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents
为覆盖而收集，以证据重放：面向长时程智能体的自适应高效在线蒸馏
Fully online on-policy distillation (OPD) is costly for long-horizon agents because each update depends on fresh environment rollouts. Prefix replay offers an efficient alternative by sampling current-student responses from stored histories, reducing idle time for long-tail rollouts and allowing each collected trajectory to support multiple updates. However, our analysis of the complete prefix replay pipeline identifies two limitations in long-horizon settings. In trajectory collection, teacher-only trajectories miss student-induced histories, while broader history coverage can reduce teacher reliability. In trajectory reuse, fixed position-based schedules cannot identify critical decisions that may occur at any trajectory depth. We introduce CARE-OPD (Collect for coverAge, Replay with Evidence) to address both limitations. Student-seeded teacher completions and response-level confidence gating expand history coverage while filtering unreliable updates. Hindsight replay allocation then uses downstream execution evidence to select consequential turns. Across long-horizon tasks Software Engineering and Deep Research, CARE-OPD outperforms online OPD and prior prefix replay baselines. On SWE-bench Verified, it raises task success to 59.5% while reducing end-to-end training time by 45% and environment actions by 44% relative to asynchronous online OPD.
中文翻译
完全在线的在线蒸馏（OPD）对长时程智能体来说成本较高，因为每次更新都依赖于新的环境滚动。前缀重放通过从存储的历史中采样当前学生响应，提供了一种高效的替代方案，减少了长尾滚动的空闲时间，并使每条收集到的轨迹支持多次更新。然而，我们对完整前缀重放流程的分析揭示了在长时程设置下的两个局限性：在轨迹收集阶段，仅使用教师轨迹会遗漏学生引发的历史，而更广泛的历史覆盖又可能降低教师的可靠性；在轨迹重用阶段，基于固定位置的调度无法识别可能出现在任意轨迹深度的关键决策。为此，我们提出了CARE-OPD（为覆盖而收集，以证据重放），以解决这两个问题。通过学生引导的教师补全和响应级别的置信度门控，扩展历史覆盖范围的同时过滤不可靠的更新。事后重放分配则利用下游执行证据来选择具有重要意义的转折点。在软件工程和深度研究等长时程任务中，CARE-OPD的表现优于在线OPD和先前的前缀重放基线。在SWE-bench Verified上，其任务成功率提升至59.5%，同时相比异步在线OPD，端到端训练时间减少45%，环境动作减少44%。
👤 Haowen Hou, Gen Li, Ruilin Li, Yicheng Liu, Zhen Huang et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

PoVi: Agentic Poetry-to-Video Generation with Knowledge Grounding and Figurative Understanding
PoVi：基于知识锚定与隐喻理解的智能体诗歌转视频生成
Poetry-to-video generation requires more than translating text into visual scenes. Poetic language is highly compressed, often depends on historical and cultural knowledge, and frequently conveys meaning through metaphor, imagery, and other non-literal expressions. We introduce PoVi, a benchmark and agentic framework for grounded poetry-to-video generation, evaluating semantic grounding, discourse coherence, and audiovisual realization. PoVi reconstructs poem semantics into an evidence-bounded representation and progressively transforms them into narration, interpretation, character assets, and shot-level visual plans, followed by narration-aligned video generation. Across three planning backbones and four video generators, PoVi consistently improves over single-call generation, with the largest gains on implicit background knowledge and metaphorical relations. PoVi provides a testbed for studying knowledge-grounded, long-horizon multimodal generation beyond direct text-to-video synthesis.
中文翻译
诗歌转视频生成不仅需要将文本翻译为视觉场景，还需处理诗意语言的高度压缩性、对历史与文化知识的依赖性，以及通过隐喻、意象等非字面表达传递意义的特点。我们提出了PoVi，一个基准测试与智能体框架，用于评估语义锚定、话语连贯性和音视频实现能力。PoVi将诗歌语义重构为受证据约束的表示形式，并逐步转化为叙述、解释、角色资产和逐镜头视觉计划，随后生成与叙述对齐的视频。在三种规划骨干和四种视频生成器上，PoVi始终优于单次调用生成方法，尤其在隐含背景知识和隐喻关系方面提升显著。PoVi为研究超越直接文本到视频合成的知识锚定型、长时程多模态生成提供了实验平台。

[2611.34529]

AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base
AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base
We present AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base, a mixture-of-experts language model with 80 billion total parameters and approximately 3 billion active parameters per token, trained from scratch at Yandex and released under the Apache 2.0 license. The model combines Kimi Delta Attention with full attention, uses Attention Residuals, and is trained with Muon. Training comprises general pretraining on 17.5 trillion tokens, two context-extension stages, and a dedicated stage for reasoning and agentic interaction. We describe the data collection and augmentation pipelines, evaluation protocols, architectural ablations, and scaling-law experiments. We identify the limitations of held-out training loss as a proxy for downstream performance and introduce an auxiliary loss on mixture-of-experts outputs to stabilize training at the predicted learning rate. Domain-specific data selection and augmentation improve factual knowledge, mathematics, and programming skills, while agent trajectories introduced during pretraining improve performance on agentic benchmarks after supervised fine-tuning. In zero-shot reasoning evaluation, the model achieves leading results among open base models of comparable size, with 88.7% pass@8 on IMO-AnswerBench and 82.9% pass@8 on LiveCodeBench v5-v6. To evaluate Russian-language factual knowledge, we introduce and release two benchmarks with their evaluation protocols: WikiWebFacts, which tests short factual questions drawn from encyclopedic sources and search queries, and HardMultiQA, which tests more demanding factual questions inspired by user interactions. Our model scores 86.5% on WikiWebFacts and 67.9% on HardMultiQA, compared with 83.2% and 65.4% for DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Base (284B total and 13B active parameters) and 72.8% and 54.5% for Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base (120B total and 12B active parameters), outperforming both larger models on these benchmarks.
中文翻译
我们提出了 AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base，这是一个具有800亿总参数、每token约30亿活跃参数的专家混合语言模型，由Yandex从头训练并以Apache 2.0许可证发布。该模型结合了Kimi Delta Attention与全注意力机制，采用注意力残差，并使用Muon进行训练。训练包括在17.5万亿token上的通用预训练、两个上下文扩展阶段，以及一个专门用于推理和代理交互的阶段。我们描述了数据收集与增强流程、评估协议、架构消融实验和缩放定律实验。我们指出保留训练损失作为下游性能代理的局限性，并引入一种针对专家混合输出的辅助损失，以在预测学习率下稳定训练。特定领域数据选择与增强提升了事实知识、数学和编程能力；预训练期间引入的代理轨迹在监督微调后提升了代理基准表现。在零样本推理评估中，该模型在同规模开源基础模型中表现领先，在IMO-AnswerBench上达到88.7%的pass@8，在LiveCodeBench v5-v6上达到82.9%的pass@8。为评估俄语事实知识，我们引入并发布了两个基准及其评估协议：WikiWebFacts（测试来自百科来源和搜索查询的简短事实问题）和HardMultiQA（测试更具挑战性的事实问题，灵感来自用户交互）。我们的模型在WikiWebFacts上得分为86.5%，在HardMultiQA上得分为67.9%，优于DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Base（284B总参数，13B活跃参数）的83.2%和65.4%，也优于Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base（120B总参数，12B活跃参数）的72.8%和54.5%，在这些基准上超越了更大模型的表现。

When Skills Fail to Compose: Compatibility and Retention in Language Models
当技能无法组合时：语言模型中的兼容性与保留性
Skill acquisition alone does not establish a reusable skill library: task proficiency does not specify a compositional interface. Motivated by agents that acquire and refine skills, we study independently learned soft-prompt skills on frozen language models, with skill identities and execution order supplied. Across four digit-task bases and three record-editing models, mean composition accuracy in one call is at most 7.6%, compared with 88.2% to 100% over two calls that add computation and an explicit intermediate output. Neutral-insertion training improves robustness but leaves substantial interference, which block length and per-token norm alone cannot explain on two of four digit bases. Blocks of the same operation need not be interchangeable: after training next to frozen learned partners, a post hoc test on two Qwen bases shows that replacing those partners with independently learned blocks of the same operations lowers median composition accuracy from 98.0% and 95.3% to 12.7% and 0.5%, although every replacement scores at least 90% alone. Repair can improve a composition while impairing the original skill, and rehearsal improves retention while transfer remains limited on Qwen2.5-0.5B. Internal interventions constrain explanations without identifying a shared failure mechanism. These findings motivate evaluating compatibility, retention and transfer alongside acquisition when building skill libraries for self-evolving agents. Code is available at https://github.com/YanLin-Quinne/SkillCompatibility.
中文翻译
仅掌握技能并不能建立可复用的技能库：任务熟练度并未定义可组合的接口。受能够获取并优化技能的智能体启发，我们研究了在冻结的语言模型上独立学习的软提示技能，其中技能身份和执行顺序由外部提供。在四个数字任务基准和三个记录编辑模型上，单次调用的平均组合准确率最高仅为7.6%，而两次调用（增加计算量并显式输出中间结果）的准确率则达到88.2%至100%。中性插入训练提升了鲁棒性，但仍存在显著干扰，这种干扰无法仅通过块长度或每标记归一化来解释。相同操作的块并不一定可互换：在两个Qwen基线模型上，经过与冻结学习伙伴共同训练后，事后测试显示，用独立学习的同操作块替换这些伙伴，会使中位组合准确率从98.0%和95.3%降至12.7%和0.5%，尽管每个替换块单独测试得分均不低于90%。修复可能改善组合性能，但会损害原始技能；重练能提升保留性，但在Qwen2.5-0.5B上迁移能力仍有限。内部干预虽限制了解释范围，但未能识别出共享的失败机制。这些发现表明，在构建自演化智能体的技能库时，应同时评估兼容性、保留性和迁移性。代码已公开于 https://github.com/YanLin-Quinne/SkillCompatibility。

Learning from Unreliable Operator References: Claim-Level Rewards for Customer Support Agents
从不可靠的操作员引用中学习：面向客户支持智能体的声明级奖励
Even a correct operator reply can be an unreliable training target for a customer-support agent. About a quarter of the factual claims in these replies lacked full support in the agent's knowledge base and tool records. We treat operator replies as claims to verify rather than answers to imitate. Unverified claims guide revisions to the knowledge base and tools. Verified claims serve as coverage targets in a claim-level GRPO reward that scores factual precision against the evidence the agent retrieved for its answer. On an offline benchmark, 8.6% of the GRPO agent's text answers were flagged for hallucinations, compared with 13.6% for the supervised fine-tuning baseline. In a randomized A/B test against the existing procedural bot, the deployed system increased the share of conversations closed without a human operator from 63.3% to 80.8%.

IRTIQA: Evolving in English, Evaluated in Urdu -- Does Agent Self-Evolution Transfer from English to Urdu and Code-Switched Input?
IRTIQA：在英语中进化，在乌尔都语中评估——智能体自演化能否从英语迁移到乌尔都语及混合语言输入？
As LLM agents increasingly learn from their own experience, the tasks they learn from and are evaluated on are almost always in English. We ask whether self-evolution transfers from English to Urdu, and release IRTIQA, a benchmark of aligned English, Urdu-translated, and Urdu code-switched variants of DROP, SNIPS, AppWorld, and SkillsBench, built with a matrix-language code-switching pipeline and human verification. Using IRTIQA, we evaluate three self-evolving frameworks, Tool-R0 (self-play RL), AgentEvolver (experience-guided RL), and Autogenesis (protocol-level evolution), against their unevolved backbones. Self-evolution often raises capability: AgentEvolver lifts AppWorld goal completion from near zero to 20-36% across all conditions, and Autogenesis improves SkillsBench pass rate from 16.48% to 22.22%. But it rarely closes the language gap. Evolved systems remain stronger in English, by up to 0.26 DROP F1, the gap is largest on multi-step tasks, and code-switching behaves as a distinct condition rather than degraded translation. On a shared Gemma-4 31B backbone, the gap exists before evolution, and RL-based evolution can hurt Urdu reasoning more than English. Language parity must therefore be an explicit design target, as it does not emerge from self-improvement alone.
中文翻译
随着大语言模型智能体越来越多地从自身经验中学习，它们所学习和评估的任务几乎始终以英语为主。我们探讨自演化是否能从英语迁移到乌尔都语，并发布IRTIQA基准，该基准包含DROP、SNIPS、AppWorld和SkillsBench的对齐英文、乌尔都语翻译版及乌尔都语混杂版本，采用矩阵语言混杂管道构建并经人工验证。利用IRTIQA，我们评估三种自演化框架（Tool-R0，自对弈强化学习；AgentEvolver，经验引导强化学习；Autogenesis，协议级演化）与其未演化的基础模型之间的差异。自演化通常能提升能力：AgentEvolver使AppWorld的目标完成率从接近零提升至20%-36%；Autogenesis将SkillsBench通过率从16.48%提升至22.22%。但很少能弥合语言差距。演化后的系统在英语中仍更强，最多相差0.26的DROP F1，差距在多步任务中最大，且语言混杂表现为独立条件而非劣化翻译。在共享的Gemma-4 31B基础模型上，差距在演化前即存在，且基于RL的演化反而可能比英语更损害乌尔都语推理能力。因此，语言平等必须作为明确的设计目标，因为其不会仅通过自改进自然产生。

MINE: A Training-Free Defense against Indirect Prompt Injection via Mutual Information and Embedding Variance
MINE：通过互信息与嵌入方差实现无需训练的间接提示注入防御
Existing defenses against indirect prompt injection in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) are inherently reactive: prompt-engineering methods are often overridden by the injected instructions, while supervised detectors must be retrained for each new attack style. We propose a different paradigm: defend by maximizing relevance, not by detecting attacks. Our method, MINE, is training-free and attack-agnostic, and it also serves as a query-conditioned reranker. To evaluate defenses under realistic conditions, we introduce LC-Inj-SQuAD, a long-context benchmark with an average of 4,032 words per context. We evaluate on four datasets spanning short-context QA (Inj-SQuAD, Inj-TriviaQA), long-context QA (LC-Inj-SQuAD), and agent tool-call injection (InjecAgent). Across all four, MINE achieves the best accuracy--security trade-off among training-free defenses. It is also the only defense that stays usable against a fully adaptive attacker, generalizes to agent tool-call injection, and achieves the lowest inference latency on long contexts.
中文翻译
现有的针对检索增强生成（RAG）中间接提示注入的防御方法本质上是被动的：提示工程方法常被注入指令覆盖，而监督检测器必须为每种新攻击风格重新训练。我们提出一种新范式：通过最大化相关性进行防御，而非检测攻击。我们的方法MINE无需训练且对攻击风格无感，同时可作为查询条件重排序器。为在真实条件下评估防御效果，我们引入LC-Inj-SQuAD，一个平均上下文长度达4,032词的长上下文基准。我们在四个数据集上进行评估，涵盖短上下文问答（Inj-SQuAD、Inj-TriviaQA）、长上下文问答（LC-Inj-SQuAD）以及智能体工具调用注入（InjecAgent）。在所有四个数据集中，MINE在无需训练的防御方法中实现了最佳的准确率-安全性权衡。它也是唯一能在面对完全自适应攻击者时仍保持可用性、泛化至智能体工具调用注入，并在长上下文上实现最低推理延迟的防御方法。
👤 Truong Phan, Ngoc-Quan Pham, Dat Quoc Nguyen 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Lightweight Reasoning Transfer via Chain-of-Thought Vector Projection
通过思维链向量投影实现轻量级推理迁移
Transferring reasoning capabilities from large language models to smaller ones often requires extensive teacher supervision or costly student adaptation. We study projected cross-model Chain-of-Thought (CoT) Vector transfer, a two-stage approach that keeps both models frozen. First, we learn a task-level CoT Vector in the teacher using dataset-provided solution traces and final-answer supervision. We then train a lightweight linear projector to map the fixed vector into a smaller student's residual space. Student-side training uses only final-answer cross-entropy on a small subset of task training examples; it requires neither teacher outputs nor intermediate solution traces. We select the student injection layer and intervention scale on a labeled support set and report results on the disjoint remainder of each evaluation split. On GSM8K and MATH, the projected vector improves base students by 10.37-21.98 accuracy points across the evaluated teacher-student settings. On four code-generation benchmarks, the Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct student gains 3.23-4.67 pass@1 points, while the base Qwen2.5-7B student gains up to 9.65 points. These results show that a teacher-side reasoning vector can be reused across smaller students with different residual dimensions through projector-only adaptation.

Stable Gated DeltaNet-2: Understanding and Mitigating Recurrent State Instability
稳定门控DeltaNet-2：理解并缓解循环状态不稳定性
Linear attention offers an efficient solution for long-sequence modeling via linear complexity and fixed-size recurrent states. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2) introduces independent channel-wise control over erasure and writing, enhancing state-update expressiveness and in-context retrieval, yet it suffers from training failures during long-context training. We trace these failures to the asymmetry between reading and modification directions during erasure, which yields non-normal erase operators whose directional gains accumulate across tokens, triggering exponential state growth even without new writes. To resolve this, we propose \model{}, employing symmetric erasure to ensure non-expansive state propagation and decay-regulated residual writing to preserve writing flexibility. Theoretically, we establish an $O(\sqrt{T})$ upper bound on recurrent state growth while retaining chunkwise parallel computation. Experiments on ~1.6B parameter models show that \model{} achieves stable training alongside improved long-context retrieval. Compared to GDN-2, it boosts the 128K average RULER score by 6.80 points in the recurrent setting and average accuracy across nine downstream tasks by 0.62 points in the hybrid setting.

线性注意力通过线性复杂度和固定大小的循环状态，为长序列建模提供了一种高效解决方案。门控DeltaNet-2（GDN-2）引入了独立的通道级擦除与写入控制，增强了状态更新的表达能力与上下文检索性能，但在长上下文训练中存在训练失败问题。我们追溯其根源在于擦除过程中读取与修改方向的不对称性，导致非正规擦除算子的方向增益在token间累积，即使无新写入也会引发指数级状态增长。为解决此问题，我们提出 exttt{model}，采用对称擦除确保状态传播不扩张，并通过衰减调节的残差写入保留写入灵活性。理论上，我们建立了递归状态增长的$O( oot{}{T})$上界，同时保留分块并行计算能力。在约16亿参数模型上的实验表明， exttt{model}实现了稳定训练，并提升了长上下文检索性能。相较于GDN-2，在递归设置下，128K平均RULER得分提升6.80分；在混合设置下，九个下游任务的平均准确率提升0.62分。

Fine-Tuning Without Gradients? Closed-Form, LLM-Written Classifiers on a Frozen Encoder
无需梯度的微调？基于冻结编码器的闭式、LLM编写的分类器
Can a classifier computed in closed form on a frozen encoder match gradient fine-tuning? We present closed-form weight-space programming (WSP): a large language model (LLM), used as a design assistant, writes programs that read a frozen masked language model through cloze-style prompt templates at several layers, fit closed-form heads (shrinkage discriminant analysis, kernel ridge regression and the encoder's label-word prior) on labelled data, and select among a pool of candidate heads by cross-fitting. No gradient is computed and no encoder weight changes. We instantiate the framework on RoBERTa-large. On 11 development datasets with 512 to 8,192 training labels, WSP is ahead of full fine-tuning (FT) and LoRA at every label budget (by 0.3 to 0.6 points on average; the registered non-inferiority tests pass) and within 0.5 points of prompt-based fine-tuning (PFT); these datasets also shaped the recipe. With fewer labels its lead over FT and LoRA grows: 1.2 to 3.5 points with 64 and 128 labels, and 3.8 to 5.3 points in the few-shot setting of 16 labels per class, where WSP is again within half a point of PFT on the development datasets. On six held-out datasets, WSP stays ahead of LoRA and within half a point of FT on average, although with six datasets no registered test passes, and PFT is ahead, by 1.30 points with 8,192 labels. WSP never collapses and is deterministic, but it needs one encoder pass per template (17 here). We report all eight recipe versions, every decision that saw test results, and 27 ideas that did not help.

ChemDeduce: Benchmarking LLMs for Unknown Chemical Identification through Interactive Simulated Experiments
ChemDeduce：通过交互式模拟实验基准测试LLM在未知化学物质识别中的表现
Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in chemical knowledge and reasoning, yet it remains unclear whether they can solve problems whose answers must be discovered through experimentation. We investigate this question through Unknown Chemical Identification, where a model must actively design experiments, interpret observations, update hypotheses, and ultimately identify a hidden substance. We introduce ChemDeduce, an interactive evaluation framework for assessing LLMs through simulated experiments. ChemDeduce formulates chemical identification as a sequential decision process and provides a stateful simulated laboratory with explicit instrument and operational constraints, enabling scalable and reproducible evaluation of iterative experimental reasoning. We benchmark eight frontier LLMs on 200 chemicals, yielding 1600 model--target pairs and 4800 episodes over three independent repetitions, and evaluate identification accuracy, operational compliance, interaction efficiency, and trajectory-level reasoning quality. Despite generating seemingly plausible experimental plans, current models achieve an average identification accuracy ranging from 23.83% to 67.33%, revealing substantial limitations in experiment selection, evidence interpretation, and sequential hypothesis refinement.

Tells of a Lying Machine: Analyzing LLM-Generated Strategic Dialogue in Chinese Werewolf
说谎机器的线索：分析中文狼人杀中大模型生成的战略对话
Social-deduction games give models opposing objectives, but they also give roles different information and speaking obligations. We analyze 5,404 public utterances from 150 Chinese Werewolf games played by 12 models, comparing ordinary wolves with ordinary villagers so that special roles are excluded. Private knowledge and goals still differ, and no utterance is verified as true or false. After model and length adjustment, wolf utterances score higher on both certainty and uncertainty (coefficients +0.074 and +0.072). Emotional intensification has the largest rank-biserial effect (r = .145), but it depends on a single degree marker and correlates weakly with human-rated affect. Three annotators' ratings of 392 utterances show higher wolf scores on four behavioral dimensions, and five LLM judges reach 60.8%-70.2% accuracy on a shared evaluation set. In a generation probe that prompts six models with a wolf identity but a villager speaking goal under fixed public context, certainty (+0.164) and emotional intensification (+0.095) increase. These findings describe role-associated language rather than verified lying, and they argue for explicit comparison groups and lexicon sensitivity checks in game-based deception evaluation. Data and code are available at https://github.com/justinchoi929/Tells-of-a-Lying-Machine

NORMCRAFT: Cultural Norm Generation for Context-Aware Language Model Alignment

Cultural alignment requires large language models (LLMs) to infer often-implicit social norms from a scenario and its cultural context. Existing approaches typically adapt individual models or supply external cultural knowledge, while learning norm inference independently of downstream response generation remains underexplored. We introduce NORMCRAFT, a modular framework that decouples norm inference from response generation. Through norm-centric multi-view supervision, we train an LLM as a norm generator to recover shared norms from prospective, adherence, and violation scenarios without downstream response labels. The generated norms guide a separate, frozen LLM (the responder). With Qwen3.5-4B as the responder, NORMCRAFT outperforms prompting and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines on four of five downstream metrics. On SafeWorld, its responses reflect the reference norms more often (Coverage) and more precisely (Faithfulness), exceeding Responder SFT by 18.0 and 14.1 percentage points, respectively. Direct norm evaluation also shows higher norm quality than the base and few-shot generators. The same trained generator improves cultural alignment on SafeWorld across fourteen frozen LLMs spanning five families and 0.8B to 27B parameters, with the largest gains for the smallest models.

Sports Agent: A Self-Evolving System for Conversational Data Analytics
体育智能体：一种用于对话数据分析的自演化系统
Conversational sports analytics requires accurate, evidence-based responses with low latency. Changes in query distributions, data resources, and model behavior can degrade static agent configurations, motivating controlled evolution guided by runtime evidence. We present Sports Agent, a self-evolving framework integrating an Agentic SQL Model for structured-data reasoning, a harness for planning and tool orchestration, and a unified Feedback Engine connecting online execution with offline evolution. The engine assesses responses and execution trajectories, verifies factual claims, diagnoses failures, and generates evolution signals. Model evolution combines SQL-to-Question Scaling for coverage expansion with Graph-Guided Scaling for error-targeted synthesis. Generated questions undergo trajectory distillation and quality filtering for iterative supervised fine-tuning, followed by a separate Post-RL stage. Harness evolution translates recurrent failures into bounded prompt and skill updates, validated through independent audits, execution replay, and regression testing. Performance-tolerance checks guard against unacceptable degradation during simplification. The resulting Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-based model outperforms Qwen3.8-Max on sports question answering and analysis while achieving approximately three times the inference efficiency. Across 30 harness-evolution rounds, the aggregate performance score improves by 10.8 points. In a live sports analytics product, the evolved system improves factual correctness by 10.5 points and average response length, a proxy for answer richness, by 158.1%, while reducing average latency by 41.3% relative to the baseline.

Rethinking Sequential Locate-Then-Edit: Optimality and Stability
重新思考顺序定位-编辑：最优性与稳定性
The Locate-Then-Edit framework enables efficient knowledge updates in large language models without requiring full retraining. Recent research has increasingly focused on sequential editing, where knowledge arrives continuously and the model is updated incrementally. However, existing sequential methods commonly suffer from catastrophic forgetting and model collapse after only a few hundred edits. In contrast, batch editing - which jointly optimizes all edits and yields the optimal solution under a specified editing objective - can support tens of thousands of edits with high edit efficacy while preserving the model's general capabilities, resulting in a substantial performance gap between the two editing paradigms. In this paper, we demonstrate that this gap can be closed via a batch-equivalent sequential editing approach. Moreover, we investigate the underlying mechanism of model degradation in existing sequential methods by analyzing the convergence behavior of weight perturbations as edits accumulate. Our analysis reveals that current methods typically exhibit linear or logarithmic growth in perturbation, whereas the proposed batch-equivalent method achieves time-invariant growth, thereby theoretically guaranteeing long-term stability and preserving the integrity of pretrained knowledge throughout massive-scale sequential editing.
中文翻译
定位-编辑框架可在无需完整重训练的情况下实现大语言模型的知识高效更新。近期研究越来越多关注顺序编辑，即知识持续到来并逐步更新模型。然而，现有顺序方法通常在数百次编辑后就出现灾难性遗忘和模型崩溃。相比之下，批量编辑——联合优化所有编辑并达到指定编辑目标下的最优解——可支持数万次编辑，保持高编辑有效性并保留模型的通用能力，导致两种编辑范式间存在显著性能差距。本文证明，通过一种等效于批量编辑的顺序编辑方法，这一差距可以被弥合。此外，我们通过分析权重扰动随编辑累积的收敛行为，探究了现有顺序方法中模型退化的根本机制。分析表明，当前方法通常表现出线性或对数增长的扰动，而所提出的等效批量方法实现了时间不变的增长，从而理论上保证了长期稳定性，并在大规模顺序编辑过程中始终保护预训练知识的完整性。
👤 Bingqing Liu, Wei Liu, Zhiying Deng, Jun Wang, Yuhua Li et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33670]

Rethinking Knowledge Distillation for Diffusion Language Models
重新思考扩散语言模型的知识蒸馏
Knowledge distillation has recently proven highly effective for improving autoregressive models, but its applicability to DLMs remains poorly understood. We present the first systematic study of KD for DLMs and uncover two non-intuitive failures of classical forward-KL (FKL) distillation: (i) student perplexity does not improve monotonically with more teacher-generated data, and (ii) on the same teacher-generated corpus, FKL distillation underperforms simply training the student with the original DLM denoising objective. To address them, we propose a unified, data-free distillation framework for masked DLMs. First, the framework runs an off-policy distillation phase that absorbs general knowledge from a static set of teacher-generated data. Then, the framework employs on-policy distillation which consists of three techniques: a trajectory loss along the student's own reverse process, confidence reweighting that down-weights tokens where the teacher is unsure, and a trust-region anchor that stabilizes the moving target. On MDLM, the resulting student attains a GPT-2 perplexity of $24.75$, surpassing the teacher's $27.04$; on Dream-7B distillation to a 93M student model, the framework reduces Qwen-2.5 perplexity from the FKL-distilled baseline of $25.23$ to $19.83$ while preserving performance on zero-shot general capability benchmarks.
中文翻译
知识蒸馏在提升自回归模型方面已被证明非常有效，但其在扩散语言模型（DLMs）中的适用性仍不清楚。我们首次对DLMs的知识蒸馏进行了系统性研究，并揭示了经典前向KL（FKL）蒸馏的两个非直观缺陷：(i) 学生模型的困惑度不会随着教师生成数据量增加而单调改善；(ii) 在同一教师生成语料库上，FKL蒸馏的表现不如直接使用原始DLM去噪目标训练学生模型。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种针对掩码DLM的统一、无数据蒸馏框架。首先，框架执行离策略蒸馏阶段，从静态教师生成数据集中吸收通用知识；然后，采用在策略蒸馏，包含三项技术：沿学生自身反向过程的轨迹损失、置信度重加权（降低教师不确定时的token权重）以及信任区域锚点（稳定移动目标）。在MDLM上，所得学生模型达到GPT-2困惑度24.75，超过教师的27.04；在Dream-7B蒸馏至93M学生模型时，框架将Qwen-2.5的困惑度从FKL蒸馏基线的25.23降至19.83，同时保持零样本通用能力基准上的性能。

Sparse-to-Sparse Feature Learning for Cross-Layer Analysis and Steering
跨层分析与引导中的稀疏到稀疏特征学习
Understanding how models internally represent information is a central challenge in interpretation and control. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose dense hidden representations into sparse features, and cross-layer methods capture structure spanning multiple layers. However, existing objectives do not explicitly train the sparse code at one layer to predict the sparse code at the next layer. We propose sparse-to-sparse feature learning (S2S), where sparse features at each intermediate layer are trained to predict those of the immediately following layer, with the final layer anchored by an output-distribution loss and optimization proceeding sequentially from output to input. Experiments across eight language models show stronger adjacent-layer correspondence than matched sparse-feature baselines. We evaluate feature-based interventions at matched output-distribution shift, separating steering-induced effects from sparse-pathway reconstruction effects. Causal ablation provides complementary evidence beyond correspondence metrics.
中文翻译
理解模型内部如何表示信息是解释与控制的核心挑战。稀疏自编码器（SAEs）将密集的隐藏表示分解为稀疏特征，而跨层方法则捕捉跨越多层的结构。然而，现有目标并未显式训练某一层的稀疏代码以预测下一层的稀疏代码。我们提出稀疏到稀疏特征学习（S2S），其中每个中间层的稀疏特征被训练以预测紧随其后的层的稀疏特征，最终层通过输出分布损失锚定，优化从输出向输入顺序进行。在八种语言模型上的实验表明，S2S相比匹配的稀疏特征基线具有更强的相邻层对应性。我们在匹配的输出分布偏移下评估基于特征的干预，将引导引起的效应与稀疏路径重建效应分离。因果消融提供了对应性度量之外的补充证据。

Failure Broadens, Success Sharpens: Continual Adaptation of Tool-Use Agents Across Diverse Tasks with Dual-Polarity Memory
失败拓宽，成功精炼：通过双极性记忆实现跨多样化任务的工具使用智能体持续适应
Large language model agents can self-improve over time by utilizing procedural memory derived from prior task trajectories, yet the distinct utilities of memories derived from local successes and failures remain underexplored. This work studies this question in a realistic setting with heterogeneous streams of multi-domain tool-use tasks and various environment constraints common in real-world scenarios. We develop \emph{Dual-Polarity Memory with Periodic Maintenance} (DualPM), a simple, retrieval-free memory workflow characterized by (1) customizable reflection and procedural memory generation from both local successes and failures within each trajectory, and (2) periodic memory consolidation over long task streams under a given memory capacity, preventing memory explosion. Online prequential evaluation with fine-grained metrics reveals that failure memory more strongly broadens coverage, success memory better streamlines execution, while combining both polarities effectively integrates both benefits. Further studies demonstrate that DualPM enables cross-domain memory generalization and continual memory adaptation, while external evaluation on EvoMemBench reveals the portability and effectiveness of DualPM as a general long-term procedural memory method.
中文翻译
大型语言模型代理可通过利用来自先前任务轨迹的程序化记忆实现自我改进，但源自局部成功与失败的记忆的差异化效用仍缺乏深入探索。本研究在包含异构多领域工具使用任务及真实场景中常见的各种环境约束的现实设置下探讨该问题。我们开发了 extit{周期维护的双极性记忆}（DualPM），一种简单且无需检索的记忆工作流，其特点为：(1) 可定制地从每条轨迹中的局部成功与失败中生成反思和程序化记忆；(2) 在给定记忆容量下对长期任务流进行周期性记忆整合，防止记忆爆炸。通过细粒度指标的在线预序评估显示，失败记忆更显著地拓宽覆盖范围，成功记忆则更有效地优化执行流程，而结合两种极性可有效整合两者优势。进一步研究表明，DualPM支持跨领域记忆泛化与持续记忆适应；外部在EvoMemBench上的评估揭示了DualPM作为通用长期程序化记忆方法的可移植性与有效性。

Using AI to Simulate the Behaviour of European Populations
利用AI模拟欧洲人口的行为
Language models can stand in for survey respondents, but leading approaches build their simulated people from interviews or individual records. We show that a model can learn how European population groups respond from published statistics, without interviews and without training on individual records. We fine-tune an open-weight language model on more than 12 million published group-level answer distributions from about 40 European countries, setting it up to predict each group's departure from a reference distribution. On opinion surveys from its training families it calls the direction of group differences right 84% of the time, ahead of a demographic statistical model (77%, paired test) and level with Claude Opus 5 and 5.5, which it also ties on accuracy; the frontier models are more accurate on new questions. On 278 held-out questions, almost all from the survey families seen in training, its error is 12% lower than that statistical model. It runs on a single 32 GB GPU or a high-memory laptop, and in a test with an earlier model, an evidence gate calibrated on half of a survey's questions fell back to the reference where the model's adjustments did not help. Coupled to a transport simulation of a planned metro closure, with a conventional elasticity as the reference, it adds a testable age gradient to the response, which we freeze for later scoring.
中文翻译
语言模型可以充当调查受访者的替代品，但主流方法依赖访谈或个体记录构建模拟人群。我们展示了一个模型如何仅从已发布的统计数据中学习欧洲群体的响应模式，无需访谈也无需在个体记录上训练。我们将一个开源权重语言模型在来自约40个欧洲国家的超过1200万条发布过的群体级回答分布上进行微调，使其能够预测每个群体相对于参考分布的偏离情况。在训练家族的民意调查上，它正确判断群体差异方向的比例达到84%，优于人口统计学模型（77%，配对检验），并与Claude Opus 5和5.5持平，准确率也相当；前沿模型在新问题上表现更优。在278个保留问题上，其误差比统计模型低12%。该模型可在单块32GB GPU或高内存笔记本上运行；在一项测试中，一个基于一半调查问题校准的证据门在早期模型测试中回归至参考值，说明模型调整未带来帮助。结合一个计划中的地铁关闭交通模拟，以传统弹性为参考，它增加了可检验的年龄梯度响应，我们将其冻结用于后续评分。

Learning from Recurrence: Internalizing Depth-Induced Preferences in Looped Language Models
从循环中学习：在循环语言模型中内化深度诱导的偏好
A looped language model repeatedly applies the same block to its hidden state before predicting each token. The number of passes is its recurrent depth. Increasing this depth gives the model more computation per token with the same parameters. Can the shared block learn from the predictions it makes at greater depth? Following prior work, we retrofit pretrained OLMo-2-1B with a shared recurrent block and continue mid-training over varying recurrent depths. In the four-pass model, additional passes mainly redistribute probability among tokens that already rank highly after one pass. We use the continuations generated at greater depth to train only the shared block through a single pass, without reference answers or correctness filtering. We call this transfer preference write-back. Write-back further improves one-pass and multi-pass accuracy on most tasks. At their target depths, the updated models outperform a non-recurrent control matched for mid-training data and token budget on all six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with a mean relative gain in accuracy of up to 46.2% across tasks. At four-pass inference, one-pass training outperforms full-depth training on the same targets in the model with input injection. This advantage disappears in the matched model without injection. On GSM8K, using the updated block only on passes two through four recovers nearly the entire four-pass gain, showing that an update learned in one pass can improve later applications of the same block.
中文翻译
循环语言模型通过反复应用相同的模块作用于隐藏状态来预测每个词元，其循环深度即为迭代次数。增加深度可在参数不变的情况下提升每词元的计算量。共享模块能否从高深度下的预测中学习？受先前工作启发，我们对预训练的OLMo-2-1B模型引入共享循环模块，并在不同循环深度下继续训练。在四次迭代模型中，额外的迭代主要在一次迭代后已排名靠前的词元间重新分配概率。我们利用高深度生成的延续文本，仅通过一次迭代训练共享模块，无需参考答案或正确性过滤。这种方法称为转移偏好写回。写回进一步提升了单次和多次迭代在多数任务上的准确率。在目标深度下，更新后的模型在所有六个数学推理基准测试上均优于相同中期训练数据和词元预算的非循环对照组，平均准确率相对提升高达46.2%。在四次迭代推理时，一次训练在输入注入模型中表现优于全深度训练，但在无注入的匹配模型中该优势消失。在GSM8K任务中，仅在第二至第四次迭代使用更新后的模块即可恢复几乎全部四次迭代的增益，表明一次训练中学习到的更新可改善同一模块后续的应用效果。
👤 Hui Chen, Joshua Elsdon, Can Goksen 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Lost in Translation, Not in Confidence: Benchmarking and Calibrating Multilingual Decision Models
翻译迷失，信心未失：多语言决策模型的基准测试与校准
Decision models are language models fine-tuned to return a probability for each option of a typed query, which routers and guardrails threshold directly. They are built on multilingual bases but ranked almost entirely in English. We evaluate 25 top-ranked open-weight decision models on 17 tasks in 14 languages, comparing each language with English on parallel items. Every model loses accuracy in every language, from about 2 points in high-resource languages to 8 in Tamil, while confidence falls only half as much: a threshold set for 95\% accuracy in English yields 90% in Tamil. The size of the loss is set by the language and the base model rather than by leaderboard rank. In 81 controlled fine-tuning runs, task skill learned in English transfers to other languages and a few thousand target-language items narrow large losses, but neither more data nor full-parameter tuning closes them. Finally, the same test items are answered more accurately in all 23 benchmark--language pairs we compare when an LLM rather than a person translated them, so LLM-translated test sets understate the gap.
中文翻译
决策模型是经过微调的语言模型，可为类型化查询的每个选项返回概率，路由器和防护机制直接使用这些概率进行阈值判断。这些模型基于多语言基础，但排名几乎完全以英语为主。我们评估了25个排名靠前的开源权重决策模型在14种语言中的17项任务表现，并将每种语言与英语在平行任务上进行对比。每个模型在所有语言中均出现准确率下降，从高资源语言约2个百分点到泰米尔语的8个百分点不等，而置信度仅下降一半：针对英语设定95%准确率的阈值在泰米尔语中仍可达到90%。损失大小由语言和基础模型决定，而非排行榜排名。在81次受控微调实验中，英语中学到的任务技能可迁移到其他语言，少量目标语言数据可缩小大范围损失，但无论增加数据量还是全参数微调都无法彻底消除。最后，在所有23个基准-语言对中，当测试题由大语言模型而非人类翻译时，回答准确率更高，说明由LLM翻译的测试集低估了实际差距。
👤 Mingzhe Du, Thong Thanh Nguyen, Nguyen Tran Cong Duy, See-Kiong Ng, Luu Anh Tuan 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33197]

Extraneous Cognitive Load in Large Language Models
大型语言模型中的额外认知负荷
Cognitive Load Theory (CLT) shows that human problem-solving is affected by both intrinsic load, arising from task difficulty, and extraneous load, arising from how task information is presented. Large language models (LLMs) show analogous effects: presentation alone can affect model performance, but these effects have not yet been systematically isolated from task difficulty. To address this gap, we introduce CogLoadBench, a controlled benchmark that varies five CLT-inspired presentation factors across three reasoning task families. Across models, we find that higher extraneous load conditions often reduce accuracy by either adding competition or complicating the reasoning path. To support diagnosis and mitigation of load-related effects, we propose the Extraneous Load Score (ELS), a prompt-time metric computed from activations that estimates a model's extraneous load, and show that it generalizes across reasoning task types and load levels. We further show that shifting model representations toward lower-load directions at inference time can improve average model performance without changing prompt content.
中文翻译
认知负荷理论（CLT）表明，人类解决问题的表现受到内在负荷（源于任务难度）和额外负荷（源于任务信息呈现方式）的影响。大型语言模型（LLMs）表现出类似效应：仅信息呈现方式就会影响模型性能，但这些影响尚未与任务难度系统地区分开来。为填补这一空白，我们提出了CogLoadBench，一个受控基准测试，通过在三个推理任务类别中变化五个受CLT启发的呈现因素。在多个模型中，我们发现更高的额外负荷条件通常会降低准确率，原因在于增加了竞争或复杂化了推理路径。为支持对负荷相关效应的诊断与缓解，我们提出了一种额外负荷评分（ELS），这是一种在提示阶段计算的激活指标，可估计模型的额外负荷，并证明其在不同推理任务类型和负荷水平间具有泛化能力。我们进一步表明，在推理时将模型表示向低负荷方向调整，可在不改变提示内容的前提下提升平均模型性能。
👤 Arushi Gupta, Sheng Liu, James Zou 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv

Verified experience improves materials prediction with large language models
经验证据提升大语言模型在材料预测中的表现
Materials discovery is an iterative loop: experiments and calculations verify predictions, and each outcome can inform the next. Yet most predictors are specialized single-task models fixed after training. Large language models have recently emerged as predictors across heterogeneous tasks but generally underperform specialized models, and retrieval-augmented variants discard their own outcomes. Benchmarks also score queries in isolation, leaving improvement from outcomes untested. Here we construct MatMemBench, a sequential benchmark of 22 materials tasks that reveals outcomes only after prediction and tests online adaptation, memory retention and cross-task transfer. We present MatRecall, a framework that lets large language models store verified predictions, with evidence, outcome and error, as episodes and recall them without retraining. MatRecall improved median performance by 62% over a memory-free model, exceeded specialized models on 11 tasks, remained useful after feedback stopped and transferred between related tasks. Verified experience could become a reusable resource for self-improving discovery loops.
中文翻译
材料发现是一个迭代循环：实验和计算验证预测，每次结果均可指导下一次预测。然而，大多数预测模型是训练完成后固定的单任务专用模型。大语言模型最近作为跨异构任务的预测器出现，但通常表现不如专用模型，且检索增强型变体会丢弃自身的预测结果。基准测试也孤立地评估查询，未测试结果带来的改进。本文构建了MatMemBench，一个包含22个材料任务的序列化基准测试，仅在预测后揭示结果，用于测试在线适应、记忆保持和跨任务迁移。我们提出MatRecall框架，使大语言模型能够以“事件”形式存储经验证的预测结果，包括证据、结果和误差，并在无需重新训练的情况下进行回忆。MatRecall使中位性能相比无记忆模型提升了62%，在11个任务上超越了专用模型，即使反馈停止后仍具实用性，并能在相关任务间实现迁移。经验证据可能成为自改进发现循环中的可复用资源。
👤 Yiming Zhang, Koji Tsuda 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34923]
agentic-rl
Mixture-of-Loops Transformer
环路混合Transformer
Looped Transformers boost expressivity by reusing shared layers across loops, yet at the cost of efficiency. Pushing their expressivity-efficiency frontier requires rethinking the core mechanism connecting adjacent loops: What should be passed as a Transformer loops? We introduce Mixture-of-Loops Transformer (MoLT), whose Mixture-of-Loops Attention (MoLA) uses keys and values (KV) as memory for selective reuse across depths and loops. Under loop residual, MoLT-R retains the residual stream and reads local KV across loops for expressivity. Under loop KV, MoLT-KV instead restarts from embeddings and reads current-loop local KV with preceding-loop memory KV at earlier positions for efficiency. MoLT-KV eliminates same-position cross-loop dependence and, paired with loop forcing for parallel training, unlocks single-traversal Mixture-of-Loops (MoL) decoding beyond the training loop horizon. To balance efficiency and quality, we introduce U-shaped MoLT (U-MoLT), which separates memory construction from prediction readout and skips unused decoder traversals without changing predictions. In 400B-token pretraining, U-MoLT outperforms OLMo3 across 700M/1.5B dense and 3B MoE models, achieving 1.7x validation cross-entropy (CE) token efficiency at 700M while improving 3B MoE 10-domain perplexity by 0.75 and 10-task accuracy by 2.1 percentage points. In 100B-token continued pretraining of Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base, our U-MoLT adaptation lowers CE on all 11 domains over the matched baseline with 1.4x mean token efficiency. At inference, U-MoLT with MoL-Hybrid-2 matches non-looped OLMo3 latency at 996.6 vs. 1,000.6 ms on S-size (~113M) models. Code is available at https://github.com/hustvl/MoLT
中文翻译
环路Transformer通过在环路间复用共享层来提升表达能力，但以效率为代价。为了突破其表达能力与效率的边界，需要重新思考相邻环路之间的核心连接机制：应如何传递Transformer环路信息？我们提出环路混合Transformer（MoLT），其环路混合注意力（MoLA）使用键值（KV）作为记忆，在深度和环路间实现选择性复用。在环路残差设置下，MoLT-R保留残差流，并跨环路读取局部KV以增强表达能力；在环路KV设置下，MoLT-KV则从嵌入重新开始，读取当前环路的局部KV以及前一环路的记忆KV以提高效率。MoLT-KV消除了相同位置的跨环路依赖，并结合环路强制训练策略，实现了超越训练环路范围的单次遍历环路混合（MoL）解码。为平衡效率与质量，我们引入U型MoLT（U-MoLT），将记忆构建与预测读取分离，并跳过未使用的解码遍历而不改变预测结果。在4000亿token预训练中，U-MoLT在7亿/15亿稠密模型和30亿MoE模型上均优于OLMo3，7亿模型实现1.7倍验证交叉熵（CE）的token效率提升，同时使30亿MoE模型在10个领域困惑度降低0.75，10项任务准确率提升2.1个百分点。在Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base的1000亿token持续预训练中，U-MoLT适应版本在所有11个领域上的CE均低于匹配基线，平均token效率提升1.4倍。推理阶段，U-MoLT配合MoL-Hybrid-2在S尺寸（约1130万参数）模型上达到996.6毫秒延迟，接近非环路OLMo3的1000.6毫秒。代码已开源：https://github.com/hustvl/MoLT

When Every Rollout Fails: Splicing Learner-Conditioned Options into Long-Horizon Agent RL
当每次轨迹都失败时：将学习者条件化选项拼接进长时程智能体强化学习
Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often yields all-fail groups on long-horizon agent tasks: every sampled trajectory receives zero reward, leaving group-relative methods without a reward-based policy gradient. A common remedy supplies complete solutions from a stronger model, but full trajectories are expensive and expose the learner to long off-policy histories. We introduce SPLICE, which constructs local training targets from failed current-policy trajectories online. For each all-fail group, a proposal model selects a splice point in a current-policy trajectory and generates several bounded options from that point. We estimate each option's value using repeated current-policy continuations, convert positive estimates into a return-weighted local target, and fit the current policy on the options. We evaluate SPLICE across two policy families on two long-horizon agent tasks, Software Engineering with SWE-bench Verified and Deep Research with BrowseComp-Plus. SPLICE outperforms GRPO and full-trajectory guidance baselines in all four settings, improving over GRPO by 2.8--9.8 percentage points. On initially failed SWE tasks, it raises GRPO's graduation rate by 10.8 percentage points. Further experiments show that SPLICE improves performance while reducing costly proposal-model computation.
中文翻译
基于群体的强化学习在长时程智能体任务中常出现全失败群体：所有采样轨迹均获得零奖励，导致基于奖励的群体相对方法无法生成策略梯度。常见解决方案是从更强模型中提供完整解法，但完整轨迹成本高且使学习者暴露于长期离策略历史中。我们提出SPLICE，通过在线构建失败当前策略轨迹的局部训练目标。对于每个全失败群体，提议模型在当前策略轨迹中选择一个拼接点，并从该点生成多个有界选项。我们通过重复当前策略延续来估计每个选项的价值，将正向估计转换为返回加权的局部目标，并在这些选项上拟合当前策略。我们在两个策略族和两个长时程智能体任务（软件工程中的SWE-bench Verified和深度研究中的BrowseComp-Plus）上评估SPLICE，结果表明其在全部四个设置中均优于GRPO和完整轨迹引导基线，性能提升2.8至9.8个百分点。在初始失败的SWE任务上，其将GRPO的毕业率提升10.8个百分点。进一步实验显示，SPLICE在提升性能的同时减少了昂贵的提议模型计算量。

When the Proxy Is Wrong: Mismatch-Corrected Bandits for Adaptive Data Sampling
当代理错误时：用于自适应数据采样的错配校正贝叶斯模型
Data allocation methods often use a small proxy model to select training data for a larger target model. However, this proxy can rank data differently from the target, leading to suboptimal training performance. To address this problem, we model the mismatch between the proxy and target models and incorporate it into the allocation decision. To do so, the problem is cast as a multi-fidelity bandit, where each arm is a data intervention. The proxy reward is observed every round, whereas the target reward is revealed only on rare audits of the same update. These paired observations identify arm-wise mismatches without assuming a shared offset or preservation of rankings. Mismatch-corrected bandits correct the proxy with paired residuals, audit only uncertain decisions, and pace the audit budget over training. From a theoretical viewpoint, we prove a regret decomposition and lower bounds under stationarity. These bounds show that proxy-only allocation can incur linear regret and that a minimum number of paired audits is necessary. Under abrupt changes, using the audit budget early can also incur linear regret. A sliding-window policy that spreads audits over training achieves regret of order $\sqrt{Γ_T T\log T}$ where $Γ_T$ denotes the number of change points. Experimentally, we test the approach in a closed-loop proof of concept on CivilComments-WILDS, where BERT-Small guides the training of BERT-Large. Mismatch correction improves worst-group accuracy over a target-only policy with the same target-model budget. Spreading audits over training improves worst-group accuracy by 6.3 to 10.5 percentage points at the same audit count.
中文翻译
数据分配方法通常使用小型代理模型来为大型目标模型选择训练数据。然而，该代理可能与目标模型对数据的排序不同，导致训练性能不佳。为解决此问题，我们建模了代理与目标模型之间的错配，并将其纳入分配决策中。为此，将问题转化为多保真度贝叶斯模型，其中每个臂代表一种数据干预。每轮可观察到代理奖励，而目标奖励仅在稀疏的相同更新审计中揭示。成对观测识别出各臂的错配，无需假设共享偏移或排名保持不变。错配校正贝叶斯模型通过成对残差校正代理，仅审计不确定决策，并在训练过程中合理分配审计预算。从理论角度看，我们证明了平稳条件下的损失分解和下界。这些边界表明，仅使用代理的分配可能导致线性损失，且需要最少数量的成对审计。在突变情况下，过早使用审计预算也可能导致线性损失。采用滑动窗口策略将审计分散在整个训练过程，可实现阶为 $\sqrt{Γ_T T\log T}$ 的损失，其中 $Γ_T$ 表示变化点的数量。实验上，我们在 CivilComments-WILDS 上进行闭环概念验证，BERT-Small 指导 BERT-Large 的训练。错配校正相较于仅使用目标模型的策略，在相同目标模型预算下提升了最差群体的准确率。将审计分散在整个训练过程中，在相同审计次数下使最差群体准确率提升 6.3 至 10.5 个百分点。
👤 Amine Saadi, Elissa Mhanna, Raphaël Féraud, Gwénolé Lecorvé 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

vSFT: Learning to Verbally Tune Agents at Test Time
vSFT：测试时通过语言调优智能体的方法
Large language model agents are increasingly improved via agentic reinforcement learning, which can be on-policy but suffers from expensive rollouts and sparse rewards, limiting its application at test time. In this paper, we present a verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning (vSFT) framework that enables the conjunction of dense feedback in natural language and weight updates at test time. Specifically, our vSFT forms a closed loop between an agent and a supervisor via a hypernetwork, in which the supervisor generates verbal supervision from the agent's failed trajectories, and the hypernetwork converts that supervision into low-rank weight updates to tune the agent. We validate that this test-time in-parameter evolving process outperforms both its in-context counterparts and prevalent reinforcement learning methods. In particular, our vSFT using Qwen3-4B outperforms GRPO and Reflexion by 8.7% and 13.9% in success rate on ALFWorld, and by 4.8% and 18.2% on WebShop, respectively. We believe that learning how to adapt a model, rather than an adapted model, offers a promising path towards more capable agents that improve themselves.

Strong Starts, Simple Recipes: Unified Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation from Multimodal Specialists
强起点，简单配方：从多模态专家统一进行在线策略蒸馏
Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates separately trained domain specialists into a single unified model. Existing methods focus on refining the distillation procedure while initializing the student from a base model or a multi-domain SFT checkpoint, which leaves capability integration largely to distillation itself. Instead, we treat the starting point as a design variable and propose Unified MOPD (Uni-MOPD), which pairs a strong start with a simple recipe: a training-free uniform merge of specialists from the same base model initializes the student, and short MOPD with uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL refines it. Across five multimodal domains and 19 benchmarks, our study establishes three findings. (1) The starting point matters more than subsequent training choices: before any training, the uniform merge already matches the score that the base model reaches after 200 MOPD updates, and after short distillation it matches higher-scoring, search-based starts. (2) Short MOPD recovers most of the capabilities the merge leaves unintegrated; it improves every domain, with the largest gains where the domain teacher still leads. (3) From a strong start, uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL match or exceed every evaluated sampling strategy and divergence, without schedules, per-token weighting, or truncation hyperparameters. Uni-MOPD outperforms Open-MOPD at the same training budget while training 3.3 times faster than its released code. We will release the training framework, specialist checkpoints, and unified student.
中文翻译
多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）将分别训练的领域专家整合到一个统一模型中。现有方法专注于优化蒸馏过程，而学生模型的初始化通常基于基础模型或跨领域微调检查点，导致能力融合主要依赖蒸馏本身。本文将起始点视为设计变量，提出统一MOPD（Uni-MOPD），采用强起点与简单配方相结合的方式：通过无需训练的统一合并方式，将同一基础模型下的专家合并初始化学生模型，并通过短周期MOPD结合均匀域采样和全词汇量反向KL散度进行精炼。在五个多模态领域和19个基准测试中，研究得出三个发现：(1) 起始点比后续训练选择更重要：在任何训练前，统一合并已达到基础模型经过200次MOPD更新后的得分，经短周期蒸馏后甚至超过搜索得到的高分起始点；(2) 短周期MOPD恢复了合并过程中未整合的大部分能力；在所有领域均有提升，尤其在领域教师仍占优时增益最大；(3) 从强起点出发，均匀域采样与全词汇量反向KL散度在不使用调度、逐token加权或截断超参数的情况下，表现匹配或超越所有评估过的采样策略与发散度。Uni-MOPD在相同训练预算下优于Open-MOPD，且训练速度提升3.3倍。我们将发布训练框架、专家检查点及统一学生模型。

Single-Database Rewards Are Hackable: Counterfactual Execution for Trustworthy Text-to-SQL RL
单数据库奖励可被攻破：用于可信文本到SQL强化学习的反事实执行
Reinforcement learning (RL) for text-to-SQL agents pays a query when its result matches the reference on one database. However, a wrong query can match by luck when the data lack the rows that expose its bug. We study a counterfactual-database (CF) reward that also runs the final query on a few hidden, code-generated copies of the database and pays only if all results match (strict form) or by the share matched (mean form). On 1,302 synthetic audit questions, the strict form lowers the share of buggy queries paid in full from 29.5% to 16.8% and of reward hacks from 54.1% to 1.9%, rejecting 0 of 2,847 correct queries; copies from a family-blind generator do about as well. In a controlled GRPO comparison on a 3.35B model with three seeds per arm, training on the mean form did not improve in-domain accuracy (8.4% vs 11.7%), because the two rewards disagreed on only 5-7% of rollouts; on Spider it gained 1.6 points. One seed reverses both signs, so single-seed reward comparisons mislead. Code: https://github.com/RishiiGamer2201/counterfactual-sql-rewards ; data, models, demo: https://huggingface.co/counterfactual-sql
中文翻译
针对文本到SQL智能体的强化学习（RL）在查询结果与参考答案匹配时支付奖励，但错误查询可能因数据缺乏暴露其缺陷的行而偶然匹配。我们研究了一种反事实数据库（CF）奖励机制，该机制在几个隐藏的、由代码生成的数据库副本上运行最终查询，并仅在所有结果一致（严格形式）或匹配比例一致（均值形式）时才支付奖励。在1,302个合成审计问题上，严格形式将完全支付的错误查询比例从29.5%降至16.8%，奖励欺骗比例从54.1%降至1.9%，同时拒绝了0个正确查询；来自无家族感知生成器的副本效果相当。在3.35B模型上进行受控GRPO对比实验（每臂三个种子），使用均值形式训练并未提升域内准确率（8.4% vs 11.7%），因为两种奖励在仅5-7%的轨迹上存在分歧；但在Spider数据集上提升了1.6分。一个种子反转了两个指标的方向，表明单种子奖励比较具有误导性。代码：https://github.com/RishiiGamer2201/counterfactual-sql-rewards；数据、模型、演示：https://huggingface.co/counterfactual-sql

Improving Tail-Augmented Speculative Drafting with Hidden State Mixing and Gradient Detach
通过隐藏状态混合与梯度分离改进尾部增强的推测性草稿生成
Multi-token prediction (MTP) modules and EAGLE-style drafters support efficient speculative decoding by reusing target-model hidden states. Their autoregressive use, however, introduces a training-inference mismatch: training supplies sequences of hidden states from the backbone or a single MTP step, whereas inference attends to a target-model prefix followed by a growing tail of recurrently generated MTP states. EAGLE-3's training-time test (TTT) explicitly simulates this behavior during training, at the cost of additional memory and specialized attention masks. We introduce two training-only methods for improving autoregressive drafting. Hidden State Mixing (HSM) samples among backbone and MTP hidden states aligned to the same prediction target, exposing the drafter to mixtures of states from different MTP steps without explicitly simulating inference. Hidden State Gradient Detach (HSGD) is a regularization method that stochastically blocks gradients through hidden state and embedding pathways while preserving the forward computation. Neither method adds inference cost. In joint pretraining followed by supervised fine-tuning, HSGD yields the highest acceptance rates at early draft positions, while HSM performs best later in the draft. These acceptance rate trends favor HSM for long-draft-length serving and HSGD for short-draft-length serving. Furthermore, across four Nemotron models and DeepSeek-V4-Pro, HSM-based MTP boosting with a frozen backbone improves mean acceptance length by up to 52.2% over the co-trained MTP. For EAGLE-3 drafters, HSM offers competitive acceptance with TTT, with 48% lower peak reserved training memory in a four-step Qwen3-8B comparison. HSM implementations are publicly available in Megatron-LM and NVIDIA Model Optimizer.

多标记预测（MTP）模块和EAGLE风格的草稿生成器通过重用目标模型的隐藏状态支持高效的推测性解码。然而，其自回归使用引入了训练-推理不匹配：训练时提供来自主干网络或单步MTP的隐藏状态序列，而推理时则关注目标模型前缀及递归生成的MTP状态尾部。EAGLE-3的训练时间测试（TTT）虽显式模拟此行为，但需额外内存和专用注意力掩码。我们提出两种仅用于训练的方法以改进自回归草稿生成。隐藏状态混合（HSM）在相同预测目标对齐的主干与MTP隐藏状态间采样，使草稿生成器在不显式模拟推理的情况下接触不同MTP步骤的状态混合。隐藏状态梯度分离（HSGD）是一种正则化方法，随机阻断隐藏状态和嵌入路径的梯度传播，同时保留前向计算。两种方法均不增加推理开销。在联合预训练后进行监督微调的实验中，HSGD在草稿早期位置取得最高接受率，而HSM在草稿后期表现最佳。这些接受率趋势表明，HSM更适合长草稿长度服务，HSGD更适合短草稿长度服务。此外，在四个Nemotron模型和DeepSeek-V4-Pro上，基于HSM的MTP增强（主干冻结）相比共同训练的MTP，平均接受长度提升高达52.2%。对于EAGLE-3草稿生成器，HSM在TTT竞争下表现优异，且在四步Qwen3-8B对比中峰值预留训练内存降低48%。HSM实现已公开于Megatron-LM和NVIDIA Model Optimizer。

DLaR: Dynamic Stochasticity Control for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models
DLaR：大语言模型隐式推理中的动态随机性控制
Latent reasoning enables LLMs to operate in continuous embedding space, yet existing methods suffer from deterministic inference that precludes exploration of diverse reasoning paths. While recent approaches inject stochasticity to restore exploration, we identify two fundamental limitations: \textit{uniform stochasticity} across all steps is suboptimal, and unconstrained perturbations disrupt reasoning coherence. We propose Dynamic Stochasticity Control for Latent Reasoning (DLaR), a framework with two complementary mechanisms that operate at different temporal granularities within the GRPO reinforcement learning framework. At the token level, Adaptive Stochasticity Modulation (ASM) dynamically adjusts perturbation intensity at each latent step based on a confidence-entropy fusion signal, increasing exploration when the model is uncertain and preserving stability when confident. At the training level, Training-Aware Stochasticity Tuning (TAST) progressively decays the base stochasticity level following a curriculum schedule. Through this dual-level design, DLaR establishes a hierarchical exploration-exploitation strategy that operates at both token and training levels. Extensive experiments on four in-distribution and three out-of-distribution mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that DLaR achieves the highest average performance across model scales, reaching 27.9%/50.6% Pass@1/Pass@8 on Llama-3.2-1B and 46.8%/68.2% on Qwen2.5-1.5B. Code: \href{https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR}{https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR}.
中文翻译
隐式推理使大语言模型能够在连续嵌入空间中运行，但现有方法存在确定性推理问题，限制了多样化推理路径的探索。尽管近期方法引入随机性以恢复探索能力，但我们识别出两个根本局限：所有步骤的随机性分布均匀并非最优，且不受约束的扰动会破坏推理连贯性。为此，我们提出动态随机性控制隐式推理（DLaR），一种在GRPO强化学习框架内运作的双机制互补框架，分别在不同时间粒度上发挥作用。在token层面，自适应随机性调节（ASM）基于置信度-熵融合信号动态调整每一步的扰动强度，在模型不确定时增强探索，在自信时保持稳定。在训练层面，训练感知随机性调优（TAST）遵循课程学习调度逐步衰减基础随机性水平。通过这一双层设计，DLaR建立了一个在token与训练层面协同作用的层次化探索-利用策略。在四个分布内和三个分布外的数学推理基准上的大量实验表明，DLaR在各模型尺度下均达到最高平均性能，在Llama-3.2-1B上实现Pass@1/Pass@8分别为27.9%/50.6%，在Qwen2.5-1.5B上达到46.8%/68.2%。代码已开源：https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR。
