agentic-rl
Looped Models are Fast Multi-Token Predictors
循环模型是快速的多标记预测器
Looped language models can adapt computation for predicting a token without a proportional rescaling in unique model parameters by adjusting the number of loops. This property has been used for self-speculative decoding, taking shallow loops as fast drafters and deep loops as verifiers, which is effective since drafting still uses full parameters. In this paper, we show that shallow loops can be also predictive of several future tokens and leverage this to drastically accelerate the inference of looped language models. To that end, we present Floodgates, a simple self-speculative decoding method that appends a block of mask tokens as a suffix and learns via low-rank adaptation to draft these tokens in parallel. Across seven models, five benchmarks, a range of batch sizes, and both greedy and sampled decoding, Floodgates is the fastest lossless decoding method in most cases, attaining up to 7.5x acceleration. Through analysis, we show that this speedup is enabled by all-depth training of looped models, with the shallow drafter's ability to leverage full-depth computation by conditioning on deep prefix cache evaluated during verification.

循环语言模型可通过调整循环次数来适应单个标记的预测，而无需成比例地增加唯一模型参数。这一特性已被用于自推测解码：浅层循环作为快速草稿生成器，深层循环作为验证器，效果良好，因为草稿阶段仍使用全部参数。本文表明，浅层循环也可用于预测多个未来标记，并利用此特性大幅加速循环语言模型的推理。为此，我们提出Floodgates，一种简单的自推测解码方法，通过在末尾附加掩码标记块，并通过低秩适配学习并行生成这些标记。在七种模型、五项基准、多种批量大小以及贪婪与采样解码方式下，Floodgates在大多数情况下成为最快的无损解码方法，速度最高可达7.5倍加速。通过分析，我们发现这种加速得益于循环模型的全深度训练，浅层草稿生成器能够通过在验证过程中依赖深层前缀缓存，利用完整深度计算。

The Missing Root: Geometric Imagination Without Images in Large Language Models
缺失的根源：大型语言模型中无图像的几何想象
This paper proposes a new paradigm and method for giving large language models spatial understanding. Standard language models operate exclusively over discrete words (secondary representations of things rather than continuous space itself), forcing them to guess geometry verbally. Whereas most multimodal approaches equate spatial grounding with camera images or video frames, spatial structure itself is not a picture: it consists of coordinate frames, solid boundaries, and how objects move and relate to each other in continuous space. Motivated by this distinction, we place continuous object tokens combining pose (position, orientation, and scale) and signed-distance shape embeddings alongside word tokens inside a single uninterrupted transformer sequence, allowing language and geometry to attend to each other as object scenes are built and updated step by step. Control tokens switch output automatically between natural-language words and continuous object state updates to a persistent object workspace, while an attention mask blocks earlier prose when generating the answer, so responses must be read directly from settled geometry. To prove this method works, we attach a narrow parallel spatial stream (+4.4% weights) alongside unfrozen Qwen3-1.7B layers. In our two-dimensional tests, the model spawns and transforms multi-object scenes from English descriptions, transfers shapes to held-out nouns, and relies causally on the workspace to answer spatial questions.
中文翻译
本文提出了一种新的范式和方法，以赋予大型语言模型空间理解能力。标准语言模型仅在离散词汇（事物的次级表征而非连续空间本身）上操作，迫使它们用语言猜测几何结构。大多数多模态方法将空间定位等同于相机图像或视频帧，但空间结构本身并非图片：它由坐标系、实体边界以及物体在连续空间中的运动和相互关系构成。基于这一区分，我们在单一不间断的Transformer序列中引入了结合姿态（位置、方向和尺度）与带符号距离形状嵌入的连续对象标记，使语言与几何能够逐步构建和更新对象场景时相互关注。控制标记可自动在自然语言词汇与持续对象工作区的连续状态更新之间切换，而注意力掩码则在生成答案时屏蔽早期文本，因此响应必须直接从已确定的几何结构中读取。为证明该方法有效，我们在未冻结的Qwen3-1.7B层旁添加了一个狭窄的并行空间流（+4.4%权重）。在二维测试中，模型能从英文描述生成并变换多对象场景，将形状转移到未见名词，并依赖工作区因果回答空间问题。

State Space Attention: Learning Context-Dependent Memory Retention
状态空间注意力：学习上下文相关的记忆保留
Standard attention's key-value cache grows linearly with context length. Bounding it raises the question of what a sequence model should retain for long-context reasoning. We argue this choice should be learned, and introduce State Space Attention (SSA), which learns to keep a bounded set of past representations and reads them with standard attention. A learned scorer rescores every chunk against newly arriving context and the rest of memory. A fixed-context Transformer is then the special case that always keeps the most recent tokens. We train the scorer and reader jointly, either moving gradually from a differentiable soft weighting of memories to hard retention, or with hard retention from the start. To produce signal for the scorer, we generate supervised samples during training, a technique we call recovery. We compare SSA with recurrent models, bounded-memory attention methods, and full-attention Transformers across retrieval, variable tracking, multi-hop reasoning, and language modeling. On our retrieval and reasoning benchmarks, SSA matches or comes within a few points of full attention on most tasks, far outperforms a Transformer with the same readable budget on every long-context retrieval task we evaluate, and matches or outperforms one with about 4 times that budget, while using constant inference memory. The largest remaining gap to full attention at small language model scale is on RULER. SSA makes a fixed number of tokens useful over a longer context, and since it reads memory with standard attention, we propose that it can be stacked with KV cache eviction methods. We also pretrain and post-train 400M-parameter models and measure how SSA's training and inference costs scale with context length and model size. Together, these experiments examine the capabilities and limitations of learned memory retention for long-context sequence modeling.
中文翻译
标准注意力的关键值缓存随上下文长度线性增长。限制其大小引发了序列模型在长上下文推理中应保留什么的问题。我们认为这一选择应当通过学习获得，并提出状态空间注意力（SSA），它学习保留一个有限的过去表示集，并使用标准注意力读取这些记忆。一个学习到的评分器对每个块相对于新到达的上下文和其余记忆进行重评分。固定上下文Transformer是始终保留最近标记的特例。我们联合训练评分器和读取器，或从可微软加权记忆逐渐过渡到硬保留，或从一开始就采用硬保留。为生成评分器信号，我们在训练期间生成有监督样本，这一技术称为恢复。我们在检索、变量跟踪、多跳推理和语言建模任务上对比了SSA与循环模型、有界记忆注意力方法及全注意力Transformer。在我们的检索和推理基准测试中，SSA在大多数任务上达到或接近全注意力性能，远优于具有相同可读预算的Transformer在所有长上下文检索任务上的表现，且在约四倍预算的Transformer上也表现相当或更优，同时推理内存恒定。在小规模语言模型上，与全注意力的差距主要存在于RULER任务。SSA使固定数量的标记在更长上下文中保持有用性，由于其使用标准注意力读取记忆，我们建议可将其与KV缓存淘汰方法堆叠。我们还预训练并后训练了4亿参数模型，测量SSA在上下文长度和模型规模下的训练与推理成本。这些实验共同考察了学习记忆保留在长上下文序列建模中的能力与局限。

SEEL: Towards End-to-End 3D Sign Generation with Language Models
SEEL：迈向端到端的3D手语生成
3D sign language generation aims to produce signing motion from written text. Motivated by the linguistic nature of sign language, recent methods combine a motion tokenizer with a language model to predict motion tokens from text. However, both components are optimized through a two-stage pipeline with mismatched objectives: the tokenizer minimizes reconstruction error in isolation, while the generator learns from rigid one-hot reference tokens. A straightforward end-to-end extension bridges discrete token selection with straight-through estimators (STE), suffering from biased gradients and codebook collapse. To address these limitations, we propose SEEL, an end-to-end framework that aligns tokenization and generation with motion quality through two complementary mechanisms. First, to overcome rigid one-hot supervision, reconstruction-aware generation decodes candidate tokens in context and converts their 3D motion errors into soft targets, guiding the generator with physical motion quality. Second, to propagate generation feedback without STE, generation-aware tokenization passes continuous codebook expectations through the frozen generator, routing generated-motion gradients to the tokenizer to prevent codebook collapse. Combining both mechanisms enables the tokenizer and generator to co-evolve, achieving state-of-the-art performance on CSL-Daily, How2Sign, and Phoenix-2014T. Code and models will be released to facilitate future research.
中文翻译
3D手语生成旨在从书面文本生成手语动作。受手语的语言性质启发，近期方法结合动作分词器与语言模型，从文本预测动作标记。然而，这两个组件通过两阶段流水线优化，目标不匹配：分词器独立最小化重构误差，而生成器则从刚性的one-hot参考标记学习。简单的端到端扩展通过直通估计器（STE）连接离散标记选择，导致梯度偏差和代码本崩溃。为解决这些局限，我们提出SEEL，一种通过两种互补机制实现分词与生成对齐的动作质量的端到端框架。首先，为克服刚性one-hot监督，重构感知生成在上下文中解码候选标记，并将3D动作误差转换为软目标，以物理动作质量引导生成器。其次，为在不使用STE的情况下传播生成反馈，生成感知分词通过冻结生成器传递连续代码本期望，将生成动作梯度路由至分词器，防止代码本崩溃。结合两种机制，使分词器与生成器协同进化，在CSL-Daily、How2Sign和Phoenix-2014T数据集上达到最先进性能。代码与模型将公开，以促进未来研究。

CA-RAG: Constraint-Aligned Retrieval for Structurally Faithful Long-Form Generation
CA-RAG：结构忠实的长文本生成中的约束对齐检索
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically optimises evidence selection for semantic relevance, whereas compliance-sensitive long-form generation also requires evidence supporting explicit structural obligations. Semantically relevant retrieval may therefore omit information needed for mandatory downstream clauses. We propose Constraint-Aligned RAG (CA-RAG), a retrieval-conditioning framework that augments section-specific queries with ontology-derived structural constraints and policy-grounded knowledge-graph relations before evidence selection. Retrieval-only evaluation with manually annotated evidence shows consistent improvements across retrieval budgets. At $k=6$, CA-RAG increases Precision@$k$ from $0.5268$ to $0.5692$, pooled Recall@$k$ from $0.5402$ to $0.5950$, and F1@$k$ from $0.5389$ to $0.5672$ over standard RAG. Generation experiments further show improvements in compliance and reliability in several model--domain settings, while generation-only constraints achieve higher clause coverage but lower overall compliance and reliability. These results indicate that structurally aligned retrieval can improve evidence sufficiency in compliance-sensitive long-form generation.
中文翻译

A Semantics-First Approach to Multimodal Video Captioning with Frozen Encoders and Stabilized Reinforcement Learning
以语义为先的多模态视频字幕生成：基于冻结编码器与稳定强化学习的方法
We propose the Audio-Visual Query Transformer (AVQFormer), a highly efficient architecture built upon a 2.7B parameter LLM featuring a fixed-length $K=16$ semantic alignment module, stabilized via Adaptive Self-Critical Sequence Training (ASCST) with Entropy Regularization. Multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) often rely on global audio representations to compensate for inefficient, frame-based visual encoders. While our Phase 1 prompted evaluations demonstrate that the Multimodal Base Model achieves strong deep semantic alignment (scoring 0.889 on BERTScore F1), optimizing this architecture for legacy metrics reveals a performance gap. We explore this gap as a hypothesis we term temporal alignment friction: a conflict where global audio representations disrupt the chronological sequence of visual frames, leading to penalties under visually-focused evaluation metrics. Consequently, by stripping away the audio, our Video-Only ablation conforms more closely to this rigid syntax, achieving 63.0 CIDEr. While trailing larger models such as Qwen2.5-VL (67.4 CIDEr), this establishes a strong baseline for sub-3B parameter LLM-based models. This indicates that CIDEr, as applied to datasets such as MSR-VTT, may exhibit a bias toward visually grounded syntax, limiting its ability to fully capture multimodal semantic richness.

Partial Routing: Trace-Tuned Delegation for Resource-Efficient Agentic Workflows
部分路由：面向资源高效的智能体工作流的轨迹调优委派机制
AI models vary enormously in capability and price. Model routing exploits this by sending easier tasks to cheaper models. However, a single agentic task often combines easy and difficult subtasks, making one model choice for the entire task inefficient. We introduce partial routing, which guides a frontier lead model to delegate suitable subtasks to a cheaper worker model. From both models' past runs, we identify recurring subtasks and measure each model's quality and cost on them. These measurements produce delegation guidance, with a parameter, $δ$, controlling how much is delegated. The lead decides when and what to delegate during execution, without training either model. On held-out tasks from ViBench and Terminal-Bench 4.0, partial routing achieves higher mean task performance than the lead alone and task-level routing. With $δ= 0.75$, it raises the lead's ViBench score from 70.2 to 78.5 at 19% lower cost, and its Terminal-Bench pass rate from 54% to 61% at similar cost. Giving the lead only the subtask breakdown, without a worker, also reduces cost on both benchmarks and raises the Terminal-Bench pass rate. These results show that analysis of past runs can support more efficient planning and more effective delegation, with little cost and implementation overhead.

AI模型的能力和价格差异巨大。模型路由利用这一特性，将简单任务分配给更便宜的模型。然而，单个智能体任务通常包含简单与复杂子任务，单一模型选择整个任务效率低下。我们提出部分路由，引导主模型在执行过程中将合适的子任务委派给更廉价的工作者模型。基于两模型过往运行记录，我们识别出重复出现的子任务，并测量各模型在这些任务上的质量与成本。这些测量结果生成委派指导策略，其中参数δ控制委派程度。主模型在执行期间自主决定何时及如何委派，且无需训练任一模型。在ViBench和Terminal-Bench 4.0的保留任务上，部分路由的平均任务性能优于主模型单独处理或任务级路由。当δ=0.75时，主模型在ViBench上的得分从70.2提升至78.5，成本降低19%；在Terminal-Bench上的通过率从54%提升至61%，成本相近。即使仅提供子任务分解而不配备工作者，也能在两个基准上降低成本并提升Terminal-Bench通过率。结果表明，对历史运行的分析可支持更高效的规划与更有效的委派，且开销极小。

Confidence scores for gated automation of large language models
用于大语言模型门控自动化的置信度评分
AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated decision-making. However, LLMs may produce incorrect answers with high confidence, limiting their applicability in decisions that affect individuals and organizations. Reliable confidence scores are necessary to gate such decisions, and existing estimates are frequently unavailable or too coarse to support effective gating. This work uses supervised learning to train models to predict the correctness of LLM-generated answers, enabling automated confident responses and routing uncertain ones for human review. A linear confidence score head is fitted to the final-layer hidden state of the frozen LLM backbone, and text-only metamodels are trained as an alternative for closed models. All results come from a single 2B-parameter backbone, on graded answers that are about 98% SQuAD and the remainder TruthfulQA. Against multiple baselines, the confidence score head outperforms all baselines and metamodels, automating the largest fraction of the workload at high accuracy targets. Prompted confidence scores can appear well calibrated yet lack the resolution needed for thresholding. Although transferability to unseen datasets is modest, the confidence score head remains the most robust approach. The proposed confidence scoring framework enhances the safety of automating LLM-based agents and can be adapted to gate other properties, including safety or compliance.

Temporal Weight Surgery: Repairing Video Grounding with LLM Editors
时间权重手术：使用 LLM 编辑器修复视频定位
A surgeon first finds what is wrong, then fixes only that part. We use this idea for video grounding, where video-language models often find the right moment in a long video but write its time wrong. In Temporal Weight Surgery (TWS), a general-purpose LLM reads the model's weights and activations, finds where the error arises and repairs it there, without gradients. Separate sessions find the same cause, and this diagnosis of where and why the model fails is our main contribution. Three GPT-6 Astra sessions trace a dropped digit in TimeLens-8B to one MLP layer, and the largest edit gains 7.9 mIoU on 1,215 unseen questions and 18.3 on videos of 30 minutes or more. Two Claude Opus 5.5 sessions trace minutes written as seconds in Qwen3-VL-8B to the separator and gain 2.9 and 1.3. Before any edit, its answers often have the right minute and the wrong separator, and fixing only the separator lifts their mIoU from 13.1 to 19.2. Every improving edit beats a random edit of its size, and the edited models run as fast as before.

Building a Unified Inline Code Editing Model for GitHub Copilot
构建 GitHub Copilot 的统一内联代码编辑模型
AI-assisted coding ranges from fully agentic coding to inline code suggestions in the editor. Inline suggestions require predicting a developer's most likely next edit from an editing session. The next edit may lie at the cursor, in nearby lines, or farther away, and one editing intent can span several of these. The preceding system queries separate completion, nearby edit, and long-distance edit models, each with its own output format, in a fixed sequence; it thus cannot express intents spanning distant locations in one response, can require several sequential model calls per suggestion, and prioritizes completion even when a correction would serve better. We present a unified model that predicts all three edit types in one output space: a sequence of anchored diff patches specifying locations, deleted lines, and added lines. This representation permits multiple edits per response, so the client can serve later edits from a cache without further model calls. We train this model in two phases, first unifying next edit and long-distance edit prediction, then adding code completion. We synthesize multi-edit supervision from single-edit data via model pseudolabeling and edit history playback. We use a binary LLM-graded reward, a patch validity taxonomy shared by data filtering, reward, and evaluation, and grader boosting for targeted failure modes. Phase 2 also adapts how the client processes and presents model outputs. In controlled online A/B experiments in GitHub Copilot, Phase 1 significantly reduces time to display by 6.77% and output tokens by 58.93% relative to the three-model baseline. In Phase 2, the 3-in-1 model and client adaptations reduce dismissed suggestions per user by 10.06% relative to the 2-in-1 model paired with the completion model. Throughout this report, we also share our methodology and learnings in evaluation, training, and end-to-end model and system design.
中文翻译
AI 辅助编程涵盖从完全自主的编程到编辑器内的内联代码建议。内联建议需从编辑会话中预测开发者最可能的下一步编辑。下一步编辑可能位于光标处、附近行或更远位置，一种编辑意图可能跨越多个区域。现有系统分别调用完成、邻近编辑和远距离编辑模型，每种模型输出格式不同，按固定顺序执行；因此无法在一个响应中表达跨越远距离位置的意图，可能需要多次连续模型调用，且即使修正更优也优先推荐补全。我们提出一个统一模型，将三种编辑类型在同一输出空间中预测：一组锚定的 diff 补丁，指定位置、删除行和新增行。该表示允许多个编辑共存于一次响应中，客户端可从缓存中提供后续编辑而无需额外模型调用。我们分两阶段训练该模型：首先统一下一步编辑与远距离编辑预测，然后加入代码补全。通过模型伪标签和编辑历史回放，从单编辑数据合成多编辑监督信号。采用二值 LLM 评分奖励，共享的补丁有效性分类体系用于数据过滤、奖励和评估，并通过评分器增强针对特定失败模式。第二阶段还调整了客户端处理和展示模型输出的方式。在 GitHub Copilot 的受控线上 A/B 实验中，第一阶段相比三模型基线显著减少显示时间 6.77%，输出 token 数减少 58.93%。第二阶段中，3-in-1 模型与客户端优化相比 2-in-1 模型加补全模型，用户拒绝建议次数减少 10.06%。本报告还分享了评估、训练及端到端模型与系统设计的方法论与经验。
👤 Microsoft CodeAI Team, Microsoft VS Code Team, Julia Gong, Benjamin Liggett, Shengjie Ma et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33303]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Certify What You Plant: Shortcut-Free Proof Depth in Logical Reasoning Data
验证你所设定的：逻辑推理数据中无捷径的证明深度
Procedurally generated logic problems are used to both evaluate and train reasoning in language models. Their difficulty is usually set by planting a proof, that is, by sampling a proof of the desired depth and stating its premises as the problem. However, a planted proof is only one possible proof. A problem can also have shorter proofs, or several unrelated ones, and its stated depth then overstates what solving it requires. We measure what a problem actually requires, relative to a stated set of inference rules. For each problem, we compute the steps every proof must make, the shortest proof, the number of minimal proofs and the indispensable premises, and export certificates that an independent checker verifies. Audits of eight datasets find depth that is forced (ProofWriter, Blocksworld), problems labelled depth 5 that require about two specific rule applications (FLDx2), and answers that need no particular step (ZebraLogic). We then construct problems whose every proof has a requested structure, using a catalog of shortcuts proved once per rule set. Every constructed problem is checked in full and comes with its own certificate. In training, the best data mixes depths and problem types. At equal size, such mixtures of certified problem types, including model-checking problems that forward generation does not produce, transfer better than FLDx2-style derivation data for Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B.
中文翻译
程序生成的逻辑问题被用于评估和训练语言模型的推理能力。其难度通常通过‘植入证明’来设定，即采样一个期望深度的证明，并将前提作为问题陈述。然而，植入的证明只是可能的证明之一，问题也可能存在更短的证明或多个无关的证明，因此所声明的深度会高估解决问题所需的实际要求。我们测量了问题在给定推理规则集下实际需要的复杂度。对于每个问题，我们计算所有证明必须经过的步骤、最短证明、最小证明的数量以及不可或缺的前提，并导出可由独立检查器验证的证书。对八个数据集的审计发现：某些数据集的深度是强制性的（ProofWriter, Blocksworld），某些标注为深度5的问题实际上仅需约两个特定规则的应用（FLDx2），而某些答案根本不需要特定步骤（ZebraLogic）。随后，我们构建了所有证明都具有指定结构的问题，使用每套规则集预先证明的一组捷径清单。每个构造出的问题均经过完整验证并附带自身证书。在训练中，最佳数据组合应混合不同深度和问题类型。在相同规模下，这种经认证的问题类型混合（包括前向生成无法产生的模型检验问题）相较于FLDx2风格的推导数据，在Qwen3模型从0.6B到8B的迁移中表现更优。
👤 Baptiste Juran, Dimitri Kachler, Damien Sileo 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33302]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Two Timescales of Forgetting in Fine-tuning of Language Models: Fast Behavioral Suppression and Slow Representational Drift
语言模型微调中的遗忘双重时间尺度：快速行为抑制与缓慢表征漂移
Today, a core pipeline of artificial intelligence systems is pretraining on a broad range of text corpora and then fine-tuning on specific tasks or desired behaviors. Yet we still have limited understanding of how fine-tuning on a restricted data distribution modifies representations acquired during pretraining. In particular, it remains unclear whether surface behavioral suppression happens together with the erasure of latent representations that support it. In this work, we study supervised fine-tuning (SFT) by pretraining transformers on a controlled context-free grammar with hierarchical latent structure, the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and fine-tuning with a restricted subset of its generative rules. While during pretraining the model's ability to generate with the correct grammar rules coincides with building the internal representations of the corresponding latent, we find that SFT exhibits a very different dynamics: (i) the generation of deleted rules changes on a fast timescale inversely proportional to the magnitude of the token-latent correlation; meanwhile, their corresponding representations stay intact; (ii) the hidden representations of the deleted rules become forgotten on a much longer timescale, associated with a slow drift of the network parameters. Finally, we observe analogous results in a realistic setup with natural language, suggesting that this timescale separation in forgetting is a more general phenomenon of fine-tuning language models.
中文翻译
当前人工智能系统的核心流程是先在广泛文本语料上进行预训练，再针对特定任务或期望行为进行微调。然而，我们对在有限数据分布上的微调如何改变预训练期间获得的表征仍知之甚少。特别是，表面行为抑制是否伴随支持该行为的潜在表征的消除尚不明确。本研究通过在受控的上下文无关语法（具有层次化潜在结构的随机层次模型RHM）上预训练Transformer，并使用其生成规则的受限子集进行监督微调（SFT），探讨了这一问题。尽管在预训练阶段，模型正确生成语法规则的能力与其内部潜在表征的建立同步，但SFT展现出截然不同的动态：(i) 被删除规则的生成变化发生在快速时间尺度上，且与词元-潜在相关性的大小成反比；而这些规则对应的表征保持完整；(ii) 被删除规则的隐藏表征则在更长的时间尺度上逐渐遗忘，与网络参数的缓慢漂移相关。最后，我们在真实自然语言设置中观察到类似结果，表明这种遗忘的时间尺度分离是语言模型微调中的普遍现象。
👤 Yilin Luo, Jin Hwa Lee, Andrew M. Saxe, Antonio Sclocchi 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

[2611.34938]
Tokenisation is not in APX
分词不在APX类中
In this paper, we prove that, unless P=NP, no polynomial-time algorithm can find a tokeniser whose objective is within any constant factor of the optimum, meaning that tokenisation is not in the APX complexity class. We prove this for direct and bottom-up tokenisation, under both a compression or unigram log-likelihood objectives, and for binary or larger alphabets. We further show that, even over unary alphabets, direct tokenisation is APX-hard, and thus admits no polynomial-time approximation scheme.
中文翻译
本文证明，除非P=NP，否则不存在多项式时间算法能够找到目标函数值在最优解常数倍范围内的分词器，这意味着分词问题不属于APX复杂度类。该结论对直接分词和自底向上的分词方法均成立，无论采用压缩目标或单字元对数似然目标，也适用于二进制或更大的字母表。此外，我们还证明，在仅含单一符号的字母表下，直接分词仍是APX难问题，因此不具有多项式时间近似方案。
👤 Violeta Kastreva, Philip Whittington, Dennis Komm, Tiago Pimentel 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34832]
Proverbs As A Window On Collective Prescriptions
谚语作为集体规范的窗口
Proverbs, as repositories of traditional wisdom, have informed human reasoning for millennia. Yet the collective prescriptions they encode have remained largely unquantified. We introduce a computational framework to analyze nearly 50,000 English-language proverbs. Using large language models, validated against human judgments, we map the topical landscape of proverbs, assess their prescriptive power, and quantify their recommendations on key dimensions of human judgment and behavior. We find that most proverbs are prescriptive, offering guidance in domains under individual control, such as preparation and prudent action, whereas proverbs about uncontrollable circumstances, such as fate and mortality, tend to describe rather than prescribe. When prescriptive proverbs take a consistent stance, they favor caution over risk, long-term over short-term outcomes, honesty over deception, and kindness over malice, and show a moderate tendency toward suspicion over trust. On dimensions involving genuine trade-offs, especially autonomy versus compliance, proverbs provide contradicting advice rather than a single recommendation. This analysis provides a quantitative map of the collective prescriptions in a culture's proverbial wisdom, revealing a core of prudent, honest, and forward-looking guidance alongside varied advice where circumstances dictate the best course of action.
中文翻译
谚语作为传统智慧的载体，已为人类推理服务了数千年。然而，其所蕴含的集体规范至今仍缺乏量化分析。我们提出一种计算框架，对近5万条英语谚语进行分析。利用大语言模型并经由人类判断验证，我们绘制了谚语的主题图谱，评估其规范效力，并量化其在人类判断与行为关键维度上的建议。研究发现，大多数谚语具有规范性，提供在个人可控领域（如准备与审慎行动）的指导；而关于不可控情境（如命运与死亡）的谚语则更倾向于描述而非规范。当规范性谚语立场一致时，它们倾向于谨慎而非冒险，长期而非短期结果，诚实而非欺骗，善意而非恶意，并表现出适度的怀疑倾向而非信任。在涉及真实权衡的维度（尤其是自主性与服从性之间），谚语提供相互矛盾的建议，而非单一推荐。该分析为文化谚语智慧中的集体规范提供了定量地图，揭示出以审慎、诚实和前瞻为导向的核心指导，以及在特定情境下因时制宜的多样化建议。
👤 Tomás Lejarraga, Dirk U. Wulff, Ralph Hertwig 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34725]
Bigger Model or Bigger Table? How Much Backbone a Chorus N-gram Module Replaces
更大的模型还是更大的表？Chorus N-gram模块替代了多少骨干结构
We ask how much of a transformer's backbone the Chorus N-gram module replaces. Chorus adds hashed tables of token bigrams and trigrams to its input and attention. We train models of 16M to 349M parameters on 1.34 billion tokens of Polish printed before 1918 and find the size of a plain model with the same held-out loss. A separate learning-rate search for each kind of model cuts Chorus's gain at 47M by about a third. With tables about as large as the backbone, Chorus then matches a plain model 1.34 times its size at 16M and 1.16 to 1.20 times from 107M to 349M. With these tables, each added parameter replaces at most 0.31 backbone parameters. Chorus trains slower than a plain model of its size and faster than one of the same loss. On a fact quiz the two differ about as much as two seeds of one model. The results rest on one corpus, one 8,000-token vocabulary and a fifth of an epoch.
中文翻译
我们探讨了Chorus N-gram模块在多大程度上替代了Transformer的骨干结构。Chorus在其输入和注意力机制中加入了哈希化的词对和词三元组表格。我们在13.4亿个波兰语印刷文本（1918年前）上训练了参数量从1600万到3.49亿不等的模型，并找到了具有相同保留损失的纯模型大小。针对每类模型进行独立的学习率搜索后，Chorus在4700万参数时的增益被削减约三分之一。当表格规模与骨干结构相当，Chorus在1600万参数时可达到同规模模型1.34倍的性能，在1.07亿至3.49亿参数范围内则相当于1.16至1.20倍的纯模型。在此条件下，每个新增参数最多替代0.31个骨干参数。Chorus的训练速度慢于同规模的纯模型，但快于同损失水平的模型。在事实问答测试中，两者表现差异类似于同一模型的不同随机种子。这些结果基于单一语料库、8000个词的词汇表以及五分之一个训练周期。
👤 Szymon Kocur 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

Technical Report MEG2text: Beyond Alignment in Non-Invasive Brain-to-Text
技术报告 MEG2text：非侵入式脑机文本生成中的超越对齐
Non-invasive brain-to-text seeks to recover language from brain activity without surgery. A central challenge is turning learned brain-language representations into accurate word predictions. We present MEG2text, a decoding system that combines corrected training and evaluation, brain-text alignment, a class-balanced word readout, and target-word language-model fusion. On LibriBrain100's word-ranking task, MEG2text reaches 64.12% mean Top-10 balanced accuracy, improving on published MEG-XL by 16.92 percentage points across four corpora, with gains on each corpus. Controlled comparisons show that both implementation corrections and a learned readout contribute to this improvement. Candidate-level analysis reveals that some words receive informative scores yet consistently lose to competing candidates, while an identical-feature comparison shows that learning the classification rule improves decoding without changing the representation. These findings identify the decoding rule as a consequential part of non-invasive brain-to-text and show how system improvements can make better use of aligned neural features.
中文翻译
非侵入式脑机文本旨在无需手术即可从脑活动恢复语言。核心挑战在于如何将学习到的脑-语言表示转化为准确的词预测。我们提出MEG2text，一种解码系统，结合了修正后的训练与评估、脑-文本对齐、类别平衡的词输出机制以及目标词语言模型融合。在LibriBrain100的词排名任务中，MEG2text达到64.12%的平均Top-10平衡准确率，相较于已发表的MEG-XL在四个语料库上提升了16.92个百分点，且各语料库均有提升。控制对比实验表明，实现修正和学习型输出均对性能提升有贡献。候选词层面分析显示，部分词语虽获得信息量评分，却始终败给竞争候选词；相同特征比较则表明，学习分类规则可改善解码效果而不改变表示本身。这些发现表明解码规则是非侵入式脑机文本中的关键组成部分，系统改进能更有效地利用对齐的神经特征。
👤 Yiqian Yang, Yiqun Duan 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34539]
EPPC-CARE: Staged Hierarchical Annotation of Communication Behaviors in Secure Patient-Provider Messages
EPPC-CARE：安全患者-医生消息中沟通行为的分阶段层次标注
Objectives: Patient portal messages provide invaluable information about communication dynamics between patients and healthcare providers, but extracting it manually is labor-intensive. We developed and evaluated EPPC-CARE, an automated framework for identifying communication behaviors together with the supporting text for each classification. Materials and Methods: From 867 messages, we obtained 5,516 reference standard annotations. Each annotation is a triplet of communication-related text (span), a broader communication category (Code), and a specific subcategory (Sub-code). We evaluated EPPC-CARE's identified triplets across six open-weight large language models (LLMs) against supervised fine-tuning and communication-based models such as EPPC-OASIS. We also compared EPPC-CARE with human expert annotations on 100 messages. Results: EPPC-CARE improved performance over EPPC-OASIS across all six models. EPPC-CARE's Triplet F1 improved by 2.90 percentage points on average, with a best score of 64.4%. On the human expert evaluation subset, EPPC-CARE and the human expert showed comparable agreement with the reference standard, with differences favoring EPPC-CARE. Discussion: These findings suggest that identifying triplet components (span, code, subcode) sequentially can improve performance across model families while preserving the supporting text behind each identification. EPPC-CARE links the communication code and subcode with the source text (span), supporting human verification of automated annotations in clinical informatics. EPPC-CARE may therefore make larger-scale studies of patient-provider communication more feasible and provide a foundation for human-in-the-loop annotation workflows. Conclusion: EPPC-CARE offers a more reliable and reviewable approach for automated characterization of communication behaviors in secure patient-provider messaging.
中文翻译
目的：患者门户消息提供了关于患者与医疗提供者之间沟通动态的宝贵信息，但手动提取极为耗时。我们开发并评估了EPPC-CARE，一种自动化框架，用于识别沟通行为及其对应的支撑文本。材料与方法：从867条消息中获取了5516个参考标准标注。每个标注是一个三元组：与沟通相关的文本片段（span）、更广泛的沟通类别（Code）和具体子类别（Sub-code）。我们评估了EPPC-CARE在六种开源大语言模型（LLMs）上的识别结果，对比了监督微调和基于沟通的模型如EPPC-OASIS。还与人类专家在100条消息上的标注进行了比较。结果：EPPC-CARE在所有六种模型上均优于EPPC-OASIS。EPPC-CARE的三元组F1平均提升2.90个百分点，最高达64.4%。在人类专家评估子集上，EPPC-CARE与人类专家对参考标准的吻合度相当，且差异倾向支持EPPC-CARE。讨论：这些发现表明，按顺序识别三元组组件（片段、类别、子类别）可在不同模型族中提升性能，同时保留每个识别背后的支撑文本。EPPC-CARE将沟通类别与子类别与其源文本（片段）关联，支持临床信息学中人工验证自动化标注。因此，EPPC-CARE使更大规模的患者-医生沟通研究成为可能，并为“人机协同”标注工作流奠定基础。结论：EPPC-CARE为安全患者-医生消息中沟通行为的自动化表征提供了一种更可靠、可审查的方法。

[2611.34505]
Know the Design, Pick the Attack: Sentence-Keyed Watermarks for LLMs
知其设计，择其攻击：基于句子关键词的LLM水印技术
A watermark lets the provider of a language model recognise the text its model wrote, which matters more as generated text becomes hard to tell from human writing: provenance underpins academic integrity, the fight against misinformation and new regulation. Providers have started to watermark the text of their models, and to explain how their watermarks work. Explaining a watermark, however, also tells an attacker how to break it, and there are two ways to do so: reword the text to erase the mark, or learn the mark and copy it onto text the model never wrote. Each attack has been studied on its own. We study an attacker that reads the published design to choose between them: it erases the watermarks that are hard to learn and forges the ones that survive rewording. Against it, every existing watermark we compare fails. We propose KeyedLex, which reads its secret key from the grammar of the previous sentence, which rewording changes little, and whose detector does not need to recover the key exactly. It outperforms every watermark we compare on both models we test, losing about half of its signal where the best alternative loses two thirds, at no extra cost in text quality over the best-known watermarks.
中文翻译
水印使语言模型提供者能够识别其模型生成的文本，随着生成文本越来越难以与人类写作区分，这一能力在维护学术诚信、对抗虚假信息和应对新法规方面愈发重要。模型提供者已开始对模型输出文本添加水印，并解释其工作原理。然而，解释水印机制也会暴露攻击者如何破解它，主要有两种方式：重写文本以消除水印，或学习水印并将其复制到模型从未生成过的文本上。这两种攻击各自已有研究。本文研究了一种阅读公开设计后选择攻击策略的攻击者：它会擦除难以学习的水印，同时伪造能抵御重写的水印。针对此类攻击，我们对比的所有现有水印均失效。为此，我们提出KeyedLex，它从前一句的语法中读取秘密密钥，重写对其影响极小，且检测器无需精确恢复密钥。在两种测试模型上，KeyedLex均优于所有对比水印，在信号损失方面仅约为最佳替代方案的一半，且在文本质量上不增加额外成本。
👤 Jon Carmona, Jon Irureta, Jon Imaz, Aizea Lojo, Juan Martínez-Romo et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34492]
FlyingStart: Learned Cross-Token Warm Starts for Fixed-Point Decoding in Recurrent-Depth Language Models
FlyingStart：递归深度语言模型中固定点解码的跨标记学习热启动方法
Recurrent-depth language models generate each token by iterating a shared block of layers until its hidden state converges. Inference methods for these models decide when to stop the iteration, yet every token still starts from random noise and pays the full cost of converging. We show that where the iteration starts is a second, independent lever. A token's fixed point is predictable from the state at which the previous token stopped, after a learned change of coordinates, and we introduce FlyingStart, a linear warm start that is fitted in closed form on the frozen model and costs under 5% of one loop. The iteration and its stopping rule are unchanged, so the exit criterion certifies the same distance to the fixed point from any start: a better start removes loops at unchanged accuracy. On Huginn-0125, FlyingStart matches the accuracy of the strongest training-free baseline with 14--19% fewer decoding layers, raises batched throughput by 16--18% in two serving engines, and adds a further 10--14% on top of self-speculative decoding. A contraction analysis predicts the number of loops saved on all four models we study, including one where the prediction was recorded before any evaluation, and token by token on Huginn, and it identifies when a warm start pays off: the saving grows with the number of loops a model needs to converge.
中文翻译
递归深度语言模型通过迭代共享层块生成每个标记，直到隐藏状态收敛。这类模型的推理方法决定何时停止迭代，但每个标记仍从随机噪声开始，并承担完整的收敛成本。我们发现，迭代起始点是另一个独立的控制变量。一个标记的固定点可从上一个标记停止时的状态预测得出，经过一次学习后的坐标变换，我们提出FlyingStart，一种闭式求解的线性热启动方法，仅需不到一次循环的成本（低于5%）。迭代过程和停止规则保持不变，因此退出标准始终保证从任意起点到固定点的距离一致：更好的起始点可在不降低准确率的前提下减少迭代次数。在Huginn-0125上，FlyingStart以14–19%更少的解码层达到最强无训练基线的精度，在两个服务引擎中批量吞吐量提升16–18%，并在自推测解码基础上再提升10–14%。收缩分析预测了我们在四个模型上节省的迭代次数，包括一个在评估前就已记录预测结果的模型，且在Huginn上实现了逐标记预测，还识别出热启动何时有效：节省的迭代数随模型收敛所需迭代次数增加而增长。
👤 Runguo Li, Dezhen Lu, Zixiang Ni 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34491]
Where Does the Asymmetry Enter? Staging a Fairness Audit Across a Fused Multimodal Moderation Pipeline
不对称性从何而来？在融合多模态内容审核流水线中开展公平性审计
Almost every short-video upload now passes through a classifier that fuses video, speech, and on-screen text into one decision about whether it may be seen. It moderates every language community on the platform at once, and fairness across them is judged, if at all, from one accuracy score. That score is compatible with error falling almost entirely on one language group. An audit that goes looking reads the model's decisions, the last stage of a long pipeline and the one whose test has the fewest items. We ask where in a fused pipeline a subgroup asymmetry can be detected at all: in what the classifier may read, in what its branches encode, or in what it decides. We answer this with a post-hoc audit of a published TikTok classifier, holding all three stages to one significance standard using only its logged decisions, labels, and pre-fusion features, with source language as the subgroup axis. The asymmetry is significant at the stage deciding what the model may read, and again in what the text branch encodes. At the earlier stage, a language-blind cap admits less of a Vietnamese clip than an English one, for reasons of segmentation, not verbosity. The asymmetry is undetectable at the decision, where the smallest subgroup leaves twelve items to test. An audit reading only decisions looks where its evidence is thinnest; the same test staged upstream recovers a disparity the decision-level view returns as noise.
中文翻译
如今几乎所有短视频上传都会经过一个融合视频、语音和屏幕文字的分类器，统一判断其是否可被观看。该分类器同时作用于平台上的所有语言社区，其公平性若被评估，通常仅依据单一准确率分数。该分数可能兼容错误几乎完全集中在某一语言群体的情况。我们开展一项审计，深入分析模型决策流程——即长流水线中的最后一阶段，也是测试样本最少的阶段。我们探讨在融合流水线中，子群体不对称性可能在何处被检测到：是在分类器可读取的内容中，还是在其分支编码的信息中，抑或在其最终决策中？我们通过对已发表的TikTok分类器进行事后审计，以源语言为子群体轴线，仅使用其日志中的决策、标签和融合前特征，对三个阶段施加同一显著性标准。结果显示，不对称性在决定模型可读取内容的阶段显著存在，且在文本分支编码的信息中再次显现。在早期阶段，语言无关的限制导致越南语片段被接纳的比例低于英语片段，原因在于分词而非冗长程度。在最终决策阶段，由于最小子群体仅有十二个样本可供测试，不对称性无法被检测。仅阅读决策的审计将证据最薄弱处作为关注点；而在上游阶段进行相同测试则能恢复出决策层面视作噪声的差异。

[2611.34427]
Mitigating Hallucinations in Medical Question Answering on ESC Cardiology Guidelines: Evaluation and Retrieval Optimization
减轻ESC心脏病学指南医学问答中的幻觉：评估与检索优化
Background: Large language models produce hallucinations in medical question answering (QA). Verify-and-revise pipelines that check atomic claims against retrieved guideline evidence are a standard mitigation, but evaluations almost universally treat the verifier's output as ground truth, an assumption rarely tested. Objective: To measure, claim by claim against human judgement, how such a pipeline fails in a guideline-grounded clinical setting. Methods: We built a dataset of 160 questions (100 patient vignettes from 89 de-identified cases, 60 fact-based) over four recent ESC cardiology guidelines, all approved by a practising internist, and a retrieval corpus of 627 hand-verified guideline records at two chunk granularities. A five-stage pipeline was run under four retrieval configurations and three independent runs (1920 arm-runs); all 606 first-pass refuted claims were manually audited, an independent judge re-judged 906 decisions, and the expert blindly reviewed 100 accepted claims. Results: Only 44.7-50.6% of flagged claims were true hallucinations. Correction loops converged in 85.2-95.1% of cases, yet 35% of genuine final errors were introduced by correction. Single-run true-hallucination recall was 35.6-41.2%, the three-run union 71-80%; verifier agreement was moderate (kappa = 0.505). The blind probe estimated the accepted-claim miss rate at 3.2% (95% CI 0.7-6.7%). Final genuine-error rate: 1.7% of answers. Conclusion: Claim-level verification reduces hallucinated content by an order of magnitude, but verifier reliability, not generation, is the limiting factor and must be measured, not assumed.
中文翻译
背景：大型语言模型在医学问答中会产生幻觉。验证与修正流水线通过将原子性陈述与检索到的指南证据比对来缓解此问题，但评估几乎普遍将验证器输出视为真实情况，这一假设极少被检验。目标：在基于指南的临床环境中，逐条对照人工判断，测量此类流水线的失败情况。方法：我们构建了一个包含160个问题的数据集（100个患者案例来自89个匿名病例，60个事实型问题），覆盖四份近期ESC心脏病学指南，所有内容均由执业内科医生审核批准；并建立了一个包含627条手工验证指南记录的检索语料库，涵盖两种分块粒度。在四种检索配置和三次独立运行（共1920次运行）下执行五阶段流水线；所有606个首轮驳回的陈述均经人工审计，独立评审员重新评估了906项决策，专家盲审了100个被接受的陈述。结果：仅44.7%-50.6%的标记陈述为真正的幻觉。修正循环在85.2%-95.1%的案例中收敛，但35%的真实最终错误由修正过程引入。单次运行的真实幻觉召回率为35.6%-41.2%，三次运行的并集为71%-80%；验证器一致性为中等（kappa = 0.505）。盲探估计被接受陈述的漏检率为3.2%（95%置信区间0.7-6.7%）。最终真实错误率：答案中1.7%。结论：逐条验证可将幻觉内容减少一个数量级，但验证器可靠性而非生成本身才是主要瓶颈，必须加以测量而非假设。

Under the Influence -- of What? A Controlled Evaluation of Influence Functions
受什么影响？——影响函数的受控评估
Influence functions are a promising way to identify which training examples are responsible for a model's output. However, so far empirical evaluations on LLM pretraining level only assumed that they work as intended. This is the first work to systematically evaluate whether the 'influential' examples actually have properties relevant to a language model output, based on re-training with controlled data splits. We investigate three prominent Hessian approximation methods (GradSim, TrackStar, K-FAC) in two settings (linguistic phenomena and factual knowledge) in GPT-2 type models (68M-5.6B training tokens). We find that on average, the `influential' examples are indeed relevant, but they do not always bear the target properties directly, and the ratio and kind of relevance varies by setting.
中文翻译
影响函数是一种有前景的方法，可用于识别对模型输出负责的训练样本。然而，迄今为止，针对大语言模型预训练层面的实证评估仅假设其按预期工作。本文是首个基于受控数据划分进行再训练，系统评估‘有影响力’样本是否真正具备与语言模型输出相关属性的研究。我们在GPT-2类模型（68M–5.6B训练token）中考察了三种主流的海森矩阵近似方法（GradSim、TrackStar、K-FAC），分别在语言现象和事实知识两种场景下进行分析。结果表明，平均而言，‘有影响力’的样本确实相关，但它们并不总是直接承载目标属性，且相关性的比例和类型随场景而异。

Delta has been successfully employed as an authorship attribution method in various contexts, although its effectiveness is not always perfect. Since it is based on the most frequent words, its results can be readily interpreted and explained in plain English. In this study, we propose improving its performance by applying a resampling method to the selected stylistic features. However, the main drawback of the proposed technique is the additional computational time required to obtain more accurate results.

Is Sara Omar the true author of Dødevaskeren (The Dead Washer)? Authorship attribution in Danish literature
Sara Omar是否为《死洗者》（Dødevaskeren）的真实作者？丹麦文学中的作者归属问题
The bestseller Dødevaskeren (The Dead Washer) was published in 2017 under the name Sara Omar. A Danish colleague has suggested that the novel may have been written by another author, possibly Mads Peder Nordbo. To investigate this authorship question, four authorship attribution methods were applied, namely Delta, intertextual distance, Nearest Shrunken Centroids (NSC), and naïve Bayes. Using various parameter settings, these approaches consistently identify Nordbo's writing style in the doubtful novel, although the results do not provide conclusive evidence that he authored the novel in its entirety. In a second step, the novel was divided into non-overlapping segments of 5,000 tokens. Once again, Nordbo's style was identified in the majority of the segments. However, a small number of passages do not clearly support this conclusion and instead suggest the possible involvement of another, as yet unidentified, author, possibly Sara Omar herself.

Legal due diligence in mergers and acquisitions requires lawyers to organize heterogeneous virtual data room documents, extract transaction-relevant information and consolidate reviewed findings into a due diligence report. [2611.34090]
Disciplined Anthropomorphism: How to Extend Cognitive Language to Artificial Intelligence
有纪律的人类拟态：如何扩展认知语言至人工智能
Researchers increasingly describe AI in cognitive terms such as "understanding" and "believing", while critics denounce this as wishful anthropomorphism. Both sides align in taking a subsumptive approach, however: they ask whether AI systems fall under concepts minted for human beings, reporting verdicts as discoveries about those systems when they are largely determined by conceptual frames. Yet the problem AI poses is precisely the problem of how to conceptually frame these systems, and the explosive rise of anthropomorphism is best seen as a response to this problem that needs disciplining rather than discouragement. A disciplined anthropomorphism, I argue, should disentangle the inferential connections bundled up in our cognitive concepts and ask which connections to preserve, sever, or forge for AI. The disciplining then comes, on the one hand, from the human conceptual needs that give point to the drawing of these connections and motivate the concepts' extension in the first place; and, on the other hand, from empirical and especially mechanistic evidence that supports and constrains these connections. By reverse-engineering a concept's point in relation to a particular need before re-engineering it to better serve that point in our dealings with AI, anthropomorphizing extensions can be point-based, transposing the work those concepts do for us while revising the inferential workings through which they do it. Transposing human-tailored conceptions of understanding and belief on the pivot of the underlying conceptual needs gives us just the definitional freedom required to accommodate how AI systems differ from us, yielding conceptions that are not just thinned-out versions of existing ones, but potentially richer and better fitted to the peculiarities of AI.
中文翻译
研究人员越来越多地使用“理解”“相信”等认知术语描述人工智能，而批评者则斥之为一厢情愿的人类拟态。然而，双方均采取了归摄式立场：即判断AI系统是否属于为人类创制的概念范畴，并将结论视为对系统本身的发现，实则主要由概念框架决定。但AI所引发的核心问题正是如何概念性地界定这些系统，而拟态现象的激增应被看作对此问题的回应，需要的是规范而非压制。我主张，有纪律的人类拟态应拆解认知概念中捆绑的推理关联，思考哪些关联应保留、切断或重构以适配AI。这种规范一方面源于人类概念需求，正是这些需求赋予概念关联以意义并推动其向AI延伸；另一方面来自实证乃至机制性证据，用以支持和约束这些关联。通过在重新设计概念以更好地服务于特定需求之前，先逆向解析其背后的动机，再重构其推理机制，人类拟态的延伸可以成为以目的为导向的实践，在保留概念功能的同时重构其运作方式。将专为人类设计的理解与信念概念，围绕其底层概念需求进行转置，为我们提供了必要的定义自由，以容纳AI系统与人类的差异，从而形成不仅简化了现有概念，而且可能更丰富、更契合AI特性的新概念。
👤 Matthieu Queloz 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33953]
PINN-Slicer: Exploiting Rotational Symmetry to Solve the Photon Orbit Equation Around a Black Hole
PINN-Slicer：利用旋转对称性求解黑洞周围的光子轨道方程
Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) typically struggle with long-horizon domains, often requiring the network to be retrained as the domain grows. We present PINN-Slicer, a method for second-order autonomous ordinary differential equations, $\ddot{u} = G(u, \dot{u})$, which exploits the invariance of this class of equations under translations of the independent variable. A single PINN, embedded within a solution ansatz that enforces the initial conditions explicitly, is trained on a thin slice of the independent variable using Sobol-sampled initial conditions. The PINN is then deployed recursively, slice-by-slice, across the full domain. We demonstrate PINN-Slicer on the nonlinear equation that describes photon orbits around a black hole, and reach a point-by-point root-mean-square error (RMSE) below $10^{-3}$ in $u = r_s/r$ (the inverse radial coordinate in units of the Schwarzschild radius) relative to a finite-difference reference solution. A key advantage of PINN-Slicer is that a single trained network generalizes to any orbital initial condition within the training range, without retraining. In addition, PINN-Slicer can cope with solution domains that may not be known a priori.
中文翻译
物理信息神经网络（PINNs）通常在长时域任务中表现不佳，往往需要随着域的扩展重新训练网络。我们提出PINN-Slicer方法，适用于二阶自治常微分方程 $\ddot{u} = G(u, \dot{u})$，该方法利用此类方程在自变量平移下的不变性。一个嵌入于显式满足初始条件的解假设中的单一PINN，通过Sobol采样的初始条件在自变量的一个薄片上进行训练，随后以递归方式逐片部署至整个定义域。我们在描述黑洞周围光子轨道的非线性方程上验证了PINN-Slicer的有效性，在 $u = r_s/r$（以史瓦西半径为单位的反向径向坐标）上实现了相对于有限差分参考解点对点均方根误差（RMSE）低于 $10^{-3}$ 的精度。PINN-Slicer的关键优势在于，单个训练好的网络可泛化到训练范围内的任意轨道初始条件，无需重新训练；此外，该方法还能处理事先未知的解域。
👤 Claire David, Tlotlo Oepeng, Harrison B. Prosper 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv

DiCALM: Converting Autoregressive Convolution-Attention Language Models into Discrete Diffusion Language Models
DiCALM：将自回归卷积-注意力语言模型转换为离散扩散语言模型
Existing methods for adapting autoregressive (AR) language models to diffusion generation focus mainly on full-attention architectures. More efficient hybrid architectures remain underexplored. We introduce DiCALM, a recipe for converting the hybrid convolution-attention models of the LFM2 family (LFM2 and LFM2.5) from AR to masked-diffusion generation. DiCALM progressively extends LFM2's causal gated convolution so that it captures local context in both directions. It combines this extension with a masking warm-up and joint AR--diffusion training. Spoken narratives extensively connect moment-to-moment language processing with knowledge accumulated through language and literacy experience. This study examined that relationship using functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) during Cantonese story listening and contextual representations from large language model (LLM). The imaging cohort comprised 23 school-age children; 16 contributed complete standardized literacy and language assessments and served as held-out participants in a nested cross-subject encoding analysis of the same story. Regional summaries of the top 25% of voxels ranked by held-out correlation yielded atlas-wide means of r=0.174 and partial R2=0.0165. Higher word-dictation and overall literacy scores were associated with lower regional encoding performance. Higher Cantonese grammar scores were associated with more negative semantic-update coefficients in parietal control, somatomotor and dorsal-attention parcels. Together, these findings connect language and literacy skills to the regional strength and direction of neural coupling with narrative context. They position naturalistic context processing as a possibly measurable link between oral comprehension and literacy in childhood.

口语叙事广泛地将瞬时语言处理与通过语言和读写经验积累的知识相连接。本研究通过在粤语故事聆听过程中使用功能性磁共振成像（fMRI）以及大型语言模型（LLM）提取的情境表征，探讨这一关系。研究样本包括23名学龄儿童；其中16名完成了完整的标准化语言与读写能力评估，并作为嵌套跨被试编码分析中的保留参与者，用于同一故事的建模。情境嵌入能够预测分布式的叙事响应，尤其在颞顶叶和默认网络区域表现出显著对应性。基于保留相关性排名前25%的体素区域总结，得到全脑平均相关系数r=0.174，偏R²=0.0165。联合特征模型识别出皮层与皮层下区域（包括控制、显著性、海马及杏仁核区域）中负意外度和正预测熵系数。更高的单词听写与整体读写分数与较低的区域编码性能相关；更高的粤语语法成绩则与顶叶控制区、躯体运动区和背侧注意区的更负的语义更新系数相关。这些发现揭示了语言与读写能力与神经耦合区域强度及方向之间的关联，表明自然情境处理可能是儿童口语理解与读写能力之间一个可测量的联结。

[2611.33844]
When Rerankers Work Together: Better and Faster Reranking without Joint Training
当重排序器协同工作时：无需联合训练即可实现更好更快的重排序
Standalone reranker quality does not determine its value in a multistage RAG pipeline. We systematically study heterogeneous rerankers composed without joint training, spanning lexical, single-vector, late-interaction, and joint query-context scoring. Two lightweight semantic coarse scorers, Cache-Pointwise and Cache-Shapley, learn from passage relevance labels and share cacheable representations and linear-time singleton inference. Under matched candidates and budgets, we compare cascades with stage-removal references to measure evidence retention, repair and breakage, reader accuracy, and online pruning cost. On NoLiMa 32K with Gemma, inserting these scorers before CrossEncoder or Provence improves answer accuracy by 4.02-9.43 percentage points while accelerating online pruning by 1.57-1.88x relative to the corresponding reranker alone. Cache-Pointwise composed with ColBERTv2 through reciprocal rank fusion improves accuracy by 4.60 points at 1.36x speedup over direct ColBERTv2. The stage-removal comparisons reveal an asymmetry: a cascade can outperform its final reranker alone while underperforming its coarse selector alone. Two readers, a second multi-hop dataset, and three-stage extensions show that the gains depend on the reader, reranker pairing, and stage configuration. These findings demonstrate a practical route to better and faster reranking without joint training and motivate studying stage order, candidate budgets, and rank fusion as determinants of composition quality.

独立重排序器的质量并不能决定其在多阶段RAG流水线中的价值。我们系统研究了不进行联合训练的异构重排序器组合，涵盖词法、单向量、后期交互及查询-上下文联合评分等多种类型。两种轻量级语义粗排器Cache-Pointwise和Cache-Shapley从段落相关性标签中学习，共享可缓存表示并支持线性时间的单例推理。在匹配候选集和预算条件下，我们通过移除中间阶段的对比实验，衡量了信息保留、修复与破坏、阅读器准确率及在线剪枝成本。在NoLiMa 32K数据集上使用Gemma模型，将这些粗排器插入CrossEncoder或Provence之前，可使答案准确率提升4.02至9.43个百分点，同时在线剪枝速度提升1.57至1.88倍。Cache-Pointwise与ColBERTv2通过倒数排名融合组合后，准确率提升4.60点，速度提升1.36倍，优于直接使用ColBERTv2。阶段移除对比揭示出不对称性：一个级联结构可能优于其最终重排序器，但劣于其粗排选择器。两个阅读器、第二个多跳数据集及三阶段扩展表明，收益取决于阅读器、重排序器配对及阶段配置。这些发现展示了无需联合训练即可实现更优更快重排序的实用路径，并推动研究阶段顺序、候选预算和排序融合作为组合质量的决定因素。

Teaching a Multilingual Translation Model Regional Varieties: Castilian and Latin American Spanish, Asturian, Aragonese and Aranese with MADLAD-400-3B-MT
教授多语言翻译模型区域变体：西班牙语（卡斯蒂利亚）与拉丁美洲西班牙语、阿斯图里亚语、阿拉贡语和阿兰ese语，基于MADLAD-400-3B-MT
Massively multilingual translation models usually have one tag per language and no control over regional varieties, and they cover a region's smaller languages poorly or not at all. We report three fine-tunes of MADLAD-400-3B-MT, each adding target tags with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), back-translation of monolingual text and replay of the base model's own translations: <2es_ES> for Spanish as written in Spain, <2es_419> for Latin American Spanish, and one model for three Romance languages of the Iberian Peninsula, Asturian (<2ast>), Aragonese (<2an>) and Aranese (<2oc_aranes>). For the Spanish varieties we annotate the 250.9M documents of MADLAD-400's Spanish split with a domain-supervised Spain vs. Latin America classifier and select variety text with a rule that admitted no Latin American document in a 340-document reference set. The Castilian model chooses Spain's everyday vocabulary in 97% of a targeted test (base model 62%) and vosotros for informal plural address in 70% (10%), while improving chrF++ on human references. A first finding is tag collapse: with a new tag initialised from <2es>, the parent tag drifts to the new variety (84-87% identical outputs). In the Iberian model, contrastive pairs and a separate learning rate for the tag embeddings keep each tag in its own language. Trained only on publicly available data, mostly the TAN-IBE Wikipedia corpora, the Iberian model matches or beats the TAN-IBE Marian systems in four of six Spanish directions on FLORES+, is 1.9 BLEU behind in Spanish->Aranese, and is second only to SalamandraTA-7B in most directions. The exception, Spanish->Aragonese, reflects a difference of written variety between Aragonese Wikipedia and the FLORES+ reference, not model quality. All three fine-tunes lose 0.6-0.7 chrF++ on average in thirteen other FLORES-200 directions. We release the models, data and variety classifier under open licences

大规模多语言翻译模型通常对每种语言仅使用一个标签，且无法控制区域变体，对较小语言的覆盖也较差。我们报告了对MADLAD-400-3B-MT的三次微调，每次通过低秩适配（LoRA）、单语文本的反向翻译以及基础模型自身翻译的重放，添加目标标签：<2es_ES>用于西班牙（西班牙）语，<2es_419>用于拉丁美洲西班牙语，以及一个涵盖伊比利亚半岛三种罗曼语的语言模型（阿斯图里亚语<2ast>、阿拉贡语<2an>和阿兰ese语<2oc_aranes>）。针对西班牙语变体，我们使用领域监督分类器对MADLAD-400的西班牙语数据集中的2.509亿文档进行标注，并通过规则筛选出不包含任何拉丁美洲文档的340篇参考文档集合。卡斯蒂利亚模型在目标测试中以97%的概率选择西班牙日常词汇（基线模型为62%），在非正式复数称呼中使用vosotros的比例达70%（基线为10%），同时在人类参考标准下提升了chrF++得分。一个初步发现是标签坍缩现象：当从<2es>初始化新标签时，父标签会漂移到新变体（84%-87%输出相同）。在伊比利亚模型中，对比对和独立学习率的标签嵌入保持了各标签在其语言内的独立性。该模型仅在公开数据上训练，主要来自TAN-IBE维基百科语料库，在FLORES+的六条西班牙语方向中有四条达到或超过TAN-IBE Marian系统的表现，仅在西班牙语→阿兰ese方向落后1.9 BLEU，且在多数方向仅次于SalamandraTA-7B。例外情况——西班牙语→阿拉贡语——反映了阿拉贡语维基百科与FLORES+参考之间的书面变体差异，而非模型质量。所有三个微调模型在其他十三个FLORES-200方向上平均损失0.6-0.7 chrF++。我们已将模型、数据和变体分类器以开源许可发布。

[2611.33823]
Pitfalls of Dependency-Ready Decoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models
掩码扩散语言模型中依赖就绪解码的陷阱
Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have shown strong performance on structured reasoning tasks such as Sudoku, Trip Planning, and Countdown. MDMs introduce a freedom in the order in which tokens may be unmasked, and to exercise this freedom for good accuracy, early decoding strategies relied on signals of confidence or the downstream impact of decoding a token. However, simple examples seem to illustrate that resolving a token's dependencies before unmasking it should be important. Therefore, we examine whether this principle, which we call dependency-ready decoding, would contribute to better accuracy, from a practical and a theoretical perspective. We examine a recent decoder that implements dependency-readiness through attention, and generally find that when it performs better than confidence-based or future utility baselines, its decoding order collapses to left-to-right decoding. For theoretical analysis, we establish a definition of dependency without resorting to attention, measured by an information-theoretic score of how much revealing a single masked token (or a set) would shift the model's prediction for another. We prove that when the margin is large relative to this score, no such revelation can change the top prediction, so in this regime Maximum-Margin decoding already commits dependency-ready tokens. These findings leave only a narrow scope for future dependency-ready decoders: their potential benefit is limited to positions with margins smaller than the information-theoretic sensitivity, and reliance on attention should be questioned.
中文翻译
掩码扩散模型（MDMs）在结构化推理任务（如数独、行程规划、倒计时）中表现出色。MDMs允许以任意顺序解掩码token，为了获得高精度，早期解码策略依赖于置信度信号或解码token的下游影响。然而，简单示例表明，应在解掩码前解决token的依赖关系。因此，我们从实践和理论角度考察“依赖就绪解码”是否能提升准确性。我们分析了一种最近通过注意力实现依赖就绪性的解码器，发现当其优于基于置信度或未来效用的基线时，其解码顺序往往退化为从左到右的解码。在理论分析中，我们提出一种不依赖注意力的依赖定义，通过信息论评分衡量揭示单个或一组掩码token对模型预测的影响程度。我们证明，当边际值相对于该评分较大时，任何揭示都无法改变最高预测，因此在此范围内最大间隔解码已自动完成依赖就绪。这些发现表明，未来依赖就绪解码器的潜在收益极为有限：仅适用于边际值小于信息论敏感度的位置，且应质疑对注意力机制的依赖。
👤 Lina Berrayana, Christopher Malon, Yoichi Ishibashi, Taichi Aida, Masafumi Oyamada 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33789]
Multimodal Safety Evaluation of Vision-Language Models in Japanese
日语视觉-语言模型的多模态安全评估
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in Japanese-language settings, yet multimodal safety evaluation has been largely English-centric. We extend the Multimodal Safety Test Suite (MSTS) of Röttger et al. (2025) to Japanese as the 12th language, contributing 320 machine-translated and post-edited cases with reference answers plus 82 culturally localized Japanese images. We introduce an evaluation framework that (i) extends MSTS's 3-class safety grouping with a 5-point scale that also rewards useful (not just safe) responses, and (ii) separates image recognition from safety judgment to isolate recognition errors. Using this framework, we evaluate five modern Japanese VLMs (Gemini-3-Flash, GPT-5, InternVL3, LLM-JP-4-VL, Qwen3-VL) against human evaluation, and find substantial variance in the safety readiness across the models. Finally, we evaluate two LLM-as-a-Judge models and find that providing a reference answer improves agreement with human safety judgments, though recall for detecting unsafe responses remains limited, underscoring the difficulty of automating multimodal safety evaluation. The dataset created for our study will be released upon publication.

[2611.33690]
Refusal Is Not Scored: A Coverage Limit in Content-Based Bias Evaluation
拒绝不计分：基于内容的偏见评估中的覆盖局限
Bias audits of conversational models often discard refusals (responses such as "I can't assist with that request") before scoring, a filtering step which is rarely examined as a measurement decision even though it determines which responses an audit is capable of evaluating. This work examines what the step removes, applying seven bias-evaluation instruments spanning both deployed paradigms, three dictionary-based and four LLM-as-judge, to two intersectional datasets of 360 generated responses each. In both datasets, the filter removed a demographically skewed subset produced entirely by one of the three responder models, which refused North Korean prompts at 2.15 times the rate of American prompts on declarative content and 1.76 times on operational content. We then remove the filter and pass every refusal to all seven instruments. Bias-Lexicon scores refusals as clean, and including them shrinks the nationality disparity it reports. HurtLex appears to register the disparity, but we trace this entirely to a single lexicon entry matching the token "can" in "I can't". The LLM judges either flag most responses or rate refusals as unbiased. For the two judges that discriminate, including refusals lowers the nationality disparity they report, and no instrument identifies the refusal disparity. The failure is therefore a coverage limit upstream of scoring rather than a calibration gap that a better lexicon or judge prompt would be expected to close, which makes the behavioral channel an instrument is sensitive to a question of construct validity rather than one of method selection.
中文翻译
对话模型的偏见审计通常在评分前丢弃拒绝响应（如“我无法协助该请求”），这一过滤步骤虽决定审计可评估的响应范围，却极少被当作测量决策进行审视。本研究考察该步骤移除了什么内容，采用七种偏见评估工具（涵盖两种部署范式，三种基于词典的方法和四种以LLM为裁判的方法），对两组各360个生成响应的交集数据集进行了分析。在两个数据集中，该过滤器移除了由三个响应模型之一产生的具有人口统计学偏差的子集，该模型对朝鲜相关提示的拒绝率是美国提示的2.15倍（陈述性内容）和1.76倍（操作性内容）。随后我们移除过滤器，将所有拒绝响应提交给全部七种工具。Bias-Lexicon将拒绝响应视为无偏，包含它们后缩小了其报告的国籍差异；HurtLex似乎识别出该差异，但我们发现这完全源于一个匹配“can”token的词典条目。以LLM为裁判的工具要么标记大多数响应，要么将拒绝响应评为无偏。对于其中两个具有区分性的裁判，包含拒绝响应后降低了其报告的国籍差异，且没有任何工具识别出拒绝差异。因此，问题在于评分之前的覆盖局限，而非可通过更好词典或裁判提示弥补的校准差距，这意味着工具敏感的行为通道是一个建构有效性问题，而非方法选择问题。
👤 Afifah Kashif, Malak Sadek, Jin Huang, Hatice Gunes 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33668]
Verifiable but Not Predictable: What Sentence Embeddings Know About Propositional Decomposition
可验证但不可预测：句向量对命题分解的认知
Quality control for proposition decomposition by large language models (LLMs) raises two asymmetric questions. After decomposition, one can verify: given a sentence and a candidate proposition, does the proposition belong to that sentence? Before decomposition, one would like to predict: how many propositions should the sentence yield? On PropSegmEnt, a corpus of English Wikipedia sentences each segmented by three human annotators, we find that the two questions live in different representational spaces. A natural language inference (NLI) judge verifies membership with 92.8% accuracy and an area under the ROC curve (AUC) of 0.977, and its residual errors turn out to be mostly propositions that are true in document context rather than genuine entailment mistakes. Vector geometry cannot verify: cosine similarity saturates, and a sentence embedding is not a linear combination of its propositions' embeddings. For prediction, every symbolic anchor we test, namely token count, the CPIDR idea-density counter, and a Neo-Davidsonian predicate count, reduces to a length proxy once token count is controlled. A linear ridge probe on sentence embeddings is the only predictor that keeps genuine count signal: its length-partialled correlation with human counts is 0.40-0.49 with bootstrap confidence intervals (CIs) excluding zero, and it orders length-matched sentence pairs correctly 67-71% of the time against a 50% chance level, statistically matching the inter-annotator agreement ceiling. This signal survives INT8 quantization without loss, INT4 at a small cost, and Matryoshka truncation to 256 dimensions, and it works as well in a 2021 encoder as in a 2025 one. Its structured weakness is a systematic under-splitting of long sentences. We distill the protocol lessons, including a retracted pilot study, into a checklist for probing length-confounded quantities.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）进行命题分解的质量控制引发两个不对称问题。分解后可验证：给定一个句子和候选命题，该命题是否属于该句子？分解前希望预测：该句子应产生多少个命题？在PropSegmEnt数据集（每条英文维基百科句子由三位人工标注者分割）上，我们发现这两个问题存在于不同的表征空间中。自然语言推理（NLI）裁判对成员关系验证的准确率达92.8%，ROC曲线下面积（AUC）为0.977，其残余错误主要来自文档上下文中为真的命题，而非真正的蕴含错误。向量几何无法验证：余弦相似度趋于饱和，且句子向量并非其命题向量的线性组合。对于预测，我们测试的所有符号锚点（包括词数、CPIDR思想密度计数器、Neo-Davidsonian谓词计数）在控制词数后均退化为长度代理。唯一能保留真实计数信号的预测方法是在线性岭探测器对句子向量的探测：其在去除长度影响后的相关性为0.40–0.49，Bootstrap置信区间排除零值，且在长度匹配的句子对中正确排序比例达67–71%（高于50%随机水平），统计上接近标注者间一致性的上限。该信号在INT8量化下无损失，INT4下略有损失，Matryoshka截断至256维后仍有效，且在2021年编码器与2025年编码器中表现相当。其结构性弱点是系统性地低估长句的拆分。我们总结了实验教训（包括已撤稿的初步研究），形成了一份探测长度混淆量的检查清单。
👤 Qingan Li, Tianxiao Wu, Xiling Cui 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33432]
Open-Source FUTO Keyboard Layouts and Predictive Text for a Few Endangered Languages
开源FUTO键盘布局与少数濒危语言的预测文本
We present open-source FUTO Keyboard layouts and predictive text dictionaries for five endangered Uralic languages: Erzya, Moksha, Komi-Zyrian, Livonian, and Skolt Sami. Existing Divvun Android keyboard layouts are converted automatically into the YAML format used by FUTO Keyboard. For predictive text, we construct weighted AOSP dictionaries using translated Finnish lemma frequencies together with morphological frequencies extracted from small target-language corpora using finite-state transducers. This combination provides a practical way to rank inflected word forms when large monolingual corpora are unavailable. The resulting resources offer reusable mobile keyboard support for endangered Uralic languages.

[2611.33266]
FrIdéo: French News Outlet Ideology Scoring via Multi-Source Evidence Fusion and Distancing-Aware Lexical Scoring
FrIdéo：基于多源证据融合与距离感知词汇评分的法语新闻媒体意识形态打分
Outlet ideology is a standard explanatory variable in media framing, media effects, and political communication research, but continuous, ecosystem-wide coverage lets it enter as graded exposure rather than a coarse bin. For France, existing references are either broad but non-analytic or analytic but sparse and source-specific, and none we are aware of offers ecosystem-wide coverage on a unified continuous scale. We contribute FrIdéo: to our knowledge the first ecosystem-wide continuous ideology scores for the French press, a transparent composite score for 30 French news outlets, fusing nine independent families of evidence with empirical-Bayes-style shrinkage toward a structural prior. For the text-based family we introduce symmetric plain/qualified scoring, which separates the plain use of ideologically loaded vocabulary from its qualified (distanced) use on both sides, a distinction bag-of-words methods miss. The resulting scale runs from L'Humanité on the far left to Valeurs actuelles on the far right; the far-right pole is near-indistinguishable and CNews ties Le Figaro. Because the text family reads current articles, it also captures editorial change that static sources miss, such as the Journal du Dimanche's sharp rightward shift after its 2023 ownership change. The ordering is robust to perturbation and to dropping any single family, and agrees with held-out Media Bias Fact Check labels. We release the scores, confidence intervals, and replication code.
中文翻译
媒体意识形态是媒体框架、媒体效果和政治传播研究中的标准解释变量，但连续性的、覆盖整个生态系统的报道使其成为分级暴露而非粗略分类。对于法国而言，现有参考文献要么范围广泛但缺乏分析性，要么分析性强但稀疏且局限于特定来源，目前尚无任何已知工作提供统一连续尺度上的全国性媒体生态覆盖。我们提出FrIdéo：据我们所知首个针对法国新闻媒体的生态系统级连续意识形态评分系统，为30家法国新闻机构构建透明的综合评分，融合九类独立证据，并采用类似经验贝叶斯的收缩方法向结构先验靠拢。针对文本类证据，我们引入对称的纯文本/限定文本评分方法，区分意识形态词汇的直接使用与其限定（去距离化）使用，这一区别是传统词袋方法所忽略的。所得量表从左翼极点的《人道报》延伸至右翼极点的《当代价值》，右翼极点几乎难以区分，CNews与《费加罗报》并列。由于文本类评分基于最新文章，还能捕捉静态来源无法反映的编辑变化，例如《星期日杂志》在2023年所有权变更后的急剧右倾。该排序对扰动和剔除任一证据族均保持稳健，并与保留的媒体偏见事实核查标签一致。我们公开发布评分、置信区间及复现代码。

[2611.33176]
Psychophysics of Duration Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models
多模态大语言模型中时长感知的心理物理学研究
Studies of time perception in language models focus mainly on text-based settings. Yet multimodal models are increasingly used for tasks that require perceiving the passage of time, making the limits of their temporal perception important to understand. We measure the limits of duration perception from audio against a human baseline to address this gap. Drawing from psychophysics, we operationalise duration perception as a melody recognition task, testing when a melody becomes unrecognisable as it is slowed down. Of 21 tested models, most cannot recognise well-known melodies without labelled in-context examples. With in-context examples, seven recognise melodies reliably, most peaking within the median human recognition band. Generalisation is limited: (i) recognition peaks shift with in-context example speed, (ii) melody titles induce prediction bias, and (iii) providing examples with scientific pitch notation does not prevent accuracy declining at long note durations for most models. In humans, the recognition window is a property of the perceiver; in current models, it is largely a property of the prompt.
中文翻译
语言模型中的时间感知研究主要集中在文本场景。然而，随着多模态模型在需要感知时间流逝的任务中日益广泛应用，理解其时间感知能力的局限性变得尤为重要。我们通过与人类基准对比，测量了音频输入下的时长感知极限。基于心理物理学方法，我们将时长感知操作化为旋律识别任务，测试当旋律逐渐变慢至无法辨认时的临界点。在21个测试模型中，大多数模型在没有上下文标注示例的情况下无法识别知名旋律。在提供上下文示例后，有七个模型能可靠地识别旋律，多数表现集中在人类平均识别范围内。泛化能力有限：(i) 识别峰值随上下文示例的速度变化而偏移；(ii) 旋律标题引发预测偏差；(iii) 即使提供科学音高记谱法示例，大多数模型在长音符持续时间下准确率仍下降。在人类中，识别窗口是感知者自身的属性；而在当前模型中，它更多是提示（prompt）的属性。

[2611.33175]
Feature-Based Explainability of Transformer Scoring Models: A Fidelity-First Framework for Automated Writing Assessment
基于特征的Transformer评分模型可解释性：一种以保真度优先的自动写作评估框架
Transformer-based automated writing scoring models can achieve strong agreement with human scores while providing limited transparency into the response characteristics associated with their predictions. This study evaluated a feature-based post-hoc explanation approach in which explanatory fidelity was established before feature importance was interpreted. English Language Arts responses from three writing prompts in Grades 6 and 8 were analyzed, with about 12,700 responses per prompt and separate DeBERTa-v3-base research models for Expression and Conventions. Each response was represented by 61 interpretable linguistic and writing-quality features. Four approaches - theory-driven composites, hierarchical feature groupings, exploratory factor analysis, and Random Forest surrogates - were compared in their ability to reproduce DeBERTa predictions. Random Forest surrogates produced the largest observed R2 values across all six prompt-by-trait conditions, with held-out R2 values from .81 to .95. SHAP analyses showed substantial overlap in high-importance features across traits, with 75% to 90% of the top 20 features shared. Trait differentiation occurred mainly through relative feature weighting: Expression showed greater importance for lexical and conceptual indicators, whereas Conventions showed greater importance for sentence-level and syntactic-control indicators. Under researcher-defined mappings, rubric correspondence was 90%-95% for Expression and 12%-18% for Conventions. These percentages are descriptive and conditional on the mappings and available linguistic features. The findings support a fidelity-first framework for examining apparent scoring priorities while distinguishing post-hoc evidence about model behavior from direct explanation of transformer internals.

The Bundesrat corpus: A dataset of political discourse in the German Federal Council

The Bundesrat is Germany's second legislative chamber and a constitutional veto player within its political system. The members of the Bundesrat are delegates of state governments. Tracing the political discourse in the Bundesrat is challenging as it has a limited machine-readable corpus available. To address this issue, we present a speaker-attributed corpus of 1,067 Bundesrat plenary sessions from the chamber's first session on 7 September 1949 to 10 July 2026. In this period, a total of 53,362 speeches by 2,473 individuals were delivered in the chamber, including written statements. Each speech includes a speech ID, the full German text, the speaker's name, the date and location of the session, the federal state that the speaker represents, the speaker's role (if applicable), the type of contribution, and the speaker's gender. The dataset provides interdisciplinary research opportunities across computational political science, legislative studies, and parliamentary discourse analysis, coupled with discourse from the Bundestag.

[2611.34935]
Can image generation help visual reasoning? An empirical study
图像生成能否助力视觉推理？一项实证研究
Language models think in words; then multimodal models might think in images. We test whether a generated helper improves the answer to a visual question. A model describes the helper it needs and then answers using the original and generated images together. Across 13 vision-language model and generator pairs, generation lowers mean accuracy by 3.7 percentage points, with declines on nine of ten tasks. Two unified models, BAGEL and Janus-Pro-7B, each lose accuracy on eight tasks. On VSTAR, adding a generated image to the same planning hint lowers accuracy by 10.47 points. Repeated image edits yield different outcomes across tasks. ChartQAPro and MathVista stay below their no-generation baselines, while ERQA gains three points after ten edits.

[2611.34760]
One Model, Many Modes: Evolving System Prompt Ensembles for Creative Generation
一个模型，多种模式：面向创造性生成的系统提示集成演化
Preference-aligned LLMs can exhibit mode collapse, concentrating their output distributions around a narrow set of high-reward responses. This limits creative generation, which requires quality, novelty, and diversity. Existing test-time methods often emphasize diversity, giving less attention to whether individual responses are both high-quality and novel. This distinction matters in practical use, where users may seek a single compelling, original response. We formalize creative generation as a multi-objective distributional optimization problem and introduce SEPO, a training-free framework for System Prompt Ensemble Optimization. The framework combines prompt evolution with greedy max-min selection to identify behaviorally distinct system prompts under a quality constraint, then uniformly samples one prompt per response at inference time. Across four creative generation tasks with increasing quality requirements and three open-weight models, SEPO ranks first in 8 of 12 settings on the harmonic mean of quality, diversity, and novelty, while no baseline leads in more than two. On average, it raises LLM-judged diversity from 1.92 to 3.43 and novelty from 1.00 to 3.34 over base sampling (1-5 scale), with quality decreasing from 3.72 to 3.30. We further show that expanding the optimized ensemble increases separation among prompt-conditioned response distributions. These results support system prompt ensemble optimization as a promising approach to improving LLM creativity at inference time.
中文翻译
偏好对齐的大语言模型可能出现模式坍缩，使其输出分布集中在少数高奖励响应上。这限制了创造性生成，后者需要高质量、新颖性和多样性。现有测试时方法通常强调多样性，却较少关注个体响应是否兼具高质量与新颖性。这一区别在实际应用中至关重要，因为用户可能希望获得单一引人注目且原创的回应。我们将创造性生成形式化为多目标分布优化问题，并提出SEPO——一种无需训练的系统提示集成优化框架。该框架结合提示演化与贪婪最大最小选择，在质量约束下识别行为差异显著的系统提示，并在推理时均匀采样一个提示用于每条响应。在四个具有递增质量要求的创造性生成任务及三个开源权重模型上，SEPO在12个设定中的8个上以质量、多样性与新颖性的调和平均分排名第一，而无任何基线在超过两个设定中领先。平均而言，其使LLM评价的多样性从1.92提升至3.43，新颖性从1.00提升至3.34（1-5分制），质量从3.72降至3.30。我们还发现，扩展优化后的提示集成可增强提示条件响应分布间的分离度。这些结果支持系统提示集成优化作为提升大模型推理阶段创造力的有前景方法。

[2611.34758]
Reading Fine-Tuning Diffs Through Lenses: Model Diffing for Backdoor Detection
透过透镜阅读微调差异：用于后门检测的模型差异分析
We introduce a common scoring system based on token readouts to evaluate weight, activation and output-based model diffing methods for backdoor detection in fine-tuning processes. We also improve existing weight and activation based methods by incorporating the $J$-lens and its refinement, the $J{+}{+}$-lens. We test our setup in experiments where we compare a backdoored model with the base model from which it was fine-tuned. Our results show that incorporating the $J{+}{+}$-lens improves the weight-based method in all our settings, while the $J$-lens improves its ranks within single layers. The strongest single method is $ΔW J{+}{+}$, the weight difference decoded through the $J{+}{+}$-lens of the base model, which ranks a token naming the backdoor among its first ten tokens in 11 of our 12 settings. Moreover, we show that output and activation based methods such as ADL and Diff Mining can easily break if the backdoored model is penalised during fine-tuning for giving the backdoor's words probability on clean inputs, whereas the weight-based methods are not affected. Conversely, permuting the hidden units or training only the layers that read the residual stream hides the backdoor from the weight-based methods but not from ADL and Diff Mining. No single method resists both attacks.
中文翻译
我们提出一种基于token读出的通用评分体系，用于评估微调过程中基于权重、激活和输出的模型差异方法在后门检测中的表现。我们还通过引入$J$-透镜及其改进版$J{+}{+}$-透镜，提升了现有的权重和激活基方法。我们在实验中将被植入后门的模型与原始基础模型进行对比。结果表明，引入$J{+}{+}$-透镜在所有设置中均提升了权重基方法的表现，而$J$-透镜则改善了单层内的排名。最强的单一方法是$ΔW J{+}{+}$，即通过基础模型的$J{+}{+}$-透镜解码的权重差，能在12个设置中的11个里将命名后门的token排在前十位。此外，我们发现基于输出和激活的方法（如ADL和Diff Mining）在后门模型因在干净输入上对后门词汇赋予概率而受到惩罚时极易失效，而权重基方法不受影响。相反，打乱隐藏单元或仅训练读取残差流的层会隐藏后门信息，使权重基方法失效，但不会影响ADL和Diff Mining。没有任何单一方法能同时抵御这两种攻击。
