[2611.34402]
Any-Rate: Half-Bit Multi-Precision LLM Quantization with Coded Bit-Planes
Any-Rate：具有编码比特平面的半比特多精度大语言模型量化
Nested multi-precision quantization derives multiple precisions from a single checkpoint by reading only the leading bits of each weight, which makes it attractive for on-device deployment under varying memory and energy budgets. However, existing methods offer only integer bits, leaving large accuracy gaps between adjacent precisions and severe degradation at 2 bits. To address this, we propose Any-Rate, which serves 2-8 bits from one checkpoint, in half-bit steps over 2-4 bits. Any-Rate restricts some bit-planes to extended Hamming codewords in 8-weight blocks (0.5 bits per weight), shares one LUT per row across precisions, and encodes all precisions jointly with a separate target for each, reducing the loss from sharing bit-planes. Across four LLMs, Any-Rate achieves state-of-the-art accuracy among multi-precision methods at 2 and 3 bits, keeping 69% of FP16 accuracy at 2 bits, and is within one point of the best at 4 bits. It decodes about as fast as existing kernels, and an LPDDR energy model estimates up to 27% less energy at equal accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/hoonki2002/Any-Rate.
中文翻译
嵌套多精度量化通过仅读取每个权重的前导比特，从单一检查点派生多种精度，使其在内存和能耗预算变化的设备部署中极具吸引力。然而，现有方法仅支持整数比特，导致相邻精度间存在较大准确率差距，且在 2 比特时严重退化。为解决此问题，我们提出 Any-Rate，可从一个检查点提供 2-8 比特的精度，以半比特步长覆盖 2-4 比特。Any-Rate 将部分比特平面限制为 8 权重块中的扩展汉明码字（每权重 0.5 比特），在精度间共享每行一个查找表，并为每种精度单独设置目标，联合编码所有精度，减少比特平面共享带来的损失。在四个大语言模型上，Any-Rate 在 2 和 3 比特时达到多精度方法的最先进准确率，2 比特时保持 FP16 准确率的 69%，4 比特时接近最佳水平。其解码速度与现有内核相当，LPDDR 能耗模型估计在同等准确率下最多节省 27% 能量。代码可在 https://github.com/hoonki2002/Any-Rate 获取。
👤 Hoonki Lee, Haechan Shin, Hyuntaek Jung, Yeonu Choi, Sangyeob Kim 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.34190]
SOLO: Towards Sample-Faithful and Detectable LLM Watermarking under Repeated Sampling via Content-Anchored Single-Owner Contexts
SOLO：通过内容锚定单拥有者上下文实现重复采样下的样本忠实与可检测LLM水印
Providers watermark the outputs of large language models, while applications sample many responses per prompt for self-consistency, best-of-n selection, and uncertainty estimation. We show that deployed configurations of distortion-free watermarks, which deduplicate keyed contexts only within a response, couple the responses of a request: answer templates make all responses query the watermark with the same context before the answer, so collisions across responses, usually assumed rare, become the norm. Key pools reduce or avoid the coupling at the cost of multiple testing, and the exact remedy of a context history shared across the request leaves many positions unwatermarked. We propose SOLO, which keeps a request-scoped context history but anchors its keyed contexts on content selected by min-hash, so that siblings rarely compete for a context and random edits stay local. Its responses are jointly distributed as independent unwatermarked responses, each detectable with a single key. On five language models from three families and three benchmarks, SOLO preserves self-consistency and uncertainty estimation and, at a 1% false positive rate, detects more responses than four-token key pools on most tasks, also after randomly editing 10% of the tokens, and more than the context history on most clean tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhongliangGuo/solo-watermark.
中文翻译
提供商对大语言模型输出进行水印标记，而应用则对每个提示采样多个响应以实现自一致性、最佳n选一和不确定性估计。我们发现，部署的无失真水印配置（仅在单个响应内去重键值上下文）会耦合请求的多个响应：答案模板使所有响应在回答前都查询相同的水印上下文，导致响应间的冲突（通常假设罕见）成为常态。键池虽能减少或避免耦合，但需多次测试；而共享整个请求的上下文历史的精确修复方案仍会使许多位置未被水印标记。我们提出SOLO，它保留请求范围内的上下文历史，但将键值上下文锚定在通过最小哈希选择的内容上，使得兄弟响应极少竞争同一上下文，且随机编辑也局限于局部。其响应联合分布为独立的未水印响应，每个均可通过单一密钥检测。在来自三个家族的五种语言模型和三个基准测试中，SOLO保持了自一致性和不确定性估计，在1%误报率下，多数任务中检测到的响应数量超过四令牌键池，即使在随机编辑10%的词元后仍表现更优，且在多数干净任务中优于上下文历史方案。代码见：https://github.com/ZhongliangGuo/solo-watermark。
👤 Zhongliang Guo, Yan Lin, Yifei Qian, Shuai Zhao, Luwei Xiao et al. 📅 2026-11-25 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33464]
PAC Learning to Sample: On Computational Aspects of Language Generation
PAC学习采样：语言生成的计算方面
The success of language models has motivated theoretical work on learning to sample from distributions on discrete domains. A prominent abstraction is the model of language generation in the limit, introduced by Kleinberg and Mullainathan (NeurIPS 2024), in which the algorithm must generate from the target language after some finite but unspecified number of positive examples. While its positive results are surprisingly general, most guarantees are qualitative. In this work, we seek quantitative guarantees on sample and time complexity, inspired by statistical and computational learning theory. To this end, we study a PAC model of language generation, proposed (in a different context) by De, Diakonikolas, and Servedio (SODA 2015), in which a Boolean concept class $C$ over $\{-1,1\}^n$ defines the target languages: given uniformly random positive examples of an unknown concept $f \in C$, the learner must output a sampler whose output distribution (conditioned on the training data) is close in total variation to the uniform distribution over $f^{-1}(1)$. Their work implicitly gives an efficient algorithm for PAC learning to sample $C$, given three primitives for $C$: an online learner, approximate sampling and counting oracles, and an SQ learner. We ask whether these ingredients, in particular online learnability, are necessary. First, we construct a Boolean concept class $C$ for which online learning is cryptographically hard, yet PAC learning to sample is computationally efficient. Then, we develop new algorithms that use offline ERM oracles instead of online learners, together with approximate sampling/counting oracles for intersections of concepts in $C$ and an SQ learner for $C$. We view our results, together with the work of De, Diakonikolas, and Servedio, as a first step towards understanding the computational complexity of language generation.
中文翻译
语言模型的成功激发了关于从离散域分布中学习采样的理论研究。一个突出的抽象是Kleinberg和Mullainathan（NeurIPS 2024）提出的极限语言生成模型，其中算法在经过有限但未指定数量的正例后必须生成目标语言。尽管其正面结果出人意料地普遍，但大多数保证均为定性。本文旨在获得关于样本复杂度和时间复杂度的定量保证，受统计与计算学习理论启发。为此，我们研究了由De、Diakonikolas和Servedio（SODA 2015）提出的PAC语言生成模型（在另一背景下提出），其中定义目标语言的布尔概念类C作用于{-1,1}^n：给定未知概念f∈C的均匀随机正例，学习者必须输出一个采样器，其输出分布（在训练数据条件下）与f^{-1}(1)上的均匀分布的总变差距离接近。他们的工作隐含地给出了一个高效算法，用于PAC学习采样C，前提是C具备三个基本工具：在线学习器、近似采样与计数预言机，以及SQ学习器。我们探讨这些要素，特别是在线可学习性，是否必要。首先，我们构造了一个在线学习在密码学上困难的布尔概念类C，但PAC学习采样却在计算上高效。随后，我们开发了新算法，使用离线ERM预言机替代在线学习器，结合C中概念交集的近似采样/计数预言机，以及C的SQ学习器。我们认为，结合De等人的工作，我们的结果是理解语言生成计算复杂性的第一步。
👤 Alkis Kalavasis, Shuchen Li, Manolis Zampetakis 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33388]
Measuring and Predicting Image-Token Repair Boundaries in Vision-Language Models
测量与预测视觉-语言模型中的图像标记修复边界
Degraded images make vision-language models answer wrongly. Using synthetic paired blur, noise, and low light, we trace which clean image states can still repair those answers from the vision encoder through the language model. In four encoder models, 12 to 23 ranked tokens recover half of the full-patch effect, whereas random orders require four to fifteen times as many; encoder-free Gemma needs 22.2 versus 53.1. Full-block repair expires at a model-specific language-model crossing. The matched reverse-direction intervention is algebraically identical to text-token restoration, so its gap from image restoration measures a redundancy band rather than an independent predictor. A clean-image gray-ablation crossing independently predicts held-out repair with 1.53-layer error, against 3.65 for shared depth. Gemma MMBench has the only large attention-to-repair point gap; Gemma GQA does not. Restormer provides a non-oracle consistency check on two models.
中文翻译
退化的图像会导致视觉-语言模型给出错误回答。我们利用合成的模糊、噪声和低光照配对数据，追踪哪些干净图像状态仍可通过视觉编码器到语言模型的路径修复这些错误回答。在四种编码器模型中，12 到 23 个排序靠前的标记即可恢复完整块效应的一半，而随机顺序则需要四到十五倍的数量；无编码器的 Gemma 需要 22.2 与 53.1。全块修复在模型特定的语言模型交叉点处失效。匹配的反向干预在代数上等同于文本标记恢复，因此其与图像恢复之间的差距衡量的是冗余带而非独立预测因子。干净图像灰度消融交叉点可独立预测保留修复，误差为 1.53 层，而共享深度误差为 3.65。Gemma MMBench 是唯一具有显著注意力到修复点差距的模型；Gemma GQA 则没有。Restormer 为两个模型提供了非预言式的内部一致性检查。
👤 Vishal Bhutani, Prasang Gupta, Sumanth Chundru, Kevin Paul 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33304]
The Platonic Representation Hypothesis in Jacobian-Space: Language and Vision Models Agree Approximately Up to a Rotation
雅各比空间中的柏拉图表征假说：语言与视觉模型大致沿旋转一致
The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) posits that AI models converge toward a shared representation of the world, even when trained on different data modalities. Previous work has found evidence for the PRH in the activations of vision and language models. In this work, we ask whether vision and language models also converge in how they act on these representations: precisely, in how these internal representations affect the models' output. At a chosen layer of a transformer, the Jacobian lens gives every word in the vocabulary (or class in an image classifier) a vector in the hidden state: the vector that most increases the model's tendency to generate that word, at that position or later, or to predict that class. Collectively, these vectors make up what is referred to as the model's J-space. We find evidence of the PRH in J-space across vision and language models, and furthermore, we find that J-spaces mostly converge up to a rotation. That is, a single rotation, fitted on a subset of words, approximately maps one model's J-space onto another's, and so applies to words and classes it was not fitted on. A word's vector, rotated from a text-only language model's J-space into the visual patch tokens of a vision-language model, changes the object the vision-language model says is in the image (and only in the image region where it is added). Rotated into an image generator's text conditioning, a word's vector changes the object in the generated image. Rotated into an ImageNet classifier (trained without text), it raises that object's class score above almost all others, and vice versa. This suggests that models converge not only in how they represent the world, but also in how they act on it.
中文翻译
柏拉图表征假说（PRH）认为，即使在不同数据模态上训练，AI 模型也会收敛到对世界的共享表征。先前研究已在视觉与语言模型的激活中发现了 PRH 的证据。本文探讨视觉与语言模型是否也在如何作用于这些表征上趋于一致：即这些内部表征如何影响模型输出。在选定的 Transformer 层上，雅各比视角为词汇表中的每个词（或图像分类器中的每个类别）赋予一个隐藏状态向量：该向量最能增加模型在该位置或之后生成该词的可能性，或预测该类别的倾向。这些向量共同构成所谓的模型 J 空间。我们在视觉与语言模型的 J 空间中发现了 PRH 的证据，并进一步发现 J 空间主要沿旋转收敛。即，仅在部分词语上拟合的单一旋转，即可近似地将一个模型的 J 空间映射到另一个模型，且适用于未参与拟合的词语与类别。将文本语言模型的 J 空间中的词语向量旋转至视觉-语言模型的视觉标记，会改变该模型声称图像中包含的对象（且仅在添加区域）。将其旋转至图像生成器的文本条件中，会改变生成图像中的对象。将其旋转至 ImageNet 分类器（无文本训练），会使其类别得分高于几乎所有其他类别，反之亦然。这表明模型不仅在世界表征上收敛，也在如何作用于这些表征上趋于一致。
👤 Ross Murphy, Gabriel Brostow 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33259]
Fast and Scalable Abstract Interpretation for Deep Neural Networks
面向深度神经网络的快速可扩展抽象解释
Abstract interpretation can certify properties of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), but precise analyses remain expensive. General implementations repeat work when analyzing the same architecture under different inputs and properties. Interleaving this work with numerical operations limits GPU utilization and restricts how much of the analysis a tensor compiler can process together. We present Scepter, a framework for specializing DNN abstract interpreters through structural semantics. The semantics determine the structure of intermediate abstract values while deferring their numerical evaluation. Structural computations from successive operations can therefore be performed together before certification, generating an executable tensor program. With the abstract interpreter and DNN architecture fixed, the program can be reused with different weights and input regions. We prove that it computes the same bounds as the general interpreter in exact arithmetic. The generated program also exposes operations and their dependencies for tensor compilation and memory planning. We implement Scepter for the tensor programs that Neuton generates from ConstraintFlow specifications. We evaluate Scepter on eight abstract interpreters and 44 DNNs against six baseline systems on GPUs and CPUs. Scepter handles networks with up to 153x as many neurons as the largest completed by the corresponding baselines. It completes DeepPoly on ResNet50 and VGG-16, whereas none of the evaluated DeepPoly baselines completes either network. On configurations completed by both systems, GPU geometric-mean speedups over Neuton range from 11.9x to 44.4x across eight analyses, with a 2.60x geometric-mean reduction in peak memory. In an input-splitting case study, Scepter proves 68 properties for which the baselines time out. Code: https://github.com/uiuc-focal-lab/constraintflow.git
中文翻译
抽象解释可用于验证深度神经网络（DNN）的性质，但精确分析仍然代价高昂。通用实现方式在分析同一架构的不同输入和性质时重复工作，且将抽象计算与数值操作交错进行，限制了GPU利用率，并制约了张量编译器能一次性处理的分析量。我们提出Scepter框架，通过结构语义对DNN抽象解释器进行专业化。语义决定中间抽象值的结构，同时推迟其数值求值。因此，连续操作的结构计算可在认证前合并执行，生成可执行的张量程序。在抽象解释器和DNN架构固定后，该程序可复用于不同权重和输入区域。我们证明该程序在精确算术下计算结果与通用解释器一致。生成的程序还暴露了操作及其依赖关系，便于张量编译和内存规划。我们实现了Scepter，用于从ConstraintFlow规范生成的张量程序。我们在八种抽象解释器和44个DNN上评估Scepter，对比六种基线系统在GPU和CPU上的表现。Scepter能够处理的神经元数量最多达到对应基线最大完成网络的153倍。它成功完成了DeepPoly对ResNet50和VGG-16的分析，而所有评估的DeepPoly基线均未能完成。在两者均可完成的配置上，GPU几何平均加速比从11.9x到44.4x不等，峰值内存减少2.60倍。在输入分割案例研究中，Scepter证明了68个性质，而基线系统因超时无法完成。
👤 Avaljot Singh, Dushyant Bharadwaj, Yasmin Chandini Sarita, Heng Zhong, Charith Mendis et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31990]
How Much Dependency Can a Marginal Rule See? Total Correlation in Diffusion LM Parallel Decoding
一个边缘规则能看见多少依赖？扩散语言模型并行解码中的总相关性
A masked diffusion language model is fast because it commits several tokens per step. Each token is sampled from its own marginal, so the dependency between the tokens is lost, and the size of that loss is the conditional total correlation C of the committed set. Most decoders choose the set from per-position marginals, which can bound C but cannot determine it. We ask how well they predict it. We estimate C for fixed candidate sets on four masked diffusion LMs from two families. On three of the four, the best marginal-only predictor is off by about one bit (0.74 to 1.17), 1.8 to 3.7 times less than a baseline that knows only the bound, and most of this accuracy comes from the bound itself. On a subset, our estimate ranks sets like the exact KL cost of a parallel commit, but sits a median 0.4 to 1.1 bits below it on prose and when the rest of the text is still masked. Dream-instruct is the exception, mainly on prose, where its conditionals are least self-consistent. Among six common marginal rules, pooling over the set beats gating on its weakest position in all 24 paired comparisons. The measurement needs a validated readout: Dream and LLaDA place a position's prediction at different logit indices, and the wrong index inflates an inconsistency diagnostic 14 to 41 times. We give three checks that measure the convention. Our results concern predicting C for a fixed set, not what a dependency-aware decoder gains.
中文翻译
一种掩码扩散语言模型之所以快速，是因为它每一步会确定多个词元。每个词元从其自身的边缘分布中采样，因此词元之间的依赖关系丢失了，这种损失的大小即为所确定集合的条件总相关性C。大多数解码器从位置相关的边缘分布中选择集合，这可以界定C的范围，但无法确定其具体值。我们研究了这些方法对其预测能力的优劣。我们在来自两个家族的四种掩码扩散语言模型上，对固定候选集合估计了C。在四者中的三个模型上，仅基于边缘分布的最佳预测器误差约为1比特（0.74至1.17），比仅知道边界值的基线高出1.8到3.7倍，而其中大部分准确性来源于该边界本身。在子集上，我们的估计与并行提交的精确KL代价排序一致，但在散文文本中以及其余文本仍被掩码时，其结果中位数低于精确值0.4至1.1比特。Dream-instruct是例外，尤其在散文文本中，其条件分布的一致性最差。在六种常见的边缘规则中，对集合进行池化优于在最弱位置进行门控，在全部24组配对比较中均表现更优。测量需要经过验证的读出方式：Dream和LLaDA将某个位置的预测放置在不同的logit索引上，错误的索引会使不一致性诊断值膨胀14至41倍。我们提出了三种检查来衡量这一约定。我们的结果关注的是对固定集合预测C的问题，而非依赖感知解码器所能获得的收益。
👤 Dovud Asadov, Rustambek Urokov, Qobiljon Toshnazarov, Abduaziz Kayumov, Mardon Hazratov et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
